Across different generation settings, Med-D-JEPA achieves superior or competitive performance compared to several strong baselines on fidelity and diversity metrics. Compared to training with real samples, Med-D-JEPA-based synthetic pretraining improves classification AUC from 0.63 to 0.85 on BraTS2019 and from 0.78 to 0.87 on OASIS-1. In the segmentation study, pretraining on Med-D-JEPA samples improves Dice from 0.74 to 0.80 and reduces HD95 from 13.40 to 9.56 mm. These findings establish joint-embedding predictive generation as a promising direction for 3D medical image synthesis and encourage further research in this direction.

</details>


### [34] [Blind Stereoscopic Omnidirectional Image Quality Assessment Using Predictive Coding Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28798)
*Wei Zhou,André Kaup*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预测编码层次结构启发的无参考立体全景图像质量评估指标（PCH），通过局部cyclopean感知模块、全局预测感知模块和视觉质量回归器模拟人类视觉系统的观看过程，实现SOIs的高效质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体全景图像为VR环境带来沉浸式体验，但其质量评估面临视场可自由变化、双目视觉复杂等挑战；现有无参考质量评估方法在SOIs上准确性和效率不足。

Method: 基于人类视觉系统特性，PCH包含三个核心模块：(1)局部cyclopean感知模块——提取多视口并进行cyclopean转换和显著性检测，模拟人脑的局部感知与注意力；(2)全局预测感知模块——基于双目机制融合左右视图并进行预测编码层次建模，模拟观者在脑中推断全局场景；(3)视觉质量回归器——结合局部与全局感知线索输出最终质量分数。

Result: 大量实验表明，PCH与最先进的图像质量评估方法相比，取得了具有竞争力且一致更优的性能。

Conclusion: PCH通过模拟人类观看SOIs的感知过程（先局部聚合后再全局推断），有效提升了立体全景图像无参考质量评估的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Stereoscopic omnidirectional images (SOIs) have provided users with newly immersive quality of experience in virtual reality environments. However, developing efficient and accurate perceptual quality assessment metrics for SOIs remains challenging due to many factors such as freely changeable field of views and binocular vision. In this paper, based on the characteristics of the human visual system (HVS), we propose a Predictive Coding Hierarchy-inspired metric (PCH) for blind/no-reference stereoscopic omnidirectional image quality assessment. Motivated by the viewing process of SOIs, the proposed PCH includes a local cyclopean perception module, a global predictive perception module, and a visual quality regressor. First, observers browse different spherical sceneries from viewports, and aggregate the local visual information to infer the perceptual quality of SOIs. Therefore, we extract various viewports, followed by cyclopean conversion and saliency detection to approach the perception and attention of the human brain. After the local aggregation, viewers then infer the global scene in their minds. Based on the binocular mechanism, we fuse left and right views to perform predictive coding hierarchy modelling. Finally, the visual quality regressor is exploited to obtain the ultimate quality score related to both local and global perceptual cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed PCH achieves competitive and consistently improved performance compared with state-of-the-art quality assessment methods.

</details>


### [35] [A Large-scale Evaluation of Text-guided Models for Facial Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28802)
*Rahul Nair,Saurav Pandit,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次对六种流行的文本引导扩散模型进行了大规模（约100万张图像）的面部编辑评估，构建了包含169个面部编辑属性的最大数据集Face-Edit-Attributes，并发现模型在头发和配饰编辑上表现良好但在姿态编辑上困难，几乎所有模型都存在过度编辑问题，且表现出针对深肤色男性和老年面孔的过度编辑偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导扩散模型已被广泛用于整场景编辑，但尚未被全面测试用于面部编辑。现有的面部编辑方案存在不足：GANs编辑多变但不稳定，3DMMs稳定但只能修改姿态和表情。为了评估文本引导扩散模型作为替代方案在面部编辑方面的表现，需要首次大规模系统评测。

Method: 构建了Face-Edit-Attributes数据集（169个面部编辑属性，涵盖头发、配饰和姿态编辑），在CelebA和CelebSET两个流行的名人面部数据集上对六种文本引导扩散模型进行顺序面部编辑任务的大规模评估（约100万张图像），并评测了各模型的过度编辑情况和人口统计学偏差。

Result: 大多数模型在头发和配饰编辑任务上表现良好，但在姿态编辑上存在困难。所有模型都存在过度编辑问题（例如仅要求改变发型时却改变了发色）。在人口统计学偏差方面，几乎所有模型对深肤色男性和老年面孔产生了更多的过度编辑。

Conclusion: 文本引导扩散模型虽然具备稳定且多样化的面部编辑能力，但在姿态编辑和避免过度编辑方面仍需改进，且存在明显的人口统计学偏差。研究的代码和数据已公开在GitHub上，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Facial appearance editing powers popular applications like FaceApp and Photoshop. Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and 3D Morphable Models (3DMMs) have been widely used for facial editing. GANs can perform varied facial edits (e.g., changing hair color, hairstyle), but often produce unstable edits. 3DMMs produce stable edits, but can only alter pose and facial expression. Recently, text-guided diffusion models like Nano Banana have become popular for image editing. Text-guided models are a compelling alternative to GANs and 3DMMs since they can produce both stable and varied image edits. While text-guided models have been widely tested for whole-scene edits (e.g., ``make the woman play a guitar''), they have not been comprehensively tested for facial editing. We conducted the first large-scale evaluation ($\sim1$M images evaluated) of six popular text-guided models on a sequential facial editing task. We present Face-Edit-Attributes, the largest collection of $169$ facial editing attributes focused on hair, accessories, and pose edits. We compared model performance using two popular celebrity face datasets: CelebA and CelebSET. Our results show that most models performed hair and accessory edits well, but struggled with editing pose. All models over-edit (e.g., changing hair color when asked only to change the hairstyle). We also evaluated demographic biases in each model. Our results show surprising biases in overediting: almost all models created more overedits for dark-skinned male faces and old faces. The code and data for our results (including our repository of $\sim 1$M images) can be accessed \href{https://github.com/rahul1801/Face-Edit-Bench}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [36] [FigMirror: Ground It, Code It, Plot It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28814)
*Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Yaxin Luo,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FigMirror，一个基于现代计算机使用模型（computer-use models）的智能体框架，用于在保持参考图视觉风格（如配色和排版）的同时绘制新数据。作者提出了Grounded Measurement方法，通过坐标定位视觉元素并用可执行代码测量其属性来解锁模型的坐标定位与编码能力。同时构建了PlotTwin-Bench基准，包含专家策展的细粒度代码级和图像级风格度量指标，实验表明FigMirror在参考条件风格迁移任务上持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从科学图表生成可执行代码的方法主要关注重现参考图本身，而更实用的场景是将新数据绘制成参考图的视觉风格（如配色和排版）。已有方法通过像素级优化来模仿参考图，难以将风格迁移到新数据上。作者观察到，该任务的关键在于现代计算机使用模型所具备的坐标定位（coordinate grounding）和编码（coding）能力，因此希望利用这些能力来突破像素级优化的局限。

Method: 提出FigMirror，一个智能体框架，核心创新是Grounded Measurement方法：通过坐标定位（locate）视觉元素，并借助可执行代码测量（measure）其属性。该框架利用现代计算机使用模型的坐标定位能力和编码能力，将参考图的风格信息以可量化的方式提取并迁移到新数据的绘制中。同时构建PlotTwin-Bench基准，提供专家策展的细粒度代码级和图像级风格度量指标用于评估。

Result: 实验表明FigMirror在参考条件风格迁移（reference-conditioned style transfer）任务上持续优于现有方法。此外，论文中所有图均由FigMirror自动生成（除用于对比的其他方法产出图外），展示了其实际绘图能力。代码和数据已开源。

Conclusion: FigMirror通过Grounded Measurement方法，有效解锁了现代计算机使用模型的坐标定位与编码能力，实现了从参考图风格到新数据的高质量风格迁移，显著优于现有基于像素级优化的方法，并为该任务提供了标准化的评估基准PlotTwin-Bench。

Abstract: Converting scientific figures into executable code has gained increasing attention, yet existing methods primarily focus on reproducing the reference figure itself. A more practical setting is to plot new data while preserving the visual style of a reference figure (e.g., color scheme and typography). Prior approaches mimic the reference through pixel-level optimization and struggle to carry its style to new data. We show that the key to this task lies in the coordinate grounding and coding capabilities present in modern computer-use models. We propose FigMirror, an agentic framework that unlocks these capabilities through Grounded Measurement, which locates visual elements by coordinates and measures their properties through executable code. We further introduce PlotTwin-Bench, an expert-curated benchmark with fine-grained code and image-level style metrics. Experiments show that FigMirror consistently outperforms existing methods on reference-conditioned style transfer. All plots in this paper are generated by FigMirror, except those produced by other methods for comparison. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/VILA-Lab/FigMirror.

</details>


### [37] [Text-Driven Artistic Staging: Pose, Lighting, and Camera References from Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28823)
*Yunge Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了"文本到可编辑3D布景"任务，即从情感描述文本联合生成人物姿态、主光源和相机配置。作者从2,328幅具象画作中重建了11,911个文本-布景配对，训练了一个流匹配Transformer模型，支持可变人数并生成多个布景方案，在检索任务上达到32.2%的R@1，远超CLIP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成方法通常将人体姿态、光照和相机位置建模为相互独立的元素，但艺术家实际是协同这些元素来传达叙事和情感的。缺乏一个能从文本联合生成这些编排元素的系统。

Method: 1) 数据构建：从2,328幅具象绘画中，通过重建SMPL人体模型、估计低频光照、恢复相机参数，并结合ArtEmis情感描述，构建11,911个文本-布景配对。2) 模型：训练一个流匹配(flow-matching)Transformer，支持可变数量的任务角色，并为每个提示生成多个布景候选项。

Result: 在留出描述上，模型检索R@1达到32.2%，相比基于CLIP的最近邻检索的16.6%有显著提升，同时大致保持了语料级别的多样性。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了从文本生成可编辑的、情感条件化的3D布景参考是可行的，为生成式方法中联合建模人体、光照和相机提供了新方向。

Abstract: Artists coordinate human pose, illumination, and camera placement to convey narrative and emotion, but existing generative methods typically model these elements independently. We introduce text-to-editable 3D staging, a task that jointly generates human poses, a dominant light, and a camera configuration from an affective description. We construct 11,911 text--staging pairs from 2,328 figurative paintings by reconstructing SMPL bodies, estimating low-frequency illumination, recovering camera parameters, and pairing each scene with ArtEmis descriptions. We train a flow-matching transformer that supports variable numbers of figures and produces multiple staging alternatives for each prompt. On held-out descriptions, the model achieves 32.2\% retrieval R@1, compared with 16.6\% for CLIP-based nearest-neighbor retrieval, while approximately preserving corpus-level diversity. These results demonstrate the feasibility of generating editable, emotionally conditioned 3D staging references from text.

</details>


### [38] [BlobBoards: Robust Markers for Accurate Pose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28830)
*James Pritts,Till Sittart,Hendrik Sauer,Silja Janßen,Felix Seegräber,David Nakath,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BlobBoards，一种由密集多尺度高斯斑点场构成的基准标记系统，通过联合检测、识别和位姿估计流程，在检测率、位姿精度和遮挡鲁棒性上均达到最先进水平，相比AprilTag在中型板上将平移误差降低了89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准标记系统（如AprilTag、ArUco）在精度、检测率和遮挡鲁棒性方面存在不足，尤其在小型标记和严重遮挡场景下表现不佳，需要一种能够跨越多种尺度、距离和倾斜角度保持可检测性并显著提升位姿估计精度的新型标记系统。

Method: BlobBoards采用由大量高斯斑点构成的多尺度密集标记场。每一块板从数百个斑点特征中注册，密集的空间覆盖约束位姿估计；多尺度设计在焦距、距离和倾斜度大幅变化时保持可检测性。使用学习得到的局部描述子与参考图案进行匹配并经空间验证，对应关系同时用于确定位姿和认证身份。

Result: 在动捕系统地面真值对比下，BlobBoards的中位平移误差为3.6-5.0mm，相比AprilTag在小板上降低89%、大板上降低70%的中位平移误差；大幅旋转失败次数远少于最先进的标签系统。检测率最高达80%（对比AprilTag 74%、ArUco 58%），在最小标记上优势最大。50%遮挡下仍能检测69%的板且误差基本不变，而AprilTag和ArUco完全无法检测。

Conclusion: BlobBoards在检测率、位姿精度和遮挡鲁棒性方面均取得了最先进的性能，其密集多尺度高斯斑点设计与学习式特征匹配管线为基准标记领域提供了可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose BlobBoards, a fiducial marker system comprising a dense, multi-scale field of Gaussian blobs and a feature-based pipeline for joint detection, identification, and pose estimation. Each board is registered from hundreds of blob features whose dense spatial coverage constrains pose, while multiple scales preserve detectability across large changes in focal length, distance, and obliquity. Learned local descriptors are matched to the reference pattern and spatially verified, so the correspondences determine pose and certify identity. Against motion-capture ground truth, BlobBoards achieve median translation errors of 3.6-5.0 mm, reducing AprilTag's median translation error by 89% on small boards and 70% on large ones. They also produce far fewer large-rotation failures than state-of-the-art tag systems. BlobBoards achieve the highest detection rate, 80% versus 74% for AprilTag and 58% for ArUco, with the largest margin on the smallest markers. Under 50% occlusion, they still detect 69% of boards with essentially unchanged median translation error, while AprilTag and ArUco detect none. In experiments BlobBoards give state-of-the-art detection rate, pose accuracy and occlusion robustness.

</details>


### [39] [MWIR-4-Plastic: The Identification of Complex End-of-Life Industrial Plastic using Mid-wave Infrared Hyperspectral Imaging and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28874)
*Elias Arbash,Andréa de Lima Ribeiro,Filipa Simões,Ahmed Jamal Afifi,Aldino Rizaldy,Yuleika Madriz,Samuel Thiele,Sandra Lorenz,Margret Fuchs,Pedram Ghamisi,Paul Scheunders,Richard Gloaguen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个公开的报废（EOF）车辆来源的黑色碎塑料高光谱图像（HSI）数据集，涵盖四种工业聚合物，包含13个配准的RGB、VNIR、SWIR和MWIR场景。作者开发了一个多模态光谱-空间框架，结合前景隔离、像素级分类和对象级多数投票，并通过适配高光谱transformer和化学计量学波段选择，实现了复杂黑色塑料的准确分类，建立了包含九种方法的综合基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回收设施中碎黑色塑料的自动分选面临重大挑战。现有方法依赖单点接触式中红外光谱或实验室高光谱成像，无法提供快速批量处理所需的空间分辨分析；现有数据集为实验室控制条件下的完整塑料而非碎料，且黑色工业塑料代表性不足；多数分类流程依赖人工区域选择和规则式光谱匹配，忽视了空间信息和现代深度学习方法。

Method: 构建首个公开的碎黑色塑料HSI数据集（EOF车辆来源，四种聚合物，13个配准多模态场景），开发多模态光谱-空间框架，包括前景隔离、像素级分类和对象级多数投票；适配来自地球观测领域的高光谱transformer，整合化学计量学波段选择；建立包含化学计量学、机器学习、深度学习九种方法的综合基准。

Result: 通过整合多模态光谱数据、先进的transformer架构和化学计量学波段选择，实现了复杂黑色塑料的准确分类。论文建立了首个涵盖九种处理方法（包括化学计量学、机器学习和深度学习架构）的综合基准，并公开了完整数据集和方法论以确保可复现性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了碎黑色塑料高光谱分析领域的空白，提供了首个公开数据集和完整基准，为工业检测中高光谱对象分析流程建立了标准，推动了回收精细化的发展。

Abstract: The automated sorting of shredded black plastics from end-of-life (EOF) industrial waste presents a significant challenge in recycling facilities, primarily due to the limitations of current sensing and analytical approaches. Existing studies predominantly rely on single-point contact-based mid-infrared spectroscopy or laboratory hyperspectral imaging (HSI) setups, which fail to provide the spatially resolved analysis necessary for fast, bulk processing. Moreover, available datasets are laboratory-controlled and focus on intact rather than shredded plastics, hindering further recycling refinement. Black industrial plastics, in particular, are underrepresented, while most classification pipelines depend on manual region selection and rule-based spectral matching, neglecting spatial information and modern deep learning (DL) methods. To address these gaps, we introduce the first publicly available HSI dataset of shredded black plastics from EOF vehicle, comprising four industrial polymers across 13 co-registered RGB, VNIR, SWIR, and MWIR scenes and their segmentation pipeline. We developed a multi-modal spectral-spatial framework that integrates foreground isolation, pixel-wise classification, and object-level majority voting. By adapting advanced hyperspectral transformers from earth observation and incorporating chemometric band selection, we achieve accurate classification of complex black plastics. The study establishes the first comprehensive benchmark using nine processing methods, including chemometric, machine learning, and DL architectures. To ensure reproducibility, the complete dataset and methodologies are publicly released, establishing a benchmark for a hyperspectral object-analysis pipeline in industrial inspection.

</details>


### [40] [Pixel-wise Geo-registration of Drone and Satellite Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28891)
*Qingyang Liu,David G Shatwell,Parth Parag Kulkarni,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SkyReg，一个用于像素级无人机到卫星地理配准的数据集和标准化基准，提供密集的逐像素地理位置监督，涵盖正交/透视视图、多种场景类型和相机配置。基于SkyReg训练的几何感知重建管线在跨视图像素级配准任务上取得了显著优于现有基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视图地理定位基准大多仅提供GPS标签，评估仅限于每张图像单个坐标，导致密集大地测量对齐（像素级配准）未被充分探索。需要一个支持密集逐像素地理监督的数据集和基准来推动这一任务的发展。

Method: 1) 构建SkyReg数据集，提供跨多样设置（正交/透视）、场景类型（城市、地标中心、郊区/乡村）和相机配置的密集逐像素地理位置标签；2) 评估广泛的基线方法，涵盖检索、特征匹配、单应性对齐和前馈3D重建；3) 基于SkyReg训练一个几何感知重建管线。

Result: 基于SkyReg训练的几何感知重建管线在跨视图配准任务上取得了最先进的结果，相比现有方法性能提升显著。

Conclusion: SkyReg填补了像素级跨视图地理配准领域缺乏标准化基准的空白，为密集地理对齐研究提供了可靠的数据基础，并验证了几何感知重建方法的有效性。

Abstract: Pixel-level cross-view geo-registration aims to align a query image (e.g., drone) to a geo-referenced satellite map so that every query pixel can be mapped to real-world GPS coordinates. Despite strong progress in cross-view geo-localization, existing benchmarks largely provide only GPS labels, limiting evaluation to a single coordinate per image and leaving dense geodetic alignment underexplored. We introduce SkyReg, a dataset and standardized benchmark for pixel-level drone-to-satellite geo-registration, providing dense per-pixel geo-location supervision across diverse settings (orthographic and perspective), scene types (urban, landmark-centric, suburban/rural), and camera configurations. Using SkyReg, we evaluate a broad set of baselines spanning retrieval, feature matching, homography-based alignment, and feed-forward 3D reconstruction. Finally, cross-view pairs from SkyReg, we train a geometry-aware reconstruction pipeline that achieves state-of-the-art results,improving performance by a significant margin.

</details>


### [41] [ReconSplat: Generalizable 3D Scene Reconstruction Beyond Observed Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28895)
*Giuseppe Stracquadanio,Kevin Raj,Julia Grabinski,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReconSplat是一个前馈式3D场景重建模型，通过将3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)作为中间可微场景表示，并与多视角潜在扩散模型(MV-LDM)集成，同时充当精化器和修复器，解决未观测区域的合理视角生成与几何一致性之间的权衡问题。模型通过变分3D潜在特征引导扩散过程，在RealEstate10K和DL3DV-10K基准上实现了优于现有方法的照片级新视角合成和精确深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景重建方法面临核心难点：在生成未观测区域（外推视角）时，既要保证生成视角的视觉合理性（外观真实性），又要维持场景几何一致性，两者往往难以兼顾。作者旨在解决这一长期存在的权衡问题。

Method: 1) 采用3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)作为中间可微场景表示；2) 集成多视角潜在扩散模型(MV-LDM)，同时扮演精化器(refiner)和修复器(inpainter)的角色，共同处理外观和场景几何；3) 将前馈3DGS编码的外观与几何变分3D潜在特征光栅化到2D潜在空间，用于引导扩散过程，从而强制几何一致性。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和DL3DV-10K真实世界基准上，ReconSplat能够生成照片级逼真的新视角和准确的深度图，在具有挑战性的外推设置中优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ReconSplat的成功表明，将3DGS的可微表示与潜在扩散模型相结合，可以在外推未知且具有挑战性的视角时，同时实现连贯且精确的场景几何与高质量新视角合成，有效缓解了视角生成合理性与几何一致性之间的固有矛盾。

Abstract: We introduce ReconSplat, a feed-forward model for 3D scene reconstruction that aims to address the longstanding trade-off between plausible view generation for unobserved regions and geometric consistency, providing both geometrically aligned novel views and sharp depth estimates. Our approach builds on 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) as an intermediate differentiable scene representation and integrates it with a multi-view latent diffusion model (MV-LDM) trained to act simultaneously as a refiner and an inpainter for appearance and scene geometry. We enforce geometric consistency by guiding the diffusion process with variational 3D latent features for appearance and geometry, encoded by the feed-forward 3DGS representation and rasterized to 2D latent space. ReconSplat produces both photorealistic novel views and accurate depth maps on real-world benchmarks, RealEstate10K and DL3DV-10K, outperforming existing methods in challenging extrapolation setups. Notably, ReconSplat allows the extrapolation of unseen and challenging viewpoints jointly with coherent and precise scene geometry.

</details>


### [42] [Coarse to Fine: Iterative Adversarial Neural Cellular Automata for Medical Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28909)
*Anh Thi Luu,Nick Lemke,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StyleGANCA，首个轻量级通用NCA-based生成对抗网络，将StyleGAN风格映射网络与自适应风格调制集成到多尺度神经细胞自动机（NCA）合成过程中，实现潜在控制图像生成。在BloodMNIST和PathMNIST上以617k参数取得最优FID/KID，支持下游多类分类器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗影像中隐私和法律限制阻碍了大规模数据共享，合成数据生成是替代方案。现有最先进的生成模型（如扩散模型）虽能生成高质量图像但计算开销大，限制了在资源受限硬件上的应用。需要一个轻量级且高效的生成模型。

Method: 提出StyleGANCA，将StyleGAN启发的映射网络和自适应风格调制整合进多尺度NCA合成过程，通过迭代局部交互实现潜在控制图像生成。在BloodMNIST和PathMNIST上对抗生成（adversarial）、变分（variational）、扩散（diffusion）及NCA基线进行比较评估。

Result: StyleGANCA在PathMNIST上以仅617k参数取得最佳FID和KID分数，图像质量与基线架构相当且参数显著更少。下游实验表明生成图像保留类别特定信息，有效支持多类分类器训练。

Conclusion: StyleGANCA证明了轻量级NCA-based GAN能够以极少的参数实现有竞争力的图像质量，为资源受限环境下的医疗影像合成数据生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large-scale, publicly available datasets have driven advances in deep learning, but privacy and legal restrictions often limit data sharing in medical imaging. Synthetic data generation offers a privacy-friendly alternative to enable the training of high-performance models on health data. While most state-of-the-art generative models produce high-quality images, they remain computationally expensive, which limits their applicability on resource-constrained hardware. We propose StyleGANCA, the first lightweight general-purpose NCA-based generative adversarial network. The architecture integrates a StyleGAN-inspired mapping network and adaptive style modulation into a multi-scale NCA synthesis process, enabling latent-controlled image generation through iterative local interactions. We evaluate StyleGANCA on BloodMNIST and PathMNIST against adversarial, variational, diffusion, and NCA-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that StyleGANCA achieves competitive image quality with substantially fewer parameters than baseline architectures, achieving the best FID and KID scores on PathMNIST with only 617k parameters. Furthermore, downstream experiments show that the generated images preserve class-specific information and effectively support the training of multi-class classifiers. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/MECLabTUDA/StyleGANCA

</details>


### [43] [mmIR: Frequency-Space Inverse Rendering for 3D Millimeter-Wave Radar ADC Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28913)
*Adnan Armouti,Yixuan Gao,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: mmIR是一个开源的可微分FMCW雷达逆向渲染器，通过将基于物理的前向模型拟合到真实采集数据，并从密集虚拟孔径重新渲染，从而合成高分辨率3D雷达数据。它利用LiDAR网格作为几何支架，通过端到端自动微分优化ITU物理材质、顶点法线和天线波束方向图。在7个室外和6个室内ColoRadar场景中，mmIR在距离-方位图上达到0.914的平均Pearson相关性（Sionna-RT为0.307），且级联成像雷达训练的模型可零训练迁移到单片雷达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D雷达数据稀缺：通用毫米波传感器因天线阵列小导致角分辨率仅数度；现有数据集只提供2D距离-方位图或稀疏点云，缺乏原始ADC信号。硬件扩展昂贵，合成孔径扫描在车队规模下不切实际，学习式合成方法又受制于数据匮乏本身，形成瓶颈。

Method: mmIR采用LiDAR辅助逆向渲染：1) 用LiDAR导出的网格作为几何骨架；2) 通过端到端自动微分优化逐顶点ITU物理材质、顶点法线和天线波束方向图；3) 应用相位相干MIMO前向模型，包含多次反射传播、极化和自由空间衍射；4) 拟合真实采集数据后，从密集虚拟孔径重渲染合成高分辨率3D雷达数据。

Result: 在7个室外和6个室内ColoRadar场景上，mmIR的距离-方位图平均Pearson相关性达0.914，远超Sionna-RT的0.307。级联成像雷达场景训练可迁移至共置单片雷达且无需重新训练（相关性0.554）。100×100单元的密集虚拟阵列可产生经LiDAR验证的单帧3D占用结果。

Conclusion: mmIR通过物理可微渲染突破了雷达数据稀缺瓶颈，实现了无需硬件扩展的高分辨率3D雷达数据合成，且具备跨雷达平台的迁移能力，为雷达感知研究和3D占用预测提供了新的数据生成范式。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D radar data is scarce. Commodity mmWave sensors use small antenna arrays that limit angular resolution to several degrees, and existing datasets provide only 2D range-azimuth maps or sparse point clouds rather than raw analog-to-digital converter (ADC) signals. Hardware scaling is expensive, synthetic-aperture scanning is impractical at fleet scale, and learned synthesis methods are bottlenecked by the very data shortage they aim to address. We present mmIR, an open-source differentiable frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar inverse renderer that fits a physics-based forward model to real captures and re-renders from dense virtual apertures to synthesize high-resolution 3D radar data. Because radar resolution is too coarse to recover geometry directly, mmIR performs LiDAR-assisted inverse rendering: using LiDAR-derived meshes as a geometric scaffold, mmIR optimizes per-vertex International Telecommunication Union (ITU) physics materials, vertex normals, and antenna beam patterns through end-to-end automatic differentiation of a phase-coherent multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) forward model with multi-bounce propagation, polarization, and free-space diffraction. On seven outdoor and six indoor ColoRadar scenes, mmIR achieves 0.914 mean Pearson correlation on range-azimuth maps versus 0.307 for Sionna-RT. Scenes trained on a cascaded imaging radar transfer to a co-located single-chip radar without re-training (0.554 correlation), and dense virtual arrays (100x100 elements) produce single-frame 3D occupancy validated against LiDAR. Project page: https://mmwave-inverse-rendering.github.io/

</details>


### [44] [ActiveAugment: Online Active Learning for Augmentation Selection in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28923)
*Noah Videcrantz,Mostafa Mehdipour Ghazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActiveAugment 提出将数据增强选择视为在线主动学习问题，基于模型预测不确定性和特征差异为每个样本动态选择最"脆弱"的增强方式，并采用联合监督分类与监督对比损失训练模型。在八个自然/医学图像基准数据集、三种训练范式下全面超越 AutoAugment、RandAugment 和 TrivialAugment，尤其在低标签预算和医学影像（数据稀缺、域偏移大）场景增益最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强策略是静态且与模型无关的：要么依赖昂贵的数据集特定策略搜索（如AutoAugment），要么对所有样本均匀随机施加增强（如RandAugment），完全不考虑模型当前的学习状态。这导致增强效率低且无法适应模型训练过程中的动态需求。

Method: 将增强选择建模为在线主动学习问题。每个训练minibatch中：1) 生成候选增强视图池；2) 结合模型预测不确定性与增强引起的特征差异对每个候选评分；3) 为每个样本选择当前模型最脆弱的增强；4) 使用联合监督分类损失和监督对比损失训练，增强类内不变性并保持类间分离。

Result: 在八个数据集（自然图像+医学图像）、CNN和Transformer架构、三种训练范式（从头训练、全微调、线性探测）下，对比八种主动选择策略，一致优于AutoAugment、RandAugment和TrivialAugment。医学图像上测试F1全面超越所有基线，低标签预算下增益最大。

Conclusion: 增强选择策略在训练过程中演化具有语义意义，选择策略的选择直接影响泛化性能。ActiveAugment展现了强大的跨域适应性，特别适用于数据稀缺和域偏移大的医学影像场景，为此类问题上提供统一、动态、模型感知的数据增强框架。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a cornerstone of deep learning pipelines, yet existing strategies treat it as a static, model-agnostic preprocessing step, either relying on expensive dataset-specific policy search or applying transformations uniformly at random, regardless of what the model has already learned. We introduce ActiveAugment, a unified framework that treats augmentation selection as an online active learning problem. For each training minibatch, ActiveAugment generates a pool of candidate augmented views and scores each candidate using a combination of the model's predictive uncertainty and the feature discrepancy induced by the augmentation. The augmentation under which the current model is most fragile is selected per sample, and the model is then trained with a joint supervised classification and supervised contrastive objective that enforces intra-class invariance to the selected augmentations while maintaining inter-class separation. We evaluate ActiveAugment on eight benchmark datasets spanning natural and medical imaging, using CNN and transformer architectures across three training regimes (training from scratch, full fine-tuning, and linear probing), and comparing eight active selection strategies for augmentation scoring. ActiveAugment outperforms AutoAugment, RandAugment, and TrivialAugment under controlled augmentation shifts across all domains and budgets, with the most pronounced gains at low labelling budgets. On medical imaging datasets, where data is scarce and domain shift relative to natural-image pretrained models is large, ActiveAugment achieves higher test F1 than all baselines, demonstrating strong cross-domain adaptability. Our analysis reveals that the augmentation selection policy evolves meaningfully during training and that strategy choice has a direct impact on generalisation. Code is available at: https://github.com/noahvide/ActiveAugment.

</details>


### [45] [NBS: No Bias Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28933)
*Vage Taamazyan,Zhuowen Shen,Stefan Hinterstoisser,Alberto Dall'Olio,Agastya Kalra,Aarrushi Shandilya,Xin Li,Wenping Wang,Kartik Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了立体匹配中必须使用架构归纳偏置的既有范式，证明一个完全无架构偏置的端到端Vision Transformer，仅通过在海量合成数据上训练，即可在精度和运行效率上同时超越所有依赖显式几何归纳偏置的最先进方法，为3D重建解锁了真正的规模化（scaling law）能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体重建是计算机视觉中最后一个仍被所有SOTA方法采用重型架构归纳偏置的任务。尽管已有研究证明通用方法可以解决该任务，但业界普遍认为归纳偏置对高质量和高效率是严格必需的。作者希望挑战这一认知，证明数据驱动学习可以超越显式工程化的几何先验。

Method: 提出一个完全去除架构归纳偏置的端到端Vision Transformer模型，不依赖任何显式的几何约束、代价体（cost volume）或立体匹配专门设计模块，在海量合成数据上进行训练，以纯数据驱动方式学习立体匹配。

Result: 实验表明，无归纳偏置的纯Transformer模型在立体匹配的精度上达到了SOTA水平，同时在运行效率上优于现有方法，证明纯粹的数据驱动学习可以超越显式设计的几何方法。

Conclusion: 显式归纳偏置不再是立体匹配的前提条件。该工作验证了纯数据驱动方法在立体任务中的可行性，为3D重建领域实现持续改进的规模化规律（scaling laws）奠定了基础，开创了无需手工几何先验的新范式。

Abstract: Stereo reconstruction is one of the last remaining Computer Vision tasks where all state-of-the-art methods employ a heavy architectural inductive bias. Even though it has been demonstrated that the task can be solved using general-purpose methods, it is widely believed that inductive biases in stereo are strictly necessary for both high-quality results and computational efficiency. We challenge this paradigm. In this paper, we demonstrate that both state-of-the-art accuracy and superior runtime efficiency are achievable with a model completely devoid of architectural inductive biases, relying instead on a simple, end-to-end Vision Transformer. By training on massive synthetic datasets, we show that pure data-driven learning can surpass explicitly engineered geometry. This work proves that explicit inductive biases are no longer a prerequisite for stereo matching, ultimately unlocking true scaling laws for continuous improvement in 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [46] [FractureFields: Contact-Aware Binary Multi-Field Transfer for Fractured 3D Gaussian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28982)
*Jianchen Wang,Runyang Qu,Fei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FractureFields 提出一种拓扑自适应的传输方法，用于破碎的3D高斯物体模拟。该方法在结构断裂后为每个碎片构建独立的质量场和动量场，通过单次P2G传递建立碎片专属的网格状态，独立推进各场演化，并在G2P更新时仅采样自身碎片的网格状态，从构造上消除跨碎片动量泄漏。对于再接触场景，引入动量守恒的接触投影，仅在两个碎片场相互接近时施加等大反向的法向冲量，保持自由分离。实验表明该方法在重建场景和受控再接触基准上消除了碎片间混合，并减少碰撞穿透。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Gaussia-MPM管道在断裂后仍保持单一欧拉速度场，导致断裂碎片共享插值支撑时读写同一网格节点，产生跨碎片动量泄漏，表现为残余粘附和物理上不真实的拉伸。作者认为断裂后模拟应当将结构断裂视为局部动力学状态的改变，而非仅仅是本构应力的改变。

Method: FractureFields包含三个关键组件：(1) 结构事件为碎片分配持久标识后，在单次粒子到网格(P2G)传递中构建碎片专属的质量场和动量场；(2) 独立推进每个碎片场演化；(3) 进行场感知的网格到粒子(G2P)更新，使粒子只采样自身碎片的网格状态。此外，添加动量守恒的接触投影，仅在两个碎片场接近时施加等大反向法向冲量以处理再接触。

Result: 在重建场景和受控再接触基准上的实验表明，碎片条件路由从构造上消除了实测的跨碎片混合，接触投影在不重新引入残余耦合的情况下减少了碰撞过程中的相互穿透。

Conclusion: 后断裂仿真应将结构断裂视为局部动力学状态的改变而非仅仅本构应力的改变。碎片专属场路由和动量守恒接触投影能够有效消除断裂碎片间的动量泄漏并正确处理再接触。

Abstract: Physics-integrated 3D Gaussian representations make it possible to simulate image-reconstructed assets directly as particles, but current Gaussia-MPM pipelines keep a single Eulerian velocity field even after fracture. When disconnected fragments share interpolation support, they still write to and read from the same grid nodes, producing cross-fragment momentum leakage that appears as residual adhesion and non-physical stretching. We present FractureFields, a topology-adaptive transfer for fractured 3D Gaussian objects. After a structural event assigns persistent fragment identities, FractureFields builds fragment-specific mass and momentum fields in a single P2G pass, advances each field independently, and performs a field-aware G2P update so particles only sample their own fragment's grid state. To handle re-contact, we add a momentum-conserving contact projection that applies equal and opposite normal impulses only when two fragment fields are approaching, preserving free separation otherwise. Experiments on reconstructed scenes and a controlled re-contact benchmark show that fragment-conditioned routing eliminates realized cross-fragment mixing by construction, while contact projection reduces interpenetration during collision without reintroducing residual coupling. Overall, we argue that post-fracture simulation should treat structural disconnection as a change in local dynamical state, not merely a change in constitutive stress.

</details>


### [47] [RoSe-SLAM: Robust Semantic-Aware Gaussian Splatting SLAM from Dynamic Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29003)
*Wenting Wang,Jiaxin Guo,Wenzhen Dong,Yun-Hui Liu,Charlie C. L. Wang,Yeung Yam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoSe-SLAM，一种利用2D基础模型语义特征增强的动态场景高斯溅射SLAM系统，通过时空运动掩码生成、遮挡感知关键帧选择和多视图语义一致性模块，实现从单目未标定输入中鲁棒解耦动态物体与静态背景，在动态TUM、Bonn和Wild-Mocap数据集上取得优于现有动态RGB SLAM基线的轨迹估计和静态场景重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLAM系统基于静态假设，在动态非结构化环境中因动态物体的干扰而出现显著的精度退化。现有动态语义SLAM依赖手工设计的语义标签，难以在动态和未标定环境中实现鲁棒的动态目标识别与静态场景重建。

Method: 1) 利用2D基础模型的语义特征蒸馏到3D高斯场中，增强动态目标识别与语义感知的多视图一致性；2) 提出空间-时间运动掩码生成模块，同时捕捉长期运动监控和短期瞬态动态；3) 在全局束调整中提出遮挡感知关键帧选择机制，以遮挡作为度量选择关键帧；4) 设计多视图语义一致性模块提升动态环境中的建图质量；5) 结合几何运动线索与语义先验，动态过滤不可靠观测并重建精确的静态场景几何。

Result: 在动态TUM、Bonn和Wild-Mocap基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在轨迹估计和静态场景建图方面均优于现有动态RGB SLAM基线，尤其在长期动态室内环境中表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过将基础模型语义特征与高斯溅射相结合，结合几何运动线索与语义先验，实现了鲁棒的动态场景解耦和高质量静态场景重建，验证了语义引导的动态SLAM方案的优越性。

Abstract: In dynamic and unstructured environments, conventional SLAM systems generally suffer from significant accuracy degeneration due to their static assumptions. In this work, we propose Robust Semantic-aware Gaussian Splatting SLAM (RoSe-SLAM), to address the dynamic challenge by a holistic semantic scene understanding from uncalibrated monocular inputs, achieving accurate camera tracking and high-quality geometry reconstruction. Unlike conventional semantic SLAM using handcrafted semantic labels, our RoSe-SLAM exploits the semantic feature from 2D foundation model to enhance the dynamic tracking and mapping performance. By distilling the rich semantic features to our Gaussian fields, our method effectively identifies dynamic distractors and achieves semantic-aware multi-view consistency, significantly enhancing the geometric reconstruction and scene inpainting. Specifically, we propose a spatial-temporal motion mask generation module, enabling both long-term motion monitoring and short-term transient dynamics capturing, achieving robust and effective disentanglement of dynamic objects and static backgrounds. During global bundle adjustment, we propose an occlusion-aware keyframe selection mechanism to prioritize the occlusion as metric to pick the keyframes, and a multi-view semantic consistency module to improve the mapping quality in dynamic environments. By combining geometric motion cues with semantic priors, our system dynamically filters unreliable observations and reconstructs accurate static scene geometry. Extensive experiments conducted on benchmark datasets including dynamic TUM, Bonn and Wild-Mocap datasets, demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance in both trajectory estimation and static scene mapping, outperforming existing dynamic RGB SLAM baselines in long-term dynamic indoor environments.

</details>


### [48] [Towards Fully Automated Medical Imaging Code Generation via Validation-based Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29016)
*Zixiao Zhao,Jing Sun,Zhe Hou,Cheng-Hao Cai,Qian Liu,Mengze Li,Zijian Zhang,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoMedImg是一个多智能体框架，用于全自动医学图像处理代码生成，通过两阶段（规划与编码）编排专门智能体，结合多阶段验证、自动上下文工程和跨项目自适应流水线合成，在六个医学影像数据集上实现零人工干预，分割Dice最高0.90，分类准确率达99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在复杂领域（如医学图像处理）的代码生成能力受限，缺乏领域知识和验证机制，需要大量人工干预才能生成可靠的流水线。现有方法无法满足医学图像处理这类对正确性和可靠性要求极高的领域任务需求。

Method: 提出AutoMedImg多智能体框架，包含两个阶段：1）规划阶段，执行数据集分析和架构设计，结合语义与形式化验证；2）编码阶段，并行生成模块并执行静态检查、运行测试和组装验证。通过多阶段验证抑制错误传播，利用领域知识库、共享记忆和验证反馈实现自动上下文工程，并通过跨项目自适应流水线合成机制基于项目相似性检索已验证组件以提升生成效率。

Result: 在六个多样且成熟的医学影像数据集上，使用五种骨干大语言模型进行广泛评估，AutoMedImg实现零人工干预，分割任务Dice分数最高达0.90，分类任务准确率达99%。

Conclusion: AutoMedImg通过多智能体协作和多阶段验证机制，有效解决了通用LLM在医学图像处理代码生成中的局限性，实现了全自动、高可靠且具跨项目学习能力的领域专用代码生成。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable promise in program generation for small-scale and conventional application development; however, they remain limited when applied to complex, domain-specific tasks such as medical image processing. General-purpose models lack explicit domain knowledge and robust validation mechanisms to ensure correctness, often requiring substantial human intervention to produce reliable processing pipelines. To address these limitations, we propose AutoMedImg, a multi-agent framework for fully automated medical image processing code generation. AutoMedImg orchestrates specialised agents across two phases: a Planning Phase that performs dataset analysis and architecture design with semantic and formal verification, and a Coding Phase that generates modules in parallel with static checking, execution testing, and assembly validation. This multi-stage validation mitigates error propagation throughout generation, while comprehensive auto-context engineering combining domain-specific knowledge bases, shared memory, and validation feedback automates context construction without manual prompting. A cross-project adaptive pipeline synthesis mechanism further accumulates validated pipelines and retrieves proven components for new tasks based on project similarity, enhancing generation efficiency through cross-project learning. Extensive evaluation across six diverse and well-established medical imaging datasets with five backbone LLMs demonstrates that AutoMedImg achieves zero human intervention, with Dice scores of up to 0.90 for segmentation tasks and 99% accuracy for classification.

</details>


### [49] [Explainable Multi-Loss Distillation Framework for Efficient and Interpretable Shrimp Disease Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29027)
*Anh Nguyen Quynh,Khang Nguyen Quoc,Luyl-Da Quach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT框架，利用多损失知识蒸馏实现虾病文本分类，并集成LIME和SHAP可解释性分析，在多个蒸馏目标下取得优于监督基线的性能，同时保持计算效率与强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虾类病害对越南等产出国进出口造成重大影响，现有分类方法主要基于图像，只能在病害显现后期发挥效用。基于文本的分类有望实现早期、及时的病害检测，但目前缺乏有效方案，因此需要探索文本分类方法以弥补图像方法的局限。

Method: 提出SALT（Shrimp disease text Analysis with multi-Loss disTillation）框架，核心是多损失知识蒸馏，并集成LIME和SHAP两种可解释性分析方法来评估模型预测、解释所学语言特征。通过多个蒸馏目标联合优化，在性能与计算效率之间取得平衡。

Result: 实验表明SALT在多个蒸馏目标下取得具有竞争力的性能，优于监督基线，并在预测性能与计算效率之间提供有利权衡。同时表现出强可解释性，能准确识别与病害描述相关的关键语言特征和语义模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明基于知识蒸馏的文本分类在未来早期虾病诊断及相关研究方向具有巨大应用潜力，为病害早期检测提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Shrimp disease classification has become an urgent issue due to its significant impact on the import-export output of producing countries, particularly Vietnam. Most existing studies focus on image-based classification, which typically operates at the late stage of disease manifestation. Therefore, text-based classification has the potential to enable early and timely disease detection. To address this limitation, we introduce the SALT (Shrimp disease text Analysis with multi-Loss disTillation) framework, which incorporates explainability analysis using Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations (LIME) and SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to evaluate model predictions and interpret the learned linguistic features. Experimental results demonstrate that SALT achieves competitive performance across multiple distillation objectives, outperforming supervised baselines while providing a favorable trade-off between predictive performance and computational efficiency. Moreover, it exhibits strong explainability, accurately identifying key linguistic features and semantic patterns relevant to disease descriptions. These findings highlight the potential of knowledge distillation-based text classification for future applications in early shrimp disease diagnosis and related research directions.

</details>


### [50] [DocIntent: Answerability-Guided Agentic Restoration for Real-World Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29037)
*Zihan Huang,Shihang Wu,Junle Liu,Peirong Zhang,Yongxin Shi,Xuhan Zheng,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DocIntent，一个无需训练的"可回答性引导的智能体恢复框架"，用于真实世界退化文档的视觉问答任务。该框架通过评估问题可回答性、识别任务相关退化并选择性调用恢复工具，配合基于对比的回滚机制来验证和修正每一步恢复操作，从而提升MLLMs在退化文档上的VQA能力。在WildDoc基准上的实验表明其持续提高了多种开源和闭源MLLM的平均得分和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界退化（模糊、阴影、畸变、摩尔纹）严重损害MLLMs的文档VQA能力。现有恢复方法需要人工设计策略且耗费大量专业知识；现有的智能体恢复框架主要针对自然图像并追求感知质量，忽视了恢复应服务于下游任务而非优化通用图像质量指标。因此需要一个面向文档VQA任务的自适应恢复框架。

Method: DocIntent包含三个核心组件：(1) 问题可回答性评估：先判断当前退化状态下问题是否可回答；(2) 任务相关退化识别与选择性恢复：识别与当前问题相关的退化类型并仅调用相应的恢复工具；(3) 基于比较的回滚机制：通过对比验证每个恢复步骤，当问题相关证据变得更难辨认时回退该步骤。整个过程无需额外训练的退化分类器或图像质量评估模型，属于训练免费框架。

Result: 在WildDoc基准上的大量实验表明，DocIntent一致性地提升了不同开源和闭源MLLMs的平均得分和回答一致性。

Conclusion: 智能体驱动的恢复应以下游任务（如文档VQA）为导向而非追求通用图像质量指标；基于可回答性引导和回滚验证的免费训练框架能够有效提升MLLMs在真实退化文档上的问答性能。

Abstract: Real-world degradations such as blur, shadow, distortion, and moire patterns severely impair the document question-answering capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Applying restoration tools before Visual Question Answering (VQA) is an intuitive solution. However, existing restoration approaches remain limited, as manually designing and executing restoration strategies is labor-intensive and requires domain expertise. Agentic restoration offers new possibilities for automation, yet existing frameworks primarily target natural images and pursue perceptual quality, overlooking that restoration should serve downstream tasks rather than optimize generic image quality metrics. To this end, we explore the value of agentic restoration for real-world degraded document VQA and propose DocIntent, a training-free Answerability-Guided Agentic Restoration framework. DocIntent first assesses question answerability, then identifies task-relevant degradations and selectively invokes restoration tools. A Comparison-Based Rollback mechanism validates each restoration step and reverts it when question-relevant evidence becomes less decipherable. The entire process requires no additional pretrained degradation classifier or image quality assessment model. Extensive experiments on the WildDoc benchmark show that DocIntent consistently improves the average score and consistency of different open- and closed-source MLLMs. The code and experimental data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [51] [sRGB Real Noise Modeling via Noise-Aware Sampling with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29038)
*Dongjin Kim,Donggoo Jung,Sungyong Baik,Tae Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新的归一化流（NF）框架，通过估计潜在相机设置来改进真实噪声图像建模，无需在采样阶段使用相机元数据，能够同时进行噪声分类和多样化噪声图像的生成，并在基准数据集上取得了领先的去噪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CNN去噪方法假设噪声服从既定分布，难以应对真实世界的噪声。虽然已有工作使用GAN和NF等生成方法建模真实噪声，但近期方法依赖相机元数据（即使在采样阶段也需要），限制了实用性。本文旨在无需采样阶段元数据的情况下估计底层相机设置，从而改进噪声建模并生成多样化的噪声分布。

Method: 引入一个新的归一化流（NF）框架，该框架具备两个功能：（1）根据相机设置对噪声进行分类；（2）基于估计的相机设置生成各种不同分布的噪声图像。通过在生成过程中估计隐式的相机参数，避免了对采样阶段元数据的依赖。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的模型生成了高质量的真实噪声，并在基准数据集上的图像去噪性能方面达到了领先水平。

Conclusion: 通过NF框架估计潜在相机设置，能够在无需元数据的情况下实现更好的真实噪声建模和多样化噪声生成，从而显著提升去噪性能，为处理真实世界噪声提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Noise poses a widespread challenge in signal processing, particularly when it comes to denoising images. Although convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have exhibited remarkable success in this field, they are predicated upon the belief that noise follows established distributions, which restricts their practicality when dealing with real-world noise. To overcome this limitation, several efforts have been taken to collect noisy image datasets from the real world. Generative methods, employing techniques such as generative adversarial networks (GANs) and normalizing flows (NFs), have emerged as a solution for generating realistic noisy images. Recent works model noise using camera metadata, however requiring metadata even for sampling phase. In contrast, in this work, we aim to estimate the underlying camera settings, enabling us to improve noise modeling and generate diverse noise distributions. To this end, we introduce a new NF framework that allows us to both classify noise based on camera settings and generate various noisy images. Through experimental results, our model demonstrates exceptional noise quality and leads in denoising performance on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [52] [Di$^2$CycleSB: Towards High-Quality Unsupervised Nighttime Visibility Enhancement via Schrödinger Bridge Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29043)
*Hanting Li,Xin Sun,Wei Ye,Jungong Han,Liang-jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Di²CycleSB，一种基于动态积分图像先验的无监督Cycle Schrödinger Bridge Transformer框架，用于夜间图像可见度增强，无需正则化约束和图像分解即可实现端到端的光效应抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间可见度增强中，光效应污染是重大挑战。现有方法通过先验驱动的正则化来估计和分解光效应，但受到手工先验的限制以及分解问题本身的无约束性（ill-posed nature）的制约。

Method: 1) 提出一种新型光效应估计器，通过聚合动态积分图像表示来参数化高斯类自适应先验，用于非均匀光晕估计；2) 提出先验信息生成器（prior-informed Generator），利用光效应表示引导Transformer块中的长距离依赖建模；3) 将光效应抑制建模为Schrödinger桥问题，构建带循环一致性约束的前向和后向桥以实现增强。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验证明了Di²CycleSB在夜间可见度增强方面的显著有效性，特别实现了无需任何正则化约束和图像分解的端到端光效应抑制。

Conclusion: Di²CycleSB通过动态积分图像先验引导的无监督Cycle Schrödinger Bridge框架，有效解决了夜间光效应抑制问题，为夜间可见度增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: Light-effect contamination poses a significant challenge to nighttime visibility enhancement. Most methods suppress light effects by estimating and decomposing them through prior-driven regularization, yet they are often limited by hand-crafted priors and ill-posed nature of decomposition. This work proposes Di$^2$CycleSB, a unsupervised Cycle Schrödinger Bridge Transformer framework guided by dynamic integral image priors, for high-quality unsupervised nighttime visibility enhancement. Specifically, a novel light-effect estimator is introduced to parameterize Gaussian-like adaptive priors by aggregating dynamic integral image representations for non-uniform glow estimation. Then, we propose a prior-informed Generator that exploits light-effect representations to guide long-range dependency modeling within our specific Transformer blocks. We formulate light-effect suppression as a Schrödinger bridge problem and construct forward and backward bridges with cycle consistency constraints to achieve visually pleasing enhancement. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the remarkable effectiveness of our Di$^2$CycleSB in enhancing nighttime visibility. In particular, it achieves effective end-to-end light-effect suppression without any regularization constraints and image decomposition. The code and models are available at https://github.com/LHTcode/Di2CycleSB.

</details>


### [53] [DReSG: Diffusion Residuals for Stylized Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29048)
*Zhongliang Liu,Wenjie Liu,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DReSG提出了一种基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的残差反馈框架，通过将注意力引导的扩散提案表示为相对当前渲染的残差目标，并逐步将其吸收到共享高斯场景中，实现参考引导的风格化。该方法结合了残差强度调制、覆盖感知视图选择和冲突过滤颜色更新，在保持场景结构和跨视图稳定性的同时实现竞争性的风格化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS风格化方法存在两个问题：基于VGG特征的方法渲染视图优化稳定但难以充分表达参考风格；基于扩散模型的方法虽有更强的图像先验，但直接逐视图或基于分数的扩散引导会导致视图漂移、局部伪影和外观更新难以控制。

Method: DReSG将注意力引导的扩散提案表示为相对当前渲染的残差目标，通过多视图高斯反馈逐步吸收残差到共享高斯场景中。在目标构建阶段调制残差强度以增强稳定性，在多视图拟合阶段组合覆盖感知视图选择与冲突过滤颜色更新以保证可控性。

Result: 大量实验表明，DReSG在参考引导风格化上达到竞争性效果，同时更好地保留了场景结构和跨视图稳定性。

Conclusion: DReSG通过3Dgrounded的残差反馈机制有效解决了扩散模型引导中的视图漂移和局部伪影问题，实现了稳定可控的3DGS风格化，为基于扩散先验的3D场景风格化提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Reference-guided stylization of scenes represented by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is important for efficient and controllable 3D content creation. Existing VGG-feature-based 3D stylization methods provide stable rendered-view optimization, but often under-represent expressive reference style cues; diffusion models offer stronger image priors, yet direct per-view or score-based diffusion guidance can lead to view drift, local artifacts, and hard-to-control appearance updates. We present DReSG, a 3D-grounded residual-feedback framework for stylized Gaussian splatting. DReSG represents attention-guided diffusion proposals as residual targets relative to the current render, and progressively absorbs these residuals into a shared Gaussian scene through multi-view Gaussian feedback. To make this feedback stable and controllable, DReSG modulates residual strength during target construction and combines coverage-aware view selection with conflict-filtered color updates during multi-view fitting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DReSG achieves competitive reference-guided stylization while better preserving scene structure and cross-view stability. Our project page is available at https://vpx-ecnu.github.io/DReSG-website/.

</details>


### [54] [AdapToPASS: Ambiguity-aware Adaptive Spherical Transformer for Panoramic Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29081)
*Soumyaratna Debnath,Weiming Zhang,Shriram Damodaran,Dingwen Xiao,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdapToPASS，一种仿生的球面Transformer，通过自适应球面注意力（AdaSpA）动态建模上下文歧义，以及双焦距球面表示和边界监督来处理几何歧义，从而在未见过的球面变换下实现鲁棒的360°全景语义分割。在Stanford2D3D和WildPASS上分别超越次优方法相对mIoU +13.38%和+18.77%，并推出<2M参数的轻量变体AdapToPASS-Swift。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有球面Transformer通常假设标准球面结构和稳定视角，但真实场景中相机运动不受约束，导致上下文和几何歧义。这些架构缺乏处理此类歧义的自适应机制，对未见过的球面变换鲁棒性不足。作者受生物感知天然具备歧义感知能力启发，旨在设计能动态适应上下文和几何扰动信号可靠性变化的球面Transformer。

Method: 1) 系统分析了现有球面Transformer架构在各种未见球面变换下的表现；2) 提出AdapToPASS，由三个核心组件构成：AdaSpA（自适应球面注意力）块，根据局部上下文歧义动态调节注意力权重，模拟自适应、上下文驱动的生物感知；Bifocal Spherical Representation，平衡视场角与空间分辨率以处理几何歧义；边界监督机制，借鉴生物视觉的边界敏感性。此外还设计了轻量级变体AdapToPASS-Swift。

Result: 在室内和室外语义分割任务上，AdapToPASS一致超越先前最先进方法。在未见球面变换下，相对mIoU在Stanford2D3D上比次优方法提升+13.38%，在WildPASS上提升+18.77%。AdapToPASS-Swift参数量少于2M，超越紧凑型基线模型同时保持对球面变换的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将生物感知的歧义适应机制引入球面Transformer设计，AdapToPASS有效解决了全景分割中因相机运动导致的上下文与几何歧义问题，在标准场景和未见变换下均显著优于现有方法。轻量变体证明了该框架在保持高效性的同时仍具备强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Spherical Transformers have emerged as a promising framework for panoramic semantic segmentation (PASS) by operating directly on spherical geometry and alleviating projection-induced distortions. However, existing architectures often assume canonical spherical structure and stable viewpoints, which are frequently violated in real-world imagery due to unconstrained camera motion, introducing contextual and geometric ambiguity. Consequently, they lack adaptive mechanisms to handle such ambiguity, limiting robustness to unseen spherical transformations. In contrast, biological perception is inherently ambiguity-aware, adapting to fluctuations in cue reliability caused by geometric and contextual variations to maintain stable interpretation under complex transformations. Motivated by this, we first systematically analyze existing PASS architectures under various unseen spherical transformations. We then introduce AdapToPASS, a novel bio-inspired Spherical Transformer that adaptively models contextual and geometric ambiguities for robust PASS. At its core, Adaptive Spherical Attention (AdaSpA) blocks dynamically modulate attention according to local contextual ambiguity, mimicking adaptive, context-driven biological perception. To address geometric ambiguity, AdapToPASS employs Bifocal Spherical Representation to balance field of view and spatial resolution, together with boundary supervision inspired by the boundary-sensitive nature of biological vision. Across indoor and outdoor semantic segmentation, AdapToPASS consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods. Under unseen spherical transformations, it surpasses the next-best method by +13.38% relative mIoU on Stanford2D3D and +18.77% on WildPASS. We further introduce AdapToPASS-Swift, a lightweight variant with fewer than 2M parameters, which surpasses compact baselines while retaining robustness to spherical transformations.

</details>


### [55] [Elastic Triangle Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29106)
*Tian Shi,Shenhan Qian,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析三角形样条绘制（triangle splatting）中的核函数设计，提出一种具有双侧梯度支持和自适应边界值的弹性核函数，在形状重建和新视角合成任务中优于现有核函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角形样条绘制作为连接神经渲染与传统网格管线的方法，其核心是核函数，但现有方法对核函数的选择不一致，且对其优化行为的分析仅限于非结构化三角面片集合的新视角合成，缺乏对核函数在通用光度优化场景下的系统研究。

Method: 将三角形样条绘制视为通用的光度优化工具，通过两个互补任务（网格优化用于形状重建、三角面片集合优化用于新视角合成）对比不同核函数的性质；提出弹性核函数，具有跨边界双侧梯度支持和自适应边界值。

Result: 在隔离比较下，弹性核函数在形状重建任务中优于现有核函数，并在大多数新视角合成基准上取得更好表现，验证了核函数设计对三角形样条绘制有效性和通用性的关键作用。

Conclusion: 核函数的设计选择对三角形样条绘制的优化鲁棒性和性能至关重要，所提出的弹性核函数实现了更广泛的应用场景覆盖，凸显了该方向的研究价值。

Abstract: While neural rendering methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting achieve remarkable visual fidelity, traditional polygonal meshes remain the backbone of established graphics pipelines. Triangle splatting bridges this gap by optimizing triangle primitives as differentiable splats, producing representations that are closer to mesh-based workflows. Central to these methods is the kernel function that softens triangle boundaries to propagate gradients to vertex positions. Existing triangle splatting methods make inconsistent choices of kernel functions, and analysis of these kernels' optimization behavior has been limited to unstructured triangle soups for novel-view synthesis. In this work, we consider triangle splatting as a generic tool for photometric optimization, comparing kernel properties through two complementary tasks: mesh optimization for shape reconstruction and triangle soup optimization for novel-view synthesis. Along with the analysis, we introduce an elastic kernel function that features bilateral gradient support across the boundary and an adaptive boundary value, which are shown to be essential for robust optimization. Under isolated comparison, our elastic kernel outperforms existing kernels on shape reconstruction and in the majority of novel-view synthesis benchmarks, demonstrating the importance of kernel design in the effectiveness and versatility of triangle splatting.

</details>


### [56] [NFAD: Nuisance-Filtered Anomaly Detection Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29112)
*Dat Cao,Son Nghiem,Phan Nguyen,Jun Rekimoto,Jhih-Ciang Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Nuisance-Filtered Anomaly Detection (NFAD)框架，通过估计并抑制由成像条件变化（光照、背景、视角等）引起的冗余（nuisance）子空间，解决工业异常检测在分布偏移下的性能退化问题。NFAD在AeBAD-S基准上达到91.0%的图像级AUROC，创下新SOTA，同时在标准基准（VisA、Real-IAD、MVTec AD）上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测基准是在受控采集条件下构建的，性能已趋于饱和，但无法直接迁移到真实部署场景。真实环境中光照、背景、视角等成像条件变化会使正常样本偏离学习到的正常分布，产生虚假异常响应，导致检测失效。现有方法缺乏在无异常标签和无目标域数据条件下缓解这一问题的有效手段。

Method: NFAD框架通过以下步骤实现：1) 利用内容保持扰动（content-preserving perturbations）在特征空间中生成匹配的特征位移；2) 从这些位移中估计冗余（nuisance）子空间；3) 在推理时抑制该子空间对异常残差的贡献。该子空间同时支持两个互补分支：全投影（full projection）用于图像级检测，选择性抑制（selective suppression）用于像素级定位，以保留局部缺陷证据。

Result: 在专为采集偏移下的AD设计的AeBAD-S基准上，NFAD获得91.0%图像级AUROC，达到新SOTA。在标准基准VisA、Real-IAD和MVTec AD上，NFAD保持与现有方法相当的性能，说明其鲁棒性不以牺牲常规AD性能为代价。

Conclusion: 显式抑制采集条件引入的冗余变化能够有效提升AD在分布偏移下的性能，同时保持标准场景下的强表现。该工作表明，将环境因素建模为可分离的冗余子空间是实现鲁棒工业异常检测的有效途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in anomaly detection (AD) for industrial inspection have pushed performance on standard benchmarks toward saturation. However, strong benchmark performance does not necessarily translate to real-world deployment, as these benchmarks are primarily collected under controlled acquisition conditions. Changes in illumination, background, viewpoint, and other environmental factors can shift normal samples away from the learned normal distribution and cause false anomaly responses. We address AD under such distribution shifts by explicitly modeling nuisance variation from changing imaging conditions in feature space. Without anomaly labels or target-domain data, our Nuisance-Filtered Anomaly Detection (NFAD) framework estimates a nuisance subspace from matched feature displacements induced by content-preserving perturbations and suppresses its contribution to anomaly residuals at inference. The same subspace supports two complementary branches: full projection for image-level detection and selective suppression for pixel-level localization, preserving evidence of localized defects. On AeBAD-S, a benchmark specifically designed for AD under acquisition shifts, NFAD achieves 91.0\% image-level AUROC, establishing a new state of the art. Notably, this robustness does not come at the expense of conventional AD performance: NFAD remains competitive on standard benchmarks that do not explicitly evaluate distribution shift, including VisA, Real-IAD, and MVTec AD. These results show that explicitly suppressing such nuisance variation improves AD under distribution shift while preserving strong performance in standard settings.

</details>


### [57] [GramLoop: Training-Free Gram-Gated Replay for Robust Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29113)
*Yang Chen,Canyu Shen,Xinzhe Rao,Yuanyi Yan,Yunlu Chen,Meng Tang,Teng Long,Vincent Tao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GramLoop是一个无需训练（training-free）的推理时增强框架，通过在冻结的DINOv3骨干网络内部重放短Transformer窗口来提升分布偏移下的密集预测性能。它利用逐层的余弦Gram一致性作为控制机制，确保重放过程不破坏DINOv3用于保持空间结构的成对补丁关系。在COCO-O上提升mAP +0.252和有效鲁棒性 +0.250，同时在五个分布偏移基准上均超越基线并保持ADE20K的干净性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布偏移条件下，冻结的DINOv3密集预测模型性能会显著退化。作者旨在不改变模型权重、任务适配器或预测头的情况下，通过在视觉骨干内部增加推理计算来提升模型的分布偏移鲁棒性。挑战在于重复的Transformer块计算必须能精炼密集特征，同时不能破坏DINOv3依赖的成对补丁关系来维持空间结构。

Method: GramLoop是一个训练无关的框架：(1) 在冻结的DINOv3骨干内部重放一个短的Transformer窗口；(2) 每次重放通过最终层的余弦Gram一致性进行控制；(3) 每个候选提议通过冻结的后续层（suffix）传播，并与标准DINOv3轨迹进行比对；(4) 在重放窗口的端点通过逐补丁门控机制决定是否接受该提议。

Result: 在目标检测和语义分割任务中，涵盖多种失真、扰动和自然域偏移，GramLoop在全部五个分布偏移基准上均优于配对的DINOv3基线。在COCO-O上mAP提升+0.252，有效鲁棒性提升+0.250，同时保持ADE20K上的干净性能不变。

Conclusion: GramLoop证明了在不改动权重或网络头的前提下，仅通过在视觉骨干内部添加受控的推理时重放计算，即可有效提升冻结大型视觉Transformer模型在分布偏移下的密集预测鲁棒性，且不牺牲干净数据的性能。

Abstract: We aim to improve frozen DINOv3 dense-prediction models under distribution shift by adding inference computation inside the visual backbone, without changing model weights, task adapters, or prediction heads. The challenge is that repeated transformer-block computation must refine dense features without disrupting the pairwise patch relations that DINOv3 uses to preserve spatial structure. We introduce GramLoop, a training-free framework that replays a short transformer window and controls each replay through final-layer cosine-Gram consistency. Each proposal is propagated through the frozen suffix, measured against the standard DINOv3 trajectory, and accepted through a patchwise gate at the replay-window endpoint. Across object detection and semantic segmentation under corruptions, perturbations, and natural shifts, GramLoop improves all five shifted benchmarks over the paired DINOv3 baseline. On COCO-O, it improves mAP by +0.252 and Effective Robustness by +0.250, while preserving clean ADE20K performance. Code will be released.

</details>


### [58] [Acoustically Grounded Cost Learning for Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29121)
*Tianrui Hui,Shaofei Huang,Qisong Han,Yaxiong Wang,Lechao Cheng,Zhedong Zheng,Zhun Zhong,Richang Hong,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AGCL框架，将开放词汇音频-视觉语义分割从类无关的语义分割重构为类别特定的任务，通过音频调制的代价生成和时序聚合模块发现类内声音来源，通过协同干扰挖掘策略区分类间混淆类别，在AVSBench-OV数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OV-AVSS方法基于类无关的前景定义，将语义多样的物体归入异构正样本集，导致模型学习到不稳定的发声模式并产生不可靠的提议。这一根本性缺陷促使作者将目标重构为类别特定的求解方式。

Method: 提出AGCL（声学接地代价学习）框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) AMCG和AGTA模块，通过低侵入式音频注入机制实现帧级发声区域高亮和视频级时序精炼，用于类内发声性发现；(2) SDM协同干扰挖掘策略，选择性惩罚声学和语义双重混淆的负类别，学习更具判别性的决策边界；(3) 将静态的、与音频无关的视觉-文本先验转化为动态的、音频接地的代价表示。

Result: 在AVSBench-OV数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，尤其在未见类别上的性能提升更为突出。

Conclusion: 通过将任务重构为类别特定求解并结合音频接地机制与干扰挖掘策略，可以有效解决开放词汇音频-视觉语义分割中类无关前景定义导致的模型不稳定和不精确问题。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation (OV-AVSS) aims to perform pixel-level segmentation of sound-emitting objects from an open set of categories. The previous method relies on a class-agnostic foreground definition, which groups semantically diverse objects into a heterogeneous positive set, causing the model to learn unstable sounding patterns and produce unreliable proposals. To address this, we reformulate the objective to be category-specific and propose a novel Acoustically Grounded Cost Learning (AGCL) framework to transform the static, audio-agnostic visual-text priors into dynamic, audio-grounded cost representations. For intra-category soundingness discovery, we devise Audio-Modulated Cost Generation (AMCG) and Audio-Guided Temporal Aggregation (AGTA) modules to enable both frame-level sounding region highlighting and video-level temporal refinement with a low-intrusive audio injection mechanism. For inter-category distractor discrimination, we introduce a Synergistic Distractor Mining (SDM) strategy, which selectively penalizes acoustically and semantically confusing negative categories to learn more discriminative decision boundaries. Extensive experiments on the AVSBench-OV dataset demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches, particularly on unseen categories. Code is available at https://github.com/spyflying/AGCL.

</details>


### [59] [Dancing Stick Figures: An Introductory Dataset for Training Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29123)
*Jin Hyuk Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dancing Stick Figures是一个合成的视频生成数据集，专门针对视频生成模型训练中的三大障碍（迭代反馈慢、数据难获取、评分粗糙）而设计。该数据集包含4,020个视频片段，每帧保留完整的状态信息和逐像素标注（深度、法线、部位标签），并提供了可在单块工作站GPU上运行的参考训练任务和Colab工作流，以及数据集特定的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型从零训练面临三个核心障碍：反馈循环长（失败只能在训练完成后发现，导致每次修复都要重新运行）；数据获取困难（强模型依赖的语料库和配方通常未公开且异构复杂）；评分方式粗糙（开放式生成没有唯一正确答案，聚合分数无法定位是哪个样本失败或哪个属性失败）。作者构建Dancing Stick Figures正是为了逐一击破这三个障碍。

Method: 该方法构建一个合成的火柴人跳舞视频数据集，具体包括：(1) 为迭代速度设计64x64、64帧的参考任务，可在单块工作站GPU上重复训练；(2) 发布0.79GB的训练集，含4,020个视频片段，包括1,340个六秒源动作每个从三个相机视角渲染，由确定性数据生成管线产生，并提供检查点和在16GB Tesla T4上以缩减预算运行参考训练管线的Colab工作流；(3) 为评分保留每帧的生成状态（ARDY cskel27关节点位置、相机、身体参数、源动作）以及逐像素深度、表面法线和部位标签，支持数据集特定的可见拓扑和局部运动指标，并通过损坏实验暴露这些指标的敏感性和盲区。

Result: 该数据集成功解决了三大训练障碍：将训练任务规模化到单GPU可重复运行；提供了完整可复现的数据生成管线和运行工作流；并借助丰富的逐帧标注实现了比聚合分数更精细的数据集特定评估指标。

Conclusion: Dancing Stick Figures证明了精心设计的小规模合成数据集可以有效缓解视频生成模型训练中迭代慢、数据不可达、评分粗糙的问题，为视频生成研究提供了可访问、可复现且可精细化评估的训练平台。

Abstract: Training a video-generation model from scratch is hard for reasons that precede model design. The feedback loop is long: a failure that appears only after a training run can make each attempted fix another run. The data are hard to reach: the corpora and recipes behind strong models are large, heterogeneous, and often unreleased. And scoring is blunt: open-ended generation has no single correct output, and an aggregate score does not by itself establish whether a sample succeeds or which property failed. Dancing Stick Figures is a synthetic video dataset built against these three obstacles. For iteration speed, its 64x64, 64-frame reference task is sized for practical repeated training on a single workstation GPU. For accessibility, the release is a 0.79-GB training tier of 4,020 video clips--1,340 six-second source motions, each rendered from three cameras by a deterministic dataset-generation harness--with checkpoints and a Colab workflow that reruns the reference training pipeline at reduced budget on a 16 GB Tesla T4. For scoring, every frame retains its generating state (ARDY cskel27 joint positions, camera, body parameters, and source motion) and per-pixel depth, surface normals, and part labels. These annotations support dataset-specific metrics for visible topology and part-wise motion; corruptions expose their sensitivities and blind spots.

</details>


### [60] [Efficient Language-to-Vision Feature Injection for Referring Single-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29126)
*Han Wang,Yuxuan Liu,Yuhan Sun,Jian Yang,Xiaotong Xu,Yixuan Lv,Zhuang Zhou,Shengyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LVTrack是一个纯Transformer框架的指代单目标跟踪方法，通过模式条件门控特征注入器调节文本引导、利用冻结的视觉-语言预训练模型降低成本，并结合混合位置编码、轻量内存机制和高斯平滑KL损失提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指代单目标跟踪的核心难点在于语言在不同阶段的作用不同：语言对目标初始化（grounding）至关重要，但在跟踪过程中过度强调会导致语义漂移。此外，现有方法通常需要昂贵的视觉-语言对齐训练，计算成本高。

Method: LVTrack包含以下关键技术：(1) 模式条件门控特征注入器（Mode-conditioned Gated Feature Injector），自适应调节文本引导以缓解语义漂移；(2) 直接利用冻结的视觉-语言预训练模型，结合针对性适配，大幅降低训练成本并保留强语言理解能力；(3) 混合相对-绝对位置编码（hybrid relative-absolute positional encodings）配合轻量内存机制提升时序定位能力；(4) 使用高斯平滑KL损失优化自回归框预测。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的大量实验表明，LVTrack取得了强性能（strong performance）。

Conclusion: LVTrack验证了通过门控机制调节跨阶段文本引导及复用冻结预训练模型的可行性，以较低训练成本实现了指代单目标跟踪的优异表现，为语言引导跟踪提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Referring single-object tracking enables language-grounded target initialization and subsequent tracking by jointly leveraging semantic cues and visual templates. The core difficulty is to use language differently across stages: it is indispensable for grounding but can induce semantic drift during tracking when overemphasized. Meanwhile, current methods often require costly vision-language alignment training. We present LVTrack, a pure transformer framework that introduces a mode-conditioned Gated Feature Injector to adaptively regulate textual guidance and alleviate semantic drift. Together with targeted adaptations, it directly harnesses a frozen vision-language pretrained model, greatly reducing training cost and preserving strong language understanding. To further improve temporal localization, LVTrack integrates hybrid relative-absolute positional encodings with a lightweight memory mechanism and optimizes autoregressive box prediction using a Gaussian-smoothed KL loss. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that LVTrack achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [61] [Chat-Edit-3D++: Interactive 3D and 4D Scene Editing via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29137)
*Shuangkang Fang,Yufeng Wang,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Wenrui Ding,Yi Yang,Shuchang Zhou,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CE3D++是一种基于对话的3D/4D场景编辑方法，通过Hash-Atlas网络将3D场景编辑重构为2D图集图像操作，实现2D编辑与3D重建工作流的解耦，并以大语言模型为核心支持任意文本输入和多轮对话，可自主调度多达30种视觉工具完成多样化的场景编辑任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言预训练模型的3D场景编辑方案存在两大缺陷：(1) 输入模式固定，缺乏文本输入的灵活性；(2) 编辑能力受限于单一或少数2D视觉模型，且需要复杂的管道设计来将这些模型集成到3D重建过程中，阻碍了其作为交互式设计工具的进一步发展。

Method: (1) 提出Hash-Atlas网络，将3D场景编辑重新表述为对2D图集图像的操作，实现2D编辑与3D重建过程的解耦；(2) 构建CE3D++，以大型语言模型（LLM）为核心，接受用户任意文本输入并解释意图，自主调用相应的视觉模型；(3) 扩展至单目4D场景，通过运动约束和轨迹数据集微调LLM，使其能够精确调度多达30种不同的视觉工具。

Result: 实验结果表明CE3D++能够有效集成多种视觉模型实现多样的视觉编辑效果，具备强大的场景理解能力和多轮对话能力。

Conclusion: CE3D++通过Hash-Atlas网络解耦2D编辑与3D重建流程，并以LLM为核心实现对话式3D/4D场景编辑，显著提升了编辑灵活性与工具调度能力，为3D场景编辑作为交互式设计工具的发展提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent work on image content manipulation based on vision-language pre-training models has been effectively extended to text-driven 3D scene editing. However, existing schemes for 3D scene editing still have certain shortcomings, hindering their further development as interactive design tools. Such schemes typically adhere to fixed input patterns, limiting flexibility in text input. Furthermore, their editing capabilities are constrained by a single or a few 2D visual models and require intricate pipeline design to integrate these models into 3D reconstruction processes. To address the aforementioned issues, we propose the Hash-Atlas network, which reformulates 3D scene editing as operations on 2D atlas images, thereby achieving a workflow decoupling of the 2D editing and 3D reconstruction processes. Building on this foundation, we introduce a dialogue-based 3D scene editing approach, termed CE3D++, which is centered on a large language model (LLM) that allows arbitrary textual input from users and interprets their intentions, subsequently facilitating the autonomous invocation of the corresponding visual models. Additionally, we extend CE3D++ to monocular 4D scenes by imposing motion constraints on moving objects and further fine-tuning the LLM by creating a trajectory dataset related to editing tasks, which enables the smaller LLM to schedule up to 30 different visual tools accurately. Experimental results demonstrate that CE3D++ effectively integrates multiple visual models to achieve diverse visual editing effects, possessing strong scene comprehension and multi-round dialog capabilities. The source codes and trained models are available at https://github.com/Fangkang515/CE3D.

</details>


### [62] [STARLINC: Satellite Trail Artifact Removal using Inter-Frame Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29145)
*Shingeon Kim,Hyeyoon Lee,Dain Kwon,Kanghyun Choi,Sunjong Park,Mi-Ryang Kim,Jeong-Eun Lee,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STARLINC，首个无需像素级标注的、基于机器学习的卫星轨迹去除框架。它通过合成轨迹生成训练数据、利用时间相邻曝光的帧间差分图突出瞬时轨迹、以及热图提供定位线索，实现像素级分割。在真实天文数据上的实验表明其显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道卫星（如Starlink）的快速扩张日益污染天文巡天图像。现有方法依赖人工目检，但现代巡天每晚产生TB级数据，人工筛查不可行。同时，通用域线条检测方法因领域不匹配（灰度高、稀疏亮星、低信噪比）无法泛化到天文图像，而从零训练新模型又缺乏大规模标注数据集。因此需要一种无需繁琐像素级标注的可靠自动化卫星轨迹去除方法。

Method: STARLINC包含三部分：(1) 合成卫星轨迹生成用于训练，避免人工标注需求；(2) 利用时间相邻曝光的帧间差分图突出瞬态轨迹，增强轨迹显著性；(3) 使用热图提供额外定位线索，辅助像素级分割任务。

Result: 在真实世界数据上的大量实验表明，STARLINC在卫星轨迹去除任务上显著优于基线方法，证明了其作为下一代天文巡天可扩展解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: STARLINC是首个无需像素级标注的ML卫星轨迹去除框架，通过合成数据生成、帧间差分和热图定位相结合，解决了天文图像中卫星轨迹去除的领域鸿沟和数据稀缺问题，为大规模天文巡天提供了可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of low Earth orbit satellites such as Starlink is increasingly contaminating astronomical surveys. In practice, contaminated images are often identified through inspection. However, modern surveys generate terabytes of data each night, making manual screening infeasible and necessitating reliable automated methods for satellite trail removal. Unfortunately, existing general-domain line detection methods fail to generalize to astronomical images due to domain mismatch, which are mostly grayscale with sparse bright stars and have a low signal-to-noise ratio. Moreover, training new models from scratch is impractical due to the lack of large-scale annotated astronomical datasets. To address these challenges, we introduce STARLINC, the first ML-based framework for satellite trail removal without requiring tedious pixel-level annotation of astronomical images. STARLINC combines synthetic satellite trail generation for training, inter-frame differential maps from temporally adjacent exposures to highlight transient trails, and heatmaps to provide additional localization cues for pixel-level segmentation. Extensive experiments on real-world data demonstrate substantial improvements over baselines, establishing STARLINC as a scalable solution for next-generation astronomical surveys. Code is available at https://github.com/starioKim/STARLINC.

</details>


### [63] [SGRNet: Spatially Guided Radiology Network for Structured Radiological Reporting of Head and Neck Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29153)
*Ayush Gupta,Vinkle Srivastav,Prateek Upadhya,Amit Gupta,Krithika Rangarajan,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SGRNet（空间引导放射学网络），将头颈癌CT报告的自动生成重构为解剖学锚定的多标签结构化报告任务，通过引入器官分割图和基于3D高斯热图的肿瘤定位图两种低成本空间先验，并经空间特征调制动态整合，在184对多中心头颈癌CECT数据上实现mAP 0.60，较强3D体素基线提升8.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化放射学报告生成可减轻临床负担并消除观察者间差异，但标准自由文本生成模型在密集区域存在幻觉风险，且在数据稀缺场景下表现不佳。头颈癌（HNC）CECT成像缺乏代谢影像（如PET）信息，造成视觉信息缺口，亟需在保证事实安全性前提下提升结构化报告精度。

Method: SGRNet将报告生成重构为解剖学锚定的多标签结构化报告任务，预测层级化临床模式下的局部肿瘤侵犯情况。网络引入两种低成本空间先验：自动化器官分割图与基于3D高斯热图的弱监督肿瘤定位图，并通过空间特征调制机制动态整合这些先验，引导网络关注肿瘤诱导的细微结构改变。

Result: 在多中心184对HNC CECT体数据及报告上，针对5个临床重要且密集排列的解剖亚区，SGRNet取得mAP 0.60，较仅用体素信息的强3D基线实现8.8个百分点的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 实证表明，将报告生成转化为解剖学引导的结构化预测任务，并引入低成本空间先验进行特征调制，能够有效弥补代谢影像信息缺失，提升头颈癌结构化放射学报告的准确性与临床安全性。

Abstract: Automated radiological report generation can alleviate clinical workloads and eliminate observer variability. However, standard free-text generation models pose hallucination risks in dense regions and fail under data scarcity. We address these challenges in Head and Neck Cancer (HNC) from contrast-enhanced CT (CECT) imaging. To enforce factual safety, we reformulate report generation as an anatomically grounded, multi-label, structured reporting task, predicting localized tumor involvement across a hierarchical clinical schema. To bridge the visual gap from missing metabolic imaging (e.g., PET), we introduce SGRNet (Spatially Guided Radiology Network), incorporating two low-cost spatial priors: automated organ segmentations and weakly supervised tumor localization maps modeled via 3D Gaussian heatmaps. These priors are dynamically integrated via spatial feature modulation to guide the network toward subtle tumor-induced structural alterations. Evaluated on a multi-centric dataset of 184 paired HNC CECT volumes and reports, on five clinically salient, densely packed anatomical subsites, SGRNet achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 0.60, an 8.8 percentage-point absolute improvement over strong volume-only 3D baselines.

</details>


### [64] [Training-Free Hidden-State Refinement for Flow-Matching Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29160)
*Yuanyi Yan,Xinzhe Rao,Canyu Shen,Yang Chen,Yunlu Chen,Meng Tang,Teng Long,Vincent Tao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练-free的循环（looping）框架，在冻结的flow-matching图像生成器的去噪器内部注入额外推理计算，无需改变模型权重或外层采样器。通过选择性地重复应用transformer层（Dense/Sparse Token Loop）、按采样进度控制循环时机（Sampling-Progress Gating），以及用Loop Guidance结合普通与循环的矢量场预测，在多个Scale-RAE模型上显著提升质量指标（如DiT2.4B上GenEval从0.4471提升至0.5691）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有flow-matching图像生成器在测试时增加计算量通常依赖增加采样步数，这会重复评估整个去噪器，导致质量增益与采样器成本强耦合。作者希望在不改变模型权重和外层采样器的前提下，利用去噪器内部（frozen transformer）的额外推理计算来提升生成质量，核心挑战在于决定哪些token、层和采样时刻应接受重复更新。

Method: 提出训练-free的循环框架：在每次去噪调用内重复应用选定的transformer层。具体包括：(1) Dense and Sparse Token Loop控制token作用范围；(2) Sampling-Progress Gating和loop layer range指定循环激活的时间和层位置；(3) loop count和strength控制重复更新的程度；(4) Loop Guidance融合普通与循环矢量场的预测结果。

Result: 在两种Scale-RAE模型规模上，循环变体在主要和辅助质量指标上均有提升，且质量-效率权衡具有竞争力。Loop Guidance在所有三个测试模型上提升两类主要指标；在Scale-RAE DiT2.4B上，GenEval从0.4471提升至0.5691，DPG-Bench从0.7656提升至0.8053。

Conclusion: 训练-free的循环框架是一种有效的测试时计算扩展方法，可在冻结生成器内部灵活分配额外计算，显著提升生成质量且不改变模型权重或外层采样流程，代码将开源。

Abstract: We aim to improve frozen flow-matching image generators by adding inference computation inside the denoiser, without changing model weights or the outer sampler. Existing generators usually spend extra test-time computation by increasing the number of sampling steps, which repeatedly evaluates the entire denoiser and couples quality gains to sampler cost. A key challenge is how to use extra computation inside a frozen transformer denoiser: the method must decide which tokens, layers, and sampling times receive repeated updates while preserving the original generation pipeline. We introduce a training-free looping framework that repeatedly applies selected transformer layers inside each denoising call. Dense and Sparse Token Loop vary the token scope; Sampling-Progress Gating and the loop layer range specify when and where looping is active; loop count and strength control the repeated updates; and Loop Guidance combines ordinary and looped vector-field predictions. Across two Scale-RAE model scales, loop variants improve primary and auxiliary quality metrics with competitive quality--efficiency trade-offs. Loop Guidance further improves both primary metrics across all three tested models; on Scale-RAE DiT2.4B, it raises GenEval from 0.4471 to 0.5691 and DPG-Bench from 0.7656 to 0.8053. Code will be released.

</details>


### [65] [Subtraction-Based Tumor Segmentation and Lesion-Centered pCR Prediction for the MAMA-MIA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29162)
*Kai Geissler,Raphael Schäfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 团队FME在MAMA-MIA挑战赛中使用五折残差编码器nnU-Net集成进行肿瘤分割，以及25个3D视频分类器集成进行pCR预测，在两个任务中均排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在外部多国队列上，从治疗前动态对比增强乳腺MRI进行原发肿瘤分割和病理完全缓解（pCR）预测的性能，以探索跨站点域偏移下的鲁棒方法。

Method: 分割任务使用仅含首期增强减去除影像的五折残差编码器nnU-Net集成，结合镜像测试时增强和最大连通域滤波；pCR预测集成25个在病灶中心裁剪的影像上预训练的3D视频分类器，输入为增强前及前两个增强后容积。

Result: 分割任务综合性能-公平性评分为0.882，Dice为0.713，归一化Hausdorff距离为0.099；pCR预测综合评分为0.664，平衡准确率为0.541，等优势差异为0.212；两项任务均排名第二。

Conclusion: 基于减影像的输入和集成方法有助于在跨站点域偏移下实现鲁棒肿瘤分割，但仅使用基线DCE-MRI进行pCR预测的能力仍然有限。

Abstract: We describe the submission of team FME to the MAMA-MIA Challenge, which evaluated primary tumor segmentation and prediction of pathological complete response (pCR) from pretreatment dynamic contrast-enhanced breast MRI on an external multi-country cohort. For segmentation, we trained a five-fold residual-encoder nnU-Net ensemble using only the first post-contrast minus pre-contrast image, combined with mirroring test-time augmentation and largest-connected-component filtering. For pCR prediction, we ensembled 25 pretrained 3D video classifiers trained on lesion-centred crops from the pre-contrast and first two post-contrast volumes. FME ranked second in both tasks. The segmentation method achieved a combined performance-fairness score of 0.882, with Dice 0.713 and normalized Hausdorff distance 0.099. The pCR method achieved a combined score of 0.664, balanced accuracy of 0.541, and equalized-odds disparity of 0.212. The results indicate that subtraction-based input and ensembling support robust tumor segmentation under cross-site domain shift, whereas pCR prediction from baseline DCE-MRI alone remains limited.
  For the submission repository, see https://github.com/FraunhoferMEVIS/MAMA-MIA-Challenge-FME

</details>


### [66] [Mapping-Based Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29164)
*Freddie Åström,Michael Felsberg,George Baravdish*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于张量的新型变分泛函，用于目标导向的图像增强和去噪。通过显式正则化，该公式融合了应用依赖的上下文信息与去噪问题的先验知识。作者证明了最小值解的存在性，并给出了张量对称性约束、凸性和几何解释的结果。该框架在涉及非线性函数的应用中（如伽马校正和定向值域滤波）表现优异，同时在通用去噪性能上与基于PDE的最先进方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中很少有变分模型能够同时描述应用依赖的信息和去噪问题的上下文知识。传统的去噪方法通常只针对噪声本身建模，忽略了特定应用场景下的目标信息；而应用特定的方法又往往缺乏严格的数学理论保障。作者旨在弥合这一空白，通过张量框架将应用导向信息与上下文约束统一在一个可证明的变分框架中。

Method: 作者提出一个基于张量的新型变分泛函，通过显式正则化项将应用依赖和上下文信息以第一性原理（first principles）的方式引入公式。具体包括：1) 利用张量结构编码应用相关的非线性信息；2) 通过正则化项约束解的空间以保证适定性；3) 证明了最小值解的存在性；4) 分析张量对称性约束、凸性等数学性质；5) 提供该泛函的几何解释。

Result: 该方法在包含非线性函数的应用中表现出色，例如伽马校正和定向值域滤波。在通用图像去噪方面，实验结果与基于PDE的最先进方法相当，验证了该框架的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 本文成功展示了如何在一个统一的张量变分框架中整合应用依赖的背景知识与去噪的先验知识，填补了该领域的研究空白。该框架不仅在特定非线性应用中具有优势，在通用去噪上也达到了最先进水平，为后续研究提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a novel tensor-based functional for targeted image enhancement and denoising. Via explicit regularization, our formulation incorporates application dependent and contextual information using first principles. Few works in literature treat variational models that describe both application dependent information and contextual knowledge of the denoising problem. We prove the existence of a minimizer and present results on tensor symmetry constraints, convexity, and geometric interpretation of the proposed functional. We show that our framework excels in applications where nonlinear functions are present such as in gamma correction and targeted value range filtering. We also study general denoising performance where we show comparable results to dedicated PDE-based state of the art methods.

</details>


### [67] [Feature-Spectral Fragility in Segmentation: Dataset Dependence, Architecture-Specific Localization, and Spectral Correlates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29167)
*Subhash Kashyap*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究分割模型内部特征表示的频谱依赖性与鲁棒性，通过三种架构（CNN、SSM、Transformer）在两个数据集上施加特征域低通滤波干预。结果显示数据集间差异显著（CVC上Dice下降100%/73.2%/30.9%，ISIC上仅9.4%/10.3%/0.6%），敏感性定位在架构特定的深度，且Fourier增强不能改善特征域退化，表明特征频谱鲁棒性与输入域频谱鲁棒性本质不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割模型鲁棒性评估主要聚焦于输入域扰动，而对学习到的特征表示内部频率内容依赖性的理解仍然不足，该缺口限制了我们对模型泛化和脆弱性机制的深入认识。

Method: 对ResNet50-UNet（CNN）、VM-UNet（SSM）和Swin-UNETR（Transformer）三种架构实施针对性的训练后低通干预，在CVC-ClinicDB和ISIC2018数据集上于内部特征表示施加干预，采用保留的测试集进行主要评估，并配合单阶段干预定位敏感层及原生特征域频谱测量。

Result: 在截止频率rho=0.25时，CVC上CNN、SSM、Transformer的Dice分别下降100%、73.2%和30.9%，而ISIC上仅分别下降9.4%、10.3%和0.6%；跨数据集差异在统计上显著。敏感性集中在架构特定深度：CNN在中后期编码器块达到峰值，SSM在早期编码器阶段。原生特征域测量显示CVC上高频能量与脆弱性呈负相关，但ISIC上仅为部分相关。Fourier增强可改善输入域低通滤波鲁棒性，但对特征域退化基本无效。

Conclusion: 特征频谱鲁棒性具有强数据集依赖性、架构特异性和输入域频谱鲁棒性解耦的特征，高频能量与脆弱性的关联仅是候选相关因素而非被证实的机制。

Abstract: Robustness of segmentation models is commonly assessed through input-domain perturbations, while dependence on frequency content within learned feature representations remains less understood. We probe this dependence using targeted post-training low-pass interventions on internal representations of three segmentation architectures, ResNet50-UNet (CNN), VM-UNet (SSM), and Swin-UNETR (Transformer), across CVC-ClinicDB and ISIC2018, with headline evaluations performed on untouched held-out test sets. At cutoff rho=0.25, feature-domain low-pass filtering causes severe degradation on CVC: Dice drops by 100%, 73.2%, and 30.9% for CNN, SSM, and Transformer, respectively, compared with 9.4%, 10.3%, and 0.6% on ISIC. The cross-dataset difference is statistically significant for every architecture. Single-stage interventions further show that sensitivity is localized at architecture-specific depths: the CNN peaks at a mid/late encoder block, whereas the SSM peaks in an early encoder stage on both datasets. Native feature-domain spectral measurements show an inverse association between high-frequency energy and fragility on CVC; the relationship is only partial on ISIC and is therefore treated as a candidate correlate rather than a proven mechanism. Finally, Fourier augmentation improves robustness to input-space low-pass filtering but leaves feature-domain degradation essentially unchanged. These results show that feature-spectral robustness is strongly dataset-dependent, architecture-specific, and distinct from input-domain spectral robustness.

</details>
