*Models Luc Debaupte,Tyler Baumgartner,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了VocalAffectBench，一个公开的、仅测试用途的基准，用于评估AI音频模型从原始音频中识别语音情感的能力。基准包含273条英语WAV片段（1.95小时，7类情感标签），六个发布基线的平均准确率仅35.5%，最强基线gemini_3_5_flash达到46.5%（随机基线为14.3%）。二次效价分析（积极/中性/消极）聚合准确率为50.9%，情感识别性能在各类别间极不均衡，中性情感召回率最高（75.6%），而惊讶（10.7%）和恐惧（15.4%）的召回率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音产品越来越需要语音中存在但转录文本中缺失的情感线索。然而，目前缺乏公开基准来评估AI音频模型是否能够仅从原始音频中识别表达性语音情感，特别是离散的、非中性的情感类别，这些类别在语音代理工作流中往往最为关键。

Method: 构建VocalAffectBench，一个公共的仅测试基准：包含273条人类录制的英语WAV片段，来自51个说话人账户，总时长1.95小时，覆盖七类情感标签（愤怒、厌恶、恐惧、快乐、中性、悲伤、惊讶），每类39条。所有基线仅从音频进行评估，不使用转录文本或上下文元数据。同时进行二级效价桶分析，将标签映射为积极、中性和消极三类（排除惊讶）。

Result: 六个发布基线的平均准确率为35.5%，最强基线gemini_3_5_flash在七分类任务上达到46.5%（远高于14.3%的随机基线，但远未达到稳健的情感识别水平）。效价桶分析（粗粒度）聚合准确率为50.9%。各类别性能极不均衡：中性召回率最高（75.6%），而惊讶仅10.7%、恐惧仅15.4%。

Conclusion: 发现显示当前评估的基线模型能从语音中提取部分情感信号，但离散的表达性情感识别仍然脆弱，尤其是对于语音代理工作流中最重要的非中性情感类别。这揭示了现有音频AI模型在语音情感理解方面仍存在显著不足，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Voice products increasingly need affective cues that are present in speech but absent from transcripts. We introduce VocalAffectBench, a public, test-only benchmark for evaluating whether AI audio models can identify expressed vocal emotion from raw audio. The benchmark contains 273 human-recorded English WAV clips from 51 speaker accounts totaling 1.95 hours across seven labels: angry, disgusted, fearful, happy, neutral, sad, and surprised, with 39 clips per class. All baselines are evaluated from audio alone, without transcripts or contextual metadata. Across six released baselines, average accuracy is 35.5%. The strongest baseline, gemini_3_5_flash, reaches 46.5% on the seven-way task, above the 14.3% random baseline but far from robust emotion recognition. A secondary valence-bucket analysis maps labels into positive, neutral, and negative classes, excluding surprised because its valence is ambiguous. Aggregate accuracy under this coarser view is 50.9%. Performance is highly uneven across classes. By recall, neutral is identified most reliably at 75.6% averaged across baselines, while surprised and fearful reach only 10.7% and 15.4%, respectively. These results show that the evaluated baselines can extract some affective signal from speech, but discrete expressed-emotion recognition remains fragile, especially for non-neutral emotions that are often most important in voice agent workflows.

</details>


### [103] [CoVA-SFT: A Large-Scale Dataset for Chain of Visual Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28958)
*Tsung-Han Wu,Heekyung Lee,Anya Ji,Haoming Chen,Trevor Darrell,Joseph E. Gonzalez,David M. Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVA-SFT（51.9K样本、222K多模态推理步骤、5种布局家族、17个复杂任务）和配套基准CoVA-Bench（1,700个测试样本），用于教会多模态语言模型在纯文本推理问题中构建和维护内部视觉工作空间。微调模型在CoVA-Bench上比所有交错CoT基线平均提升2倍以上，但仍弱于强文本CoT基线，揭示了未来工作的开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的思维链（CoT）推理在文本任务上表现优异，但纯文本CoT迫使模型将视觉问题序列化为笨拙的散文描述。虽然已有处理视觉输入的结构性方案，但社区缺乏一个大规模、多步骤、自校正的数据集来教会模型在解决纯文本推理问题时构建和维护内部视觉工作空间。

Method: 构建CoVA-SFT数据集（51.9K样本、222K+多模态推理步骤、5种布局家族、17个复杂任务），包含显式推理公式（rationale formulations）、代理式渲染（agentic renderings）和验证循环（verification loops），教授多模态语言模型交错使用文本与视觉抽象。同时构建CoVA-Bench基准（1,700个留出测试样本）用于可复现评估。

Result: 在CoVA-SFT上微调的模型在CoVA-Bench上比所有交错CoT基线平均提升超过2倍，但表现仍不及强文本CoT基线，表明该领域仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: CoVA-SFT成功教会多模态模型在纯文本推理中交错使用文本与视觉抽象，显著优于交错CoT基线，但弱于文本CoT基线，这说明视觉工作空间的构建与利用仍有待进一步研究，为未来工作指明了方向。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has dramatically improved large language models (LLMs) by allowing them to decompose problems into intermediate steps. While CoT is widely effective for linguistic tasks, text-only CoT forces models to serialize visual problems into awkward prose. Although architectural solutions exist to process visual inputs, the community lacks a massive, multi-step, self-corrected dataset to teach models how to build and maintain internal visual workspaces when solving purely textual reasoning problems. To address this limitation, we introduce CoVA-SFT, a highly structured corpus of 51.9K samples containing over 222K multimodal reasoning steps across 5 distinct layout families and 17 complex tasks, and CoVA-Bench, a companion benchmark of 1,700 held-out test samples spanning the same tasks for reproducible evaluation. By providing explicit rationale formulations, agentic renderings, and verification loops, CoVA-SFT teaches multimodal language models to interleave text and visual abstractions. We validate the dataset by demonstrating that models fine-tuned on CoVA-SFT outperform all interleaved CoT baselines by more than 2x on average on CoVA-Bench, though they still fall short of strong text-only CoT baselines, highlighting open challenges for future work.

</details>


### [104] [The Illusion of Replacement: Rethinking Specialized Machine Learning Models in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28980)
*Kiyan Rezaee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过综述159篇论文（2016-2026年）覆盖九种模态，系统考察了语言模型能否替代为结构化数据构建的专用架构。结果发现语言介导模型在特定场景（极端少样本、离散符号任务、文本标注知识图谱、大规模单模态预训练）表现出竞争力，但每当直接评估结构表示或计算而非仅看准确率时，没有证据表明通用架构替代成立。结构性专业化更多是"迁移"而非"消失"，且缩放能否消除与结构感知架构的差距仍未得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来语言模型在各领域展现出强大能力，一个根本性问题随之而来：为结构化数据专门设计的机器学习架构是否可能被通用语言模型完全取代？本文旨在通过大规模综述回答这一问题，并特别强调不应仅以预测准确率作为评判标准，还需考虑结构表示与计算能力，以避免对语言模型替代性产生过度乐观的结论。

Method: 作者对2016至2026年间发表的159篇论文进行综述，覆盖九种模态。首先区分"执行任务"与"保留并计算使任务可解的结构"两个层面；其次将现有方法归类为八种表示范式，从纯语言系统到完全专用架构逐级排列；最后从预测准确率、结构表示和结构计算三个维度系统评估各范式表现。

Result: 语言介导模型在极端少样本预测、离散符号任务、文本标注知识图谱和大规模单模态预训练等特定场景中具有高度竞争力。但在直接评估结构表示或计算时，没有任何证据表明语言模型能够实现通用的架构替代。跨独立研究社区反复出现同一模式：当语言不足时，研究者会通过图模块、结构token、专用注意力或其他非语言组件重新引入缺失的结构。

Conclusion: 专业化从语言模型视角看并未被消除，而是发生了"迁移"——结构需求通过不同形式的非语言组件重新嵌入系统中。缩放虽然能提升语言模型性能，但语言模型能否最终弥合与结构感知架构之间的差距仍是一个未经验证的开放问题。这提示研究者应谨慎对待"语言模型全面替代专用架构"的叙事。

Abstract: Can the specialized architectures that machine learning has traditionally built for structured data be replaced by language-based models? This question is examined through a review of 159 papers (2016--2026) across nine modalities, with predictive accuracy considered alongside structural representation and computation. A distinction is made between performing a task and preserving and computing the structure that makes the task tractable, and existing approaches are organized into eight representational regimes, ranging from language-only systems to fully specialized architectures. Language-mediated models are found to be highly competitive in specific settings, including extreme few-shot prediction, discretized symbolic tasks, textually annotated knowledge graphs, and large-scale single-modality pretraining. However, whenever structural representation or computation is directly evaluated rather than accuracy alone, no evidence of general architectural replacement is found. Instead, a recurring pattern is observed across independent research communities: when language alone is insufficient, the missing structure is reintroduced through a graph module, structural tokens, specialized attention, or another non-linguistic component. In this sense, specialization more often relocates than disappears. Moreover, although performance of language-based models is improved by scaling, whether the gap to a structure-aware architecture can eventually be eliminated remains untested.

</details>


### [105] [Detecting and Guiding LLM-Generated Korean Poetry with Interpretable Form-level Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28986)
*Keunhyeung Park,Seunguk Yu,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM在现代韩语诗歌生成中常产出"断行散文"式低质文本的问题，提出了两个耦合任务：(1)检测诗歌是否为人类或LLM所写；(2)引导LLM生成更接近人类写作形式的诗歌。作者从四个形式语言学维度（篇幅、结构变化、节奏不规则性、规范性遵循）提炼出五个可解释特征。在检测任务中，基于这五个特征的逻辑回归分类器在7个未见LLM上的零样本OOD检测中达到83.60的AUC-ROC，相比最强基线KatFishNet的75.84提升了7.76个AUC点（相对提升10.23%）。在生成任务中，专家评估显示GPT-5.2上特征引导的诗歌优于无约束基线，GPT-5.2和Gemini-3上的定向长度、节奏和结尾统计均向人类分布靠拢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在现代韩语诗歌创作中存在显著的形式缺陷（如"断行散文"问题），缺乏系统性的形式维度量化方法来诊断和改善LLM诗歌生成质量，同时人类与LLM诗歌的检测也是一个挑战。

Method: 提出四个形式级语言学维度（Volume、Structure Variation、Rhythmic Irregularity、Normative Adherence），操作化为五个可解释特征。检测任务采用基于这些特征的逻辑回归分类器进行零样本OOD检测；生成任务通过特征引导LLM输出，经专家与统计评估验证。

Result: 检测方面：逻辑回归分类器在7个未见LLM上零样本检测平均AUC-ROC达83.60，相比最强基线KatFishNet（75.84）提升7.76个AUC点（相对提升10.23%）；仍有一个生成器特有标点模式属于分类体系外边界案例。生成方面：GPT-5.2上专家评估偏好特征引导诗歌，GPT-5.2和Gemini-3的定向长度、节奏和结尾统计趋近人类分布。

Conclusion: 可解释的、语言特异性特征能够有效弥合LLM诗歌的诊断与引导之间的鸿沟，为LLM生成诗歌的形式质量提升提供了可行路径。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle with modern Korean poetry, producing outputs that resemble "line-broken prose." We address two coupled tasks: detecting whether a Korean poem is human- or LLM-authored, and guiding LLMs to generate poetry closer in form to human writing. We quantify the human-LLM gap along four form-level linguistic dimensions: output length (Volume), the diversity and connective use of line-final forms (Structure Variation), the irregularity of line lengths (Rhythmic Irregularity), and adherence to standard orthography (Normative Adherence). We operationalize these dimensions as five interpretable features. For detection, a logistic regression classifier over these five features attains an average AUC-ROC of 83.60 in zero-shot out-of-distribution detection across seven unseen LLMs, versus 75.84 for the strongest baseline in our comparison, KatFishNet, an absolute gain of 7.76 AUC points and a 10.23% relative improvement; one generator-specific punctuation pattern outside our taxonomy remains a boundary case. For generation, expert evaluation on GPT-5.2 prefers feature-guided poems over the unconstrained baseline, and analyses across GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3 show that targeted length, rhythm, and ending statistics move toward the human distribution. These results suggest that interpretable, language-specific features can bridge the diagnosis and guidance of LLM-generated poetry.

</details>


### [106] [A Unifying Perspective on Language Model Representations: From Filler-Role Structure to Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29034)
*Zhang Enyan,R. Thomas McCoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用张量积表示（TPR）作为语言模型可解释性的统一假设，从数学和实证上证明TPR可以统一多种先前的可解释性方法（加性类比、线性探针、稀疏自编码器和激活修补），并展示这些方法均可从TPR推导而来，且在多种模型上构建的变体与标准变体性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型可解释性方法众多，各自产生的洞见相对孤立，缺乏一个统一的底层理论框架来解释为什么这些不同的可解释性方法都能有效工作。作者希望探索是否存在一种潜在的结构，能够同时解释各种不同的可解释性发现。

Method: 将TPR（张量积表示）作为统一假设，其核心思想是将组合结构表示为向量空间中的填充物-角色绑定（filler-role bindings）。作者首先在数学上从TPR推导出加性类比、线性探针、稀疏自编码器和激活修补这四种可解释性方法；然后在实证上将该推导应用于从玩具模型到LLM的多种模型，构建上述每种方法的变体，并与标准变体进行性能比较。

Result: 数学上证明了四种可解释性方法均可从TPR推导而来；实证上，基于TPR构造的各可解释性方法变体在不同规模的模型上表现与标准变体相当。

Conclusion: TPR为语言模型可解释性提供了一个统一的理论框架，能够解释多种可解释性方法之间的内在联系。这项工作被视为迈向可解释性理想目标的一步——即对神经网络本质的统一解释，不仅由个别观察佐证，还由它们之间联系的说明来佐证。

Abstract: A wide range of methods have been proposed for interpreting language models, delivering important insights into their inner workings. However, different methods and their resulting insights stand in relative isolation: what could the underlying structure of language models be, such that they give rise to all our interpretations? In this work, we propose using Tensor Product Representations (TPRs) as a unifying hypothesis. TPRs give a concrete proposal for how compositional structure could be represented in vector space --- as filler-role bindings. We show, both mathematically and empirically, that TPRs can unify several prior interpretability methods: additive analogies, linear probing, sparse autoencoders, and activation patching. Mathematically, we show that these methods can all be derived from TPRs. Empirically, we apply the derivations to a range of different models --- from small toy models to LLMs --- to construct instances of each of the above interpretability methods; these constructed variants perform comparably to their standard variants. We view this work as a step toward what interpretability will ideally provide: a unified account of the nature of neural networks, corroborated not just by individual observations but also by an explanation of the connections between them.

</details>


### [107] [RouteSparse: Input-Conditional Pattern Routing for Budgeted Long-Context Prefilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29058)
*Chao Zhang,Yifan Ji,Ziyan Zhang,Kai Song,Fei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RouteSparse提出一种输入自适应的动态稀疏注意力路由方法，为每个注意力头和提示片段从一个小型GPU高效稀疏模式库中选择最优模式和稀疏预算，通过低成本探针估计模式效用与不确定性，并采用延迟感知路由器和密集回退机制来平衡质量与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态稀疏注意力方法（如MInference）为每个注意力头离线固定一种稀疏模式，假设该模式及其稀疏预算在所有输入上始终适用。但这种静态假设在多样化的输入下会导致质量下降或效率损失，需要一种能根据输入动态调整稀疏策略的方法。

Method: RouteSparse的核心方法包括：(1) 构建一个小型GPU高效稀疏模式库；(2) 使用低成本探针估计每种模式在给定头和提示片段上的效用与不确定性；(3) 设计延迟感知路由器，在模式之间进行选择并确定稀疏预算；(4) 对不确定情况回退到更密集的掩码；(5) 将路由问题形式化为约束风险最小化，并从被省略的概率质量推导注意力输出误差的数学证书。

Result: 在Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct模型上使用128K token提示进行评估：RouteSparse达到6.5倍稠密预填充加速，RULER分数仅下降0.2点；相比之下，固定每头路由达到7.3倍加速但RULER下降1.6点。消融实验证实输入条件路由、硬件剖析和选择性密集回退各自对质量-延迟权衡有贡献。

Conclusion: RouteSparse通过输入自适应的稀疏模式路由，在不改变模型权重的前提下显著改善长上下文预填充的效率-质量权衡，其理论误差证书和实证结果共同表明该方法在长上下文检索、问答、摘要和语言建模任务上均优于固定模式方案。

Abstract: Dynamic sparse attention can reduce the quadratic cost of long-context prefilling without changing model weights. MInference assigns each attention head one pattern offline and estimates that pattern's sparse indices for every prompt. This design is efficient, but it assumes that a head's preferred pattern and sparsity budget remain suitable across inputs. We introduce RouteSparse, which routes each head and prompt segment among a small library of GPU-efficient sparse patterns. A low-cost probe estimates pattern utility and uncertainty; a latency-aware router then selects a pattern and budget, while uncertain cases fall back to a denser mask. We formulate routing as constrained risk minimization, derive an attention-output error certificate from omitted probability mass, and evaluate the method on long-context retrieval, question answering, summarization, and language modeling. On Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct with 128K-token prompts, RouteSparse achieves $6.5\times$ dense prefill speed with a 0.2-point RULER drop relative to dense attention, compared with $7.3\times$ speed and a 1.6-point drop for fixed per-head routing. Ablations confirm that input-conditional routing, hardware profiling, and selective dense fallback each contribute to the quality--latency tradeoff.

</details>


### [108] [Not All or None: Dynamic Construction of Target-aware Memory Graph for Conversational Stance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29066)
*Yifan Xiang,Bin Liang,Yuqi Huang,Ruifeng Xu,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TamGraph（目标感知记忆图），一种用于对话立场检测的新方法。它通过逐步熵引导回溯机制从历史对话中选择性激活记忆，动态构建目标感知图来建模话语间的立场关系，在英文和中文基准上显著提升了LLM的对话立场检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话立场检测是社交媒体场景中更具挑战性的任务，需要利用跨对话会话中与目标相关的历史陈述来检测用户立场。现有方法要么考虑所有前置历史对话（引入噪声），要么完全不使用历史对话信息（丢失关键线索），缺乏一种能够动态选择性地利用目标相关信息的方法。

Method: TamGraph采用逐步、熵引导的回溯机制，从历史对话中选择性激活记忆；动态构建目标感知图（target-aware graph）来建模话语间的立场关系，从而在利用对话历史中目标相关信息的同时防止噪声引入。

Result: 在英文和中文基准数据集上的实验结果表明，TamGraph显著提升了LLM在对话立场检测任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 通过选择性记忆激活和目标感知图建模，TamGraph有效解决了对话立场检测中历史信息利用与噪声控制之间的平衡问题，证明了动态选择性利用目标相关历史信息对提升对话立场检测性能的有效性。

Abstract: Stance detection is crucial for understanding the underlying attitude of an expression towards a target. Conversational stance detection is a more challenging stance detection task in real-world social media scenarios, as it involves detecting the user's stance by leveraging the target-related historical statements across conversational sessions. In this paper, we propose target-aware Memory Graph TamGraph, a novel method that dynamically leverages target-related statements for conversational stance detection. Instead of considering all preceding historical conversations or using no prior conversation information for stance detection, our TamGraph employs a stepwise, entropy-guided backtracking mechanism to selectively activate memory from historical conversations and dynamically constructs a target-aware graph to model the stance relations among utterances. This allows the exploitation of target-related information from the conversation history for stance detection while preventing the introduction of noise. Experimental results on both English and Chinese benchmarks demonstrate that our TamGraph substantially improves LLM performance on conversational stance detection.

</details>


### [109] [Recognition-Refusal Misalignment in LLMs: Why Models Answer Structurally Unanswerable Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29109)
*Yucheng Du,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型常常对结构上不可回答的问题（如计算cot(-540°)或评估(1).startswith("1")）给出自信的错误答案而非弃权。研究表明，模型在隐藏状态中存在一个线性方向能区分可回答与结构上不可能的问题，表明模型在生成前就已表征了不可能性。然而该识别方向与安全拒答方向几乎正交，说明模型的失败源于路由缺陷而非编码缺陷——模型拥有"无可接受答案"信号，但安全拒答通路未与之对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM对结构上不可回答的问题自信作答而非弃权这一失败现象的根本原因，即这种失败是源于模型未能识别问题不可回答（识别缺陷），还是模型虽识别了但未能将该识别结果路由到弃权行为（路由缺陷）。

Method: 在1.7B到70B参数的指令微调模型上，通过线性探针寻找隐藏状态中区分可回答与结构不可能问题的线性方向（识别方向）；将该方向与安全拒答方向、行为定义的无效感知方向进行余弦相似度对比；在生成时沿识别方向进行引导（steering），观察对数学和代码问题上无效感知行为的双向、剂量依赖性改变；并通过基础模型与指令微调模型对比检查预训练终点的几何结构。

Result: 模型在隐藏状态中存在单一线性方向能区分可回答与结构不可能的问题；该识别方向与安全拒答方向几乎正交；行为定义的无效感知方向更接近识别方向但仅部分对齐，且与安全拒答方向仍近正交；沿识别方向引导能双向且剂量依赖地改变无效感知行为，随机方向无效；基础/指令微调对比表明低余弦几何在预训练终点已存在。

Conclusion: 模型对不可回答问题的自信失败更适合解释为路由失败而非编码失败：模型已具备可用的"无可接受答案"信号，但安全拒答通路未与之对齐，导致识别结果未能正确转化为弃权行为。

Abstract: Large language models often answer structurally unanswerable questions, such as computing cot(-540°) or evaluating (1).startswith("1"), instead of abstaining. We ask whether this failure reflects missing recognition or failed routing from recognition to abstention. Across instruction-tuned models from 1.7B to 70B parameters, a single linear direction in the hidden state separates answerable from structurally impossible math and code prompts, showing that models represent impossibility before generation. Yet this recognition direction is nearly orthogonal to the canonical safety-refusal direction that mediates trained harmful-content refusal. An in-domain behavior-defined invalidity-aware direction is closer to recognition, but only partially aligned with it, and remains near-orthogonal to safety refusal. Generation-time steering along the recognition direction changes invalidity-aware behavior bidirectionally and dose-responsively on structural math and code cells, while random directions do not. Base/instruct comparisons further show that the low-cosine geometry is already present at the pretraining endpoint. The confident-on-impossible failure is therefore better explained as a routing failure than as an encoding failure: the model has a usable "no admissible answer" signal, but the safety-refusal pathway is not aligned to use it.

</details>


### [110] [HEAR Who Said What: Unlocking Speaker-Attributed Reasoning via Counterfactual Voice Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29120)
*Dongwook Lee,Sangkwon Park,Eunwoo Song,Che Hyun Lee,Youngho Cho,Junho Kim,June Young Yi,Heeseung Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HEAR基准（2.4K人工验证样本，来自887个多说话人音频片段），用于诊断语音语言模型（SLMs）的说话人归属推理能力。评估20个主流SLM后发现它们在此类基础任务上表现不佳，常依赖语义先验而非真实声学线索。为此提出A2R模型，通过在Counterfactual Audio with Speaker-level Hard negatives (CASH)数据集上优化，引导模型优先关注声学语音线索，在HEAR上取得强性能并展现出对多样多说话人下游任务的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型（SLMs）在多说话人环境中部署日益增多，但其能否准确归属语音来源并基于说话人身份进行推理仍不明确。现有研究缺乏对SLMs说话人归属推理基础能力的系统性诊断，因此需要构建专门基准来评估和提升这一能力。

Method: 1) 构建HEAR基准：概念上分层（hierarchical）的说话人归属推理基准，包含2.4K人工验证样本，来自887个多样的多说话人音频片段；2) 提出CASH数据集：通过反事实音频（Counterfactual Audio）和说话人级困难负样本（Speaker-level Hard negatives）设计，指导模型优先关注声学语音线索而非语言信号；3) 训练A2R模型：一个30B参数模型，在CASH上优化以获得说话人归属推理能力。

Result: 评估20个领先SLM在HEAR上的表现发现它们在这些基础任务上存在困难，常依赖语义先验而非真实语音线索。A2R在HEAR上取得强性能，并展现出对多样多说话人下游任务的零样本泛化能力，证明学习说话人归属能解锁模型潜在的说话人感知推理能力。

Conclusion: 说话人归属推理是SLM在多说话人场景中尚待解决的关键能力缺口。通过学习声学语音线索优先化的训练（CASH），SLM可被有效引导获得说话人归属能力，且这一能力可迁移至下游任务，表明说话人归属是实现更深层说话人感知推理的基石。

Abstract: Speech Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-speaker environments, yet their ability to attribute speech to the correct speaker and reason over speaker identities remains unclear. Hence, we introduce HEAR, a conceptually hierarchical benchmark diagnosing the foundational capabilities of speaker-attributed reasoning, comprising 2.4K human-verified samples from 887 diverse multi-party audio clips. Evaluating 20 leading SLMs on HEAR reveals they struggle with these foundational tasks, often relying on semantic priors rather than actual vocal cues. To address this, we present A2R, a 30B model optimized on Counterfactual Audio with Speaker-level Hard negatives (CASH), a dataset designed to guide the model to prioritize acoustic vocal cues over linguistic signals. A2R achieves strong performance on HEAR and exhibits zero-shot generalization to diverse multi-speaker downstream tasks, demonstrating that learned speaker attribution unlocks the model's latent capacity for speaker-aware reasoning. All resources are available at https://attributetoreason.github.io/AttributeToReason/

</details>


### [111] [AI Historian: Helping historians organize and verify person-centred temporal clues from dispersed historical narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29133)
*Yifeng Lu,Zijie Yang,Jie Li,Qingkai Min,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Historian (AIH)，一个AI代理系统，帮助历史学家从分散的传记叙述中组织人物-时间证据。它以源句子为证据单元，识别人物和时间线索，验证跨文本关联并推断可比较的时间范围，同时保留可追溯的源文本证据。在六个《史记》案例中，AIH的时序定位MicroIoU达86.2%，远超直接LLM提示的17.1%和人类标注的81.3%，且耗时仅14分钟（人类需1.5小时以上），并已应用于二十四史及其他东亚史料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史记录并非完整连续的形式保存，人物活动、关系和历史背景散落在不同文本、章节和叙事视角中。历史学家需要检索、识别和比较这些材料以重建时间序列并核实来源，这一过程耗时巨大且成本高昂，亟需自动化工具辅助。

Method: AIH采用AI代理系统架构，以源句子作为证据单元：首先识别人物和时间线索，然后验证候选的跨文本关联，最终推断可比较的时间范围，同时全程保留可追溯的源文本证据，确保历史研究的可验证性。

Result: 在六个《史记》案例（涉及刘邦、项羽和萧何）中，AIH的时序定位MicroIoU达86.2%，高于人类标注的81.3%，远优于直接LLM提示的17.1%；耗时约14分钟，而人类需1小时32分钟。系统已扩展到二十四史、古代日本和韩国史以及近现代史料，结果通过西湖历史学家平台发布。

Conclusion: AIH能显著降低组织大规模史料的人力成本，将因章节化叙述而模糊的历史关联转化为可追溯、可修订的研究问题，供协作验证。这为计算历史学提供了从分散叙述中重构时间序列的高效可行路径。

Abstract: History is not preserved in complete, continuous form. Accounts of a person's activities, relationships and historical contexts are scattered across texts, chapters and narrative perspectives; historians must retrieve, identify and compare these materials to reconstruct temporal sequences and verify them against sources. Here we present AI Historian (AIH), an AI agent system that helps historians organize person-time evidence from dispersed biographical narratives. It takes source sentences as evidence units, identifies people and temporal cues, verifies candidate cross-text associations and infers comparable temporal ranges while preserving traceable source-text evidence. We evaluated AIH on six Shiji cases concerning Liu Bang, Xiang Yu and Xiao He. AIH Agent achieved a temporal-localization MicroIoU of 86.2%, compared with 81.3% for human-only annotation and 17.1% for direct large-language-model prompting; it required about 14 min, versus 1 h 32 min for human-only annotation. We further applied AIH to the Twenty-Four Histories and other ancient Chinese histories, ancient Japanese and Korean histories, and modern and contemporary historical materials, and released the results through Westlake Historian. These results indicate that AIH can reduce the cost of organizing historical materials at scale while turning connections obscured by chapter-based narration into traceable, revisable research questions for collaborative testing.

</details>


### [112] [Quantifying Error Tolerance in Synthetic Data: An Atomic-level Operand vs. Operator Perturbation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29144)
*Jiaxiang Liu,Chenhao Yuan,Shuwen Xu,Boxuan Xing,Xiusheng Huang,Yinhao Xu,Hao Liu,Wenhao Teng,Xiangwen Liao,Pengfei Cao,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOM（Atomic Tree Operation Modeling）框架，将数据分解为功能单元（f(x)→y），区分良性操作数（Operand x）扰动与致命操作符（Operator f）扰动。实验发现模型对操作数扰动鲁棒但对操作符扰动崩溃，通过优先操作符多样性而非操作数精度，ATOM合成数据在多个基准上取得显著提升（如较LIMA提升3.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成是推进大语言模型的关键，但缺乏对错误容忍度的定量分析成为瓶颈。现有过滤策略要么过于激进（可能排除有价值的样本），要么过于宽松（无法有效消除错误样本），亟需一种更精细的数据质量评估方法。

Method: 引入Atomic Tree Operation Modeling (ATOM)框架，将数据分解为功能单元f(x)→y的形式。ATOM区分两类扰动：良性操作数（Operand x）扰动和致命操作符（Operator f）扰动，从而实现对合成数据更精准的质量控制和过滤。

Result: 实验揭示双重分离现象：模型对操作数扰动具有鲁棒性，但对操作符扰动会崩溃。在过滤策略上，优先操作符精度而非激进的操作数精度，使ATOM合成数据优于严格基线（在LIMA基础上提升3.1%），表明操作符多样性比操作数精度更重要。

Conclusion: ATOM框架提供了一种细粒度的数据质量分析手段，证明合成数据生成中操作符（Operator）的多样性与正确性比操作数（Operand）的精确性对模型性能影响更大，为合成数据过滤策略提供了新的理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation has become a cornerstone for advancing large language models. However, the lack of the quantitative analysis for error tolerance became a critical bottleneck. Consequently, current filtering strategies fluctuate between two extremes: they are either overly aggressive, risking the exclusion of potentially valuable samples, or overly permissive, failing to eliminate erroneous samples effectively. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces Atomic Tree Operation Modeling (ATOM), a framework that decomposes data into functional units ($f(x)\rightarrow y$). ATOM distinguishes benign Operand $x$ perturbations from fatal Operator $f$ perturbations. The former are needlessly discarded by aggressive filtering, while the latter slip through permissive filtering. Our experiments reveal a double dissociation: models are robust to operand perturbations but collapse under operator perturbations. By prioritizing operator over aggressive operand precision, our ATOM-synthesized data outperforms rigorous baselines (e.g., +3.1% gain over LIMA), suggesting that operator diversity matters more than operand precision. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lut-hub/ATOM.

</details>


### [113] [Toward a Cross-Lingual Romanization Ecosystem for Sinitic Languages: A Paired Mandarin-Cantonese Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29170)
*Zijie Zhang,Tan Lee,Yong Cao,Benyou Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Sinitic罗马化生态系统（Sinitic Romanization Ecosystem），一个跨语言的汉语方言罗马化设计框架及配套数字基础设施和开源工作流。通过四条设计原则（音系对应、历史音韵对应、一音一符、基本拉丁字母）系统化地对齐汉语方言的罗马化方案，并开发了粤语（CantRomZJ1）、普通话（MandRomZJ1）、梅县客家话、上海吴语、南京江淮官话等方案。经Meta MMS微调评估，MandRomZJ1+CantRomZJ1条件相比Pinyin+Jyutping基线，粤语WER和CER分别降低7.80%和10.61%，表明跨语言罗马化对齐可改善低资源汉语方言语音技术中的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汉语各方言（Sinitic languages）之间缺乏系统性的跨语言罗马化对齐方案，导致语音技术在各低资源方言中的迁移受限。现有罗马化方案各自独立设计，符号表示不一致，阻碍了跨语言的学习和资源复用。本文旨在通过提出系统的设计框架来解决这一对齐缺失问题。

Method: 提出四条设计原则：1）音系对应（phonetic correspondence）——相似音用相似罗马化符号表示；2）历史音韵对应（historical-phonological correspondence）——同源词对齐罗马化字符串；3）一音一符（one-phoneme-one-symbol）；4）基本拉丁字母使用（basic Latin-letter use），同时承认这些原则间存在权衡。基于该框架开发CantRomZJ1和MandRomZJ1作为主要配对案例，并扩展到梅县客家话、上海吴语和南京江淮官话。构建开源基础设施，涵盖结构化罗马化存储、转换、解析、词典构建和输入法生成。评估上采用基于Meta MMS微调的语音到罗马化（speech-to-romanization）实验。

Result: 在语音到罗马化实验中，MandRomZJ1+CantRomZJ1条件相比Pinyin+Jyutping基线，粤语的词错误率（WER）降低7.80%，字符错误率（CER）降低10.61%，验证了跨语言罗马化对齐对低资源方言语音技术迁移的促进作用。

Conclusion: 跨语言罗马化对齐能够有效提升低资源Sinitic语音技术中的迁移性能。该设计框架加开源基础设施的组合，为汉语方言的语音资源建设和技术发展提供了可行的系统性路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Sinitic Romanization Ecosystem, a cross-lingual Sinitic romanization design framework with supporting digital infrastructure and a community-driven open-source workflow. The design framework addresses the lack of systematic cross-lingual romanization alignment among Sinitic languages through four design principles: phonetic correspondence for representing similar sounds with similar romanized symbols, historical-phonological correspondence for aligning cognate romanization strings, one-phoneme-one-symbol, and basic Latin-letter use, with a balancing consideration recognizing trade-offs among these principles. For the main paired case study, we devel-op CantRomZJ1 and MandRomZJ1, Cantonese and Manda-rin romanization schemes following the design framework, respectively. We also develop schemes for several other Sinitic languages, including Meixian Hakka, Shanghai Wu, and Nanjing Jianghuai Mandarin, following the same de-sign framework. To bring the romanization schemes into practical use, we develop open-source infrastructure for structured romanization storage, conversion, parsing, dic-tionary construction, and input-method generation. Finally, we evaluate the design framework through speech-to-romanization experiments based on Meta's Massively Mul-tilingual Speech (MMS) fine-tuning. Compared with the Pinyin+Jyutping baseline, our Man-dRomZJ1+CantRomZJ1 condition reduces Cantonese WER and CER by 7.80% and 10.61%, respectively. These results suggest that cross-lingual romanization alignment can improve transfer in low-resource Sinitic speech technology.

</details>


### [114] [Toward Cultural Alignment: Human-Centered Evaluation of Multimodal AI Stories Across Five African Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29209)
*Millicent Ochieng,Felermino D. M. A. Ali,Elizabeth A. Ankrah,Najeeb Gambo Abdulhamid,Migisha Boyd,Stephanie Nyairo,Mercy Muchai,Samuel Chege Maina,Aditya Vashistha,Anja Thieme,Jacki O'Neill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过社区扎根的混合方法评估（19位来自五个非洲社区的文化代表参与），研究了AI生成的多模态故事的文化对齐问题。研究提出了包含五大文化标记类别和八种错位机制的文化对齐分类体系，并评估了五个多模态LLM评判器，发现其可靠性和分数校准在不同社区间差异显著，没有单一评判器能在所有场景中表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI生成多模态故事评估通常依赖于自动化指标或单一文化视角，缺乏对生成内容与实际社区文化实践、关系、语言、价值观和视觉期望的深度对齐检验。研究者希望解决AI生成内容在不同文化背景下可能产生的文化错位问题，并探索自动评估是否能够替代社区参与式人工评估。

Method: 采用社区扎根的混合方法评估设计：1）招募19位来自五个非洲社区的文化代表；2）结合定量标注与定性焦点小组讨论；3）从评估结果中归纳出包含五大文化标记类别（社会、语言、程序、视觉等语境维度）和八种错位机制的文化对齐分类体系；4）额外评估五个多模态LLM评判器，对比其与社区判断的一致性。

Result: 研究发现：文化对齐不仅取决于可识别的文化标记，更取决于这些标记如何嵌入社会、语言、程序和视觉语境中；五个多模态LLM评判器的可靠性和分数校准在不同社区间差异显著，没有单一评判器能在所有五个社区场景中表现一致。

Conclusion: 研究结论主张采用社区校准的评估管线：自动化评判器需通过与社区判断进行验证，以确定其在哪些场景中可以信任、哪些场景仍需人工审查介入。这为AI生成内容的文化对齐评估提供了理论和实践框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine how well AI-generated multimodal stories align with the lived practices, relationships, language, values, and visual expectations of the communities they represent. We conduct a community-grounded mixed-methods evaluation with 19 culture representatives across five African communities, combining quantitative annotations with qualitative focus group discussions. We find that cultural alignment depends not simply on recognizable cultural markers, but on how those markers fit social, linguistic, procedural, and visual context. From these evaluations, we develop a taxonomy of cultural alignment comprising five broader cultural marker categories and eight recurring mechanisms of misalignment. We additionally evaluate five multimodal LLM judges to examine whether automated evaluation can approximate community-grounded judgments at scale. Judge reliability and score calibration vary substantially across communities, with no single judge performing consistently across all five settings. These findings motivate community-calibrated evaluation pipelines in which automated judges are validated against community judgments to determine where they can be trusted and where human review remains necessary.

</details>


### [115] [Attribute-Based Activation Steering of LLMs for Group-Specific Explanation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29215)
*Leandra Fichtel,Janek Prange,Henning Wachsmuth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活工程（activation engineering）的方法，通过计算目标群体的属性引导向量并将其添加到LLM内部激活中，从而实现针对特定人群定制化的解释生成，在特异性和事实性方面优于提示词方法和现有引导基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让用户理解新主题需要根据其背景和能力进行定制化解释，但仅靠提示词（prompting）不足以为不同人群生成个性化解释，且缺乏其他计算方法，因此需要探索如何引导LLM生成针对特定群体的定制化解释。

Method: 首先识别特定目标群体在解释风格和知识层面的组特定属性，然后基于激活工程技术计算属性引导向量，在推理过程中将这些向量添加到LLM的内部激活中，实现对生成解释的细粒度引导。

Result: 在评估中，该方法在特异性和事实性方面均优于提示词方法和现有最先进的引导基线，且通过不同目标群体的人类专家研究验证了其有效性——生成的解释能显著更好地针对目标群体定制，同时基本保持事实性。

Conclusion: 基于激活工程的属性引导方法能够有效且精确地将LLM生成的解释定制到特定目标群体，为个性化解释生成提供了一种优于提示词的可行计算方案。

Abstract: To effectively enable people to understand new topics, explanations should be tailored to their backgrounds and abilities. So far, prompting alone has been shown to be insufficient for creating such explanations and other computational methods are missing. Therefore, this paper investigates whether LLMs can be steered to generate explanations that are tailored to a specific group of people. To this end, we propose an approach that first identifies group-specific attributes in terms of explanatory style and knowledge of a specific target group. Building on activation engineering, it then computes attribute-based steering vectors and adds them to the internal activations of an LLM during inference to enable a fine-grained steering. In our experiments, we assess the steering effectiveness of our approach in terms of specificity and factuality of the generated explanations. Additionally, we evaluate the explanations in a study with human experts from different target groups. Compared to prompting and state-of-the-art steering baselines, our approach tailors the explanations significantly better to the target group while largely maintaining factuality.

</details>


### [116] [Anchoring Speech with Semantics: A Multimodal Adapter Mechanism for Automatic Speech Recognition in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29239)
*Kuan-Tang Huang,Cheng-Yeh Yang,Chien-Chun Wang,Hung-Shin Lee,Hsin-Min Wang,Berlin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAMA-ASR，一种轻量级适配器机制，通过辅助翻译的语义锚点和语音的声学锚点增强解码器，在低资源中文方言（台湾闽南语和客家话）30小时数据集上超越多种基线，且在翻译器自动生成语义锚点的实际场景下依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语音识别（ASR）面临的主要挑战是：稀缺的转写文本只能为目标端生成提供有限的监督信号。这使得模型难以在训练数据不足的情况下学习充分的目标端语言建模和序列生成能力。因此需要引入额外的辅助信息来弥补监督不足的问题。

Method: 提出SAMA-ASR轻量级适配器机制，通过跨模态适配从两个来源增强解码器：1) 辅助翻译生成的语义锚点（语义嵌入），提供话语级别的语义信息；2) 语音嵌入（声学锚点），提供基于语音的证据。在测试时，语义锚点由上游语音到文本翻译器自动生成，而非依赖oracle翻译。该机制原则上可推广到类似的编码器-解码器多任务语音模型。

Result: 在两个30小时的低资源中文方言数据集（台湾闽南语和客家话）上的实验表明，SAMA-ASR优于基于声学、先前提示式（prompt-based）和纯语义翻译引导的基线。在翻译器自动生成语义锚点的实用场景下仍保持有效；翻译器容量分析显示，紧凑的语音翻译（ST）模型即可产生有用的语义锚点。

Conclusion: SAMA-ASR通过将话语级语义信息和语音证据在token预测前注入解码器状态，有效缓解了低资源ASR中监督不足的问题。其轻量化的适配器设计和对自动语义锚点的鲁棒性使其具备实际部署价值，且紧凑ST模型即可支撑其性能。

Abstract: Low-resource ASR remains difficult because scarce transcripts provide limited supervised evidence for target-side generation. To address this gap, we propose SAMA-ASR, a lightweight adapter mechanism that augments the decoder with semantic anchors from auxiliary translations and an acoustic anchor from speech; in principle, the mechanism can be applied to similar encoder--decoder multitask speech models. Through cross-modal adaptation, SAMA-ASR conditions decoder states on translation-derived semantic embeddings and a speech embedding, combining utterance-level meaning with speech-grounded evidence before token prediction. At evaluation time, these semantic anchors can be generated automatically by an upstream speech-to-text translator rather than supplied as oracle translations. Experiments on two 30-hour datasets covering the low-resource Sinitic varieties Taiwanese Hokkien and Hakka show that SAMA-ASR improves over acoustic, prior prompt-based, and semantic-only translation-guided baselines and remains effective in practical automatic semantic-anchor settings; translator-capacity analyses show that useful semantic anchors can be produced by a compact ST model.

</details>


### [117] [When Patients Cut In: Extending Clinical Conversational AI Safety to Interruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29241)
*Zachary Ellis,Spencer Hazel,Adam Brandt,Yajie Vera He,Ernest Lim,Jared Joselowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对临床语音AI代理在实际部署中患者会打断说话的问题，提出了一种基于转录本的打断恢复评估方法，将对话分析中的重叠类别改编为三种操作类型（识别型、竞争型、过渡子单元型），测试四种非推理型LLM配置在病史采集（信息收集）和FAQ（信息提供）四个单元中的表现，评估代理是否保留临床必需内容。结果显示：信息提供单元中所有模型的失败率均上升，竞争型FAQ打断导致四种模型全部出现30/30的信息提供覆盖失败（Wilson 95% CI: 88.6-100.0%），道歉标记对恢复效果影响不一致甚至降低某些模型的恢复能力；因此打断鲁棒性不能是单一分数，评估必须基于内容、按单元报告并与部署的打断特征匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语音代理已在实际诊疗中部署，但真实患者会在代理说话中途打断，导致级联架构（语音识别->LLM->语音合成）丢失临床必需内容。然而，现有的临床对话AI基准测试几乎都假设患者会等代理说完，忽略了打断导致的内容损失问题，缺乏针对打断恢复能力的系统性评估框架。

Method: 作者提出一种基于转录本的打断恢复评估方法：将对话分析的语轮重叠类别改编为三种操作类型（识别型、竞争型、过渡子单元型）；测试四种面向部署的非推理型LLM配置；覆盖四个单元，分别对应病史采集（信息收集）和FAQ（信息提供）两类任务；评估指标为代理是否保留了临床必需内容，并比较了添加道歉标记（"sorry to interrupt"）的影响。

Result: 在信息收集单元中，目标问题失败率因模型而异；在信息提供单元中（可进行直接比较），所有模型的失败率均上升。跨单元排名不一致。竞争型FAQ打断下，四种模型全部出现30/30的信息提供覆盖失败（Wilson 95% CI: 88.6-100.0%；基线为三个模型0/30、Llama 4/30）。道歉标记可使恢复率变化数十个百分点，但效果因模型而异且不一致，对其中一个模型反而降低恢复率。

Conclusion: 打断鲁棒性不能用一个单一的分数来衡量。评估必须基于内容、按单元（cell）分别报告结果，并与实际部署场景的打断特征相匹配，才能真实反映临床语音代理在真实环境中的可靠性。

Abstract: Clinical voice agents are now deployed in routine care, where real patients do not wait their turn: they interrupt. These systems typically use a cascaded architecture (speech-to-text -> LLM -> text-to-speech), so when a patient cuts the agent off mid-utterance, clinically required content can be lost even when the model handles cooperative transcripts well. Yet clinical conversational-AI benchmarks almost universally assume patients wait for the agent to finish, missing interruption-induced loss of required content. We present a transcript-based evaluation of interruption recovery, adapting conversation-analytic overlap categories into three operational types (recognitional, competitive, transitional sub-unit) and testing four deployment-oriented, non-reasoning LLM configurations across four cells spanning history-taking (information gathering) and FAQ (information provision), scored on whether the agent preserves the clinically required content. In the gathering cells, target-question failure varied across models; in the provision cells, where arms are directly comparable, failure rose for every model. Rankings differ across cells, and competitive FAQ interruption produced 30/30 provision-coverage failures for all four models (Wilson 95% CI: 88.6-100.0%; baseline 0/30 for three, 4/30 for Llama). A brief apology marker ("sorry to interrupt") shifts recovery by tens of percentage points, inconsistently across models, and for one it reduces recovery. Interruption robustness therefore cannot be a single score: evaluation must be content-grounded, reported per cell, and matched to the deployment's interruption profile.

</details>


### [118] [Large Language Models Systematically Favor Popular Options: Evidence and Mitigation Across MCQs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29257)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,Mohammed Ali,Bhawna Piryani,Mahmoud Abdalla,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在多项选择评估中系统性偏好流行选项而非正确答案（称为"流行度偏差"），并提出了PopMCQ基准（含六种受控策略）与PopDebias轻量级推理时纠正方法，实验于22个开源LLM上验证了显著的效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项选择题是评估LLM的标准格式，但选项的流行度会混淆评估结果。现代LLM系统性偏向选择流行但错误的选项，这一偏差与置信度校准问题（模型置信度在高错误率区域依然很高）密切相关，需要被系统性的隔离和研究。

Method: 本文提出两个核心组件：(1) PopMCQ基准，包含六种受控策略来改变选项流行度同时固定正确答案，其中最对抗的设置是所有干扰项比正确选项更流行；(2) PopDebias，一种轻量级推理时纠偏方法，通过估算并从模型预测中减去流行度先验来实现纠偏，无需微调、测试时无需标签（仅需小量校准集进行参数拟合），计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在最对抗性设置下（所有干扰项比正确选项更流行），模型选择流行但错误的答案比例高达66%。在22个开源LLM（0.5B至32B参数）上的实验显示，PopDebias在所有模型上均带来一致改进，在强流行度压力下准确率提升最高达54.1个百分点。

Conclusion: LLM存在显著的流行度偏差，可通过推理时的轻量级先验移除方法有效缓解。PopMCQ和PopDebias为研究及缓解该偏差提供了可复现的基准与工具，代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Multiple-choice questions (MCQs) are a standard format for evaluating large language models (LLMs), yet the popularity of answer options can confound evaluation. Modern LLMs systematically prefer popular but incorrect options over less popular correct ones, a vulnerability we call \textbf{popularity bias}. This pattern aligns with confidence miscalibration: model confidence remains high even as accuracy collapses for popular options. To systematically isolate this phenomenon, we introduce \textbf{PopMCQ}, a benchmark with six controlled strategies that vary option popularity while keeping the correct answer fixed. In our most adversarial setting, where all distractors are more popular than the correct option, models choose popular but wrong answers 66\% of the time. To mitigate this bias, we propose \textbf{PopDebias}, a lightweight inference-time correction that estimates and removes a popularity prior from model predictions. It requires no fine-tuning, is label-free at test time (using only a small calibration split for parameter fitting), and adds negligible computational cost. Experiments on 22 open-source LLMs (0.5B to 32B parameters) show consistent improvements, with accuracy gains up to 54.1 percentage points under strong popularity pressure. The code and data are available https://github.com/DataScienceUIBK/PopMCQ

</details>


### [119] [SHADOWBENCH: Toward Reliable Automatic Evaluation of Semantic Alignment in Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29270)
*Hojae Han,Jongyoon Kim,Sanghyuk Park,Dongwook Cheon,Myungjae Jeon,Sunjong Choi,Soonho Kong,Wonseok Heo,Seung-won Hwang,Donghoon Hyeon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-Pass（语义对齐通过）指标，用称为"影子"的辅助语句来测试形式化语句的语义对齐程度，并构建了ShadowBench——一个包含178个研究生至研究级问题、覆盖8个数学领域的Lean 4全自动形式化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动形式化中，现有评估指标存在两个问题：可能接受类型正确但语义错位的语句，或拒绝用不同表述写的正确语句。需要一个更可靠的语义对齐评估方法。

Method: 受Pass@k启发，提出SA-Pass：生成的语句仅在满足三个条件时获得满分——能够编译、能推出每个影子语句（前向检查）、且能被影子语句的合取推出（后向检查）。基于此构建了ShadowBench基准。

Result: Claude Code (Opus 4.8) + Numina-Lean-Agent在ShadowBench上达到61.8%的编译率和11.2%的SA-Pass。SA-Pass在六种智能体配置的输出上与专家判断有98.8%的二元一致性。ShadowBench早期版本被用作ICML 2026 AI4Math挑战赛Track 4的基准。

Conclusion: SA-Pass通过前后向影子检查提供了一个更可靠的语义对齐评估框架，ShadowBench则构成了一个高难度的全自动形式化基准，未来可能推动该领域评估标准的发展。

Abstract: Autoformalization translates informal mathematical theorems into code for proof assistants such as Lean. A central challenge is that current evaluation metrics can accept type-correct but misaligned statements or reject correct statements written in a different formulation. Inspired by Pass@$k$, we propose SA-Pass (*Semantic Alignment Pass*), which tests formal statements using auxiliary statements called *shadows* that characterize the intended statement. A generated statement receives full credit only when it compiles, implies each shadow (forward check), and is implied by their conjunction (backward check). We instantiate SA-Pass in ShadowBench, a Lean 4 full autoformalization benchmark of 178 postgraduate- to research-level problems spanning eight mathematical areas. Claude Code (Opus 4.8) with Numina-Lean-Agent reaches $61.8\%$ compile rate and $11.2\%$ SA-Pass. Across outputs generated by six agentic configurations, SA-Pass achieves $98.8\%$ binary agreement with expert judgments. An early version of ShadowBench served as the benchmark for Track 4 of the ICML 2026 AI4Math Challenge.

</details>


### [120] [Modality Fault Lines: Structural Corruptions Reveal Fragile Omni-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29278)
*Zhaolu Kang,Meixin Wu,Yu Xue,Yingjie He,Qiming Shi,Lei Wei,Yidi Wang,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCEval（Structure-Corruption Evaluation）诊断评估协议，通过对文本、视觉、音频通道施加受控的结构性破坏（保持问题、答案空间和模态通道不变），检验全模态大语言模型在模态内部证据结构被扰动时的稳定性。基于273个人工验证的三模态样本（来自Social-IQ、OmniBench和VALOR），评估了15个专有和开源全模态系统。结果显示：结构性破坏会降低干净准确率，文本-视觉损伤构成最稳定的共享故障线，多模态退化呈非加性特征。干净环境下的全模态准确率无法证明模型在跨模态证据结构不可靠时依然稳定可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态大语言模型的评估通常在干净的文本-视觉-音频输入上进行（所有通道都存在、同步且可解释），这种评估无法区分模型的成功是否依赖于稳定的跨模态结构，还是仅仅依赖于完整输入中才存在的线索。为弥补这一评估缺口，需要设计一种能在模态保持存在但内部证据结构被扰动的情况下测试模型稳定性的诊断协议。

Method: 定义"模态故障线"（modality fault line）——即当模态仍然存在且人类可解释，但其内部证据结构被扰动时，模型行为变得不稳定的边界。据此引入SCEval诊断评估协议：保持问题、答案空间和模态通道不变，对文本、视觉和音频通道分别及联合施加受控的结构性破坏。从Social-IQ、OmniBench和VALOR构建273个人工验证的三模态样本，评估15个专有和开源全模态系统。

Result: 结构性破坏降低了干净准确率；文本-视觉损伤形成最稳定的共享故障线；多模态退化是非加性的，并非受损模态数量的简单函数。

Conclusion: 干净的全模态准确率不能证明模型在跨模态证据结构变得不可靠时仍能保持可靠性，需要将结构鲁棒性评估纳入全模态模型的诊断体系。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models are increasingly evaluated on clean text--vision--audio inputs, where every channel is present, synchronized, and readily interpretable. Such scores are often taken as evidence of robust cross-modal fusion, but clean evaluation cannot tell whether success depends on stable cross-modal structure or on cues sufficient only in intact inputs. To address this gap, we define a modality fault line: a boundary at which model behavior becomes unstable when a modality remains present and human-interpretable, but its internal evidence structure is perturbed. We introduce SCEval (Structure-Corruption Evaluation) a diagnostic evaluation protocol that keeps the question, answer space, and modality channels fixed while applying controlled structural corruptions to text, vision, and audio individually and jointly. Built from $273$ human-verified tri-modal examples from Social-IQ, OmniBench, and VALOR, SCEval evaluates $15$ proprietary and open-source omni-modal systems. The results show that structural corruption lowers clean accuracy, text--vision damage forms the most stable shared fault line, and multi-modal degradation is non-additive rather than a simple function of the number of corrupted modalities. Clean omni-modal accuracy therefore does not establish that a model will remain reliable when cross-modal evidence becomes structurally unreliable.

</details>


### [121] [Cloud and On-Premises Deployment of Uzbek Legal RAG via Targeted Retriever Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29284)
*Tatul Danielyan,Mariam Avetisyan,Hrant Davtyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了为乌兹别克语构建和运营检索增强（RAG）法律助手的实践经验，该助手需在两种运行模式下工作：受每token成本上限约束的托管云服务，以及受延迟和硬件限制、只能使用开源模型的本地部署。作者构建了两个领域基准（178条专家标注的检索基准和504对端到端问答基准），发现开源与专有模型的差距很小且可通过微调廉价弥补，因此训练了UTOE-1——乌兹别克语开源模型中SOTA的文本嵌入器。同时，通过QLoRA实验的负面结果证明微调长上下文法律问答在硬件上不切实际且无必要。论文发布了基准、评估代码和微调嵌入器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用排行榜无法反映低资源语言在法律问答场景中的真实挑战，特别是硬性运维约束（成本上限、延迟限制、数据不能离开本地基础设施）与语言资源稀缺的双重压力下，缺乏针对这一场景的评估基准和工程经验。

Method: 构建两个领域基准：178条专家标注的检索基准（含法律条文黄金片段），以及504对专家整理的端到端问答对，使用经过人类判断和异族LLM法官交叉验证的LLM评判器评分。在每个运行模式下分别评估开源与专有模型，并通过QLoRA微调实验检验性能提升的可行性与必要性。

Result: 开源与专有模型在端到端性能上的差距很小，且可通过微调廉价弥补；因此训练了UTE-1——乌兹别克语开源模型中的SOTA文本嵌入器。但QLoRA微调实验为负面结果：由于长上下文法律问答对硬件要求极高，且法律条文频繁变更使得定期重新微调成为负担，微调在工程上不切实际且没有必要。

Conclusion: 在低资源语言的法律RAG部署中，优先通过检索质量优化（如领域微调嵌入器）而非微调生成模型来弥合开源与专有模型的差距是更务实的策略。论文发布了基准、评估代码和UTE-1嵌入器，并提炼出可供类似部署参考的实践经验。

Abstract: Deploying large language models for legal question answering raises challenges that general-purpose leaderboards do not capture, particularly for low-resource languages and under hard operational constraints. We report on building and operating a retrieval-augmented (RAG) legal assistant for Uzbek that must run in two regimes: a managed cloud service that maximizes answer quality within a per-token cost ceiling, and an on-premises deployment for clients whose legal data may not leave their infrastructure, restricting us to open-weight models on limited local hardware under latency constraints. Because no evaluation existed for this setting, we build two domain benchmarks: a retrieval benchmark of 178 expert-annotated legal queries with gold provision spans, and an end-to-end benchmark of 504 expert-curated question--answer pairs scored by an LLM judge whose ratings we validate against human judgments and against an independent-family judge. Applying these benchmarks under each regime, we find the open-versus-proprietary gap is small and cheaply closed by fine-tuning. Therefore, we train UTE-1, which is a state-of-the-art text embedder among open models for Uzbek. We also demonstrate that closing the performance gap via fine-tuning is both impractical due to the intensive hardware demands of long-context legal Q\&A and unnecessary, given that legal acts change frequently. We support this by reporting a negative result from a QLoRA experiment. We distill practical guidance for similar deployments, drawn from a system serving real users in production. We release our benchmarks, evaluation code and the fine-tuned embedder (UTE-1) \href{https://metric-ai-lab.github.io/Uzbek-Legal-RAG/}{at this https URL} to support future work on low-resource legal NLP.

</details>


### [122] [Learning Simple Test-Time Environments for LLM Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29305)
*Junxuan Li,Zijun Liu,Ziyi Huang,Peng Li,Yuzhou Liu,Ming Yan,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出测试时环境分解（TTED）方法，让LLM Web代理在推理阶段将复杂环境观测分解为子模块并通过无标签学习积累经验，从而提升在真实复杂Web环境中的组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在人工构建的简单环境中表现出色，但在迁移到真实复杂环境时性能显著下降。现有研究将这种退化归因于LLM在多个简单、良好结构环境组合上的组合泛化缺口，缺乏有效方法让代理适应复杂环境。

Method: 提出TTED（Test-Time Environment Decomposition）框架：1）引入trial steps让代理将复杂环境观测分解为子模块；2）实现无标签学习方法，在推理过程中利用经验动态调整代理行为，实现测试时训练。

Result: 在合成和真实基准上的实验表明：1）在简单子环境中获得的经验可以有效地组合以提升完整环境的性能；2）子环境上的测试时训练能显著增强代理在真实Web自动化任务中的组合泛化能力。同时提供了无标签学习算法的关键设计洞见。

Conclusion: 测试时学习环境分解技能对于LLM代理在真实世界的稳健部署至关重要，随着代理访问更复杂的环境，该方法将成为提升其泛化能力的关键手段。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in manually constructed environments, yet their performance frequently collapses when transitioned to complex real-world settings. Existing research largely attribute this degradation to the compositional generalization gaps in LLMs on combinations of multiple simple, well-structured environments. In this work, we propose that LLM web agents can learn simple environment observations at test time. Specifically, we introduce trial steps for agents to decompose a complex environment observation into sub-modules, and implement a label-free learning method, Test-Time Environment Decomposition (TTED), to adapt agent behaviors with experience during inference. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate the framework's efficacy across both synthetic and realistic benchmarks, showing (1) experience gains acquired within simpler sub-environments can be effectively composed to improve performance in the full one, and (2) test-time training on sub-environments can significantly enhance the compositional generalization of agents in real-world web automation tasks. We also provide key insights in the design of the label-free learning algorithm. As more complex environments are accessed by LLM agents, we believe learning environment decomposition skills at test time will be critical for robust real-world deployment.

</details>


### [123] [Detecting and Repairing Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29307)
*Sai Krishna Reddy Mulakkayala,Niki van Stein,Aske Plaat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究检索增强生成（RAG）系统中如何修复已标记为包含不实内容的回答。作者将每个带标记的回答拆分为独立的事实主张，逐一与检索来源核对，并比较三种修复策略：删除不支持的主张、用源文本替换、以及重写。通过三个不同模型家族的评审，发现所有策略都能减少不实内容，其中删除策略效果最好但保留原文最少（64.3%），重写保留最多（80.1%）但减少效果最弱。此外，83.5%被标注为干净的回答也被编辑，表明修复并不限于有问题的回答。研究结论是这些策略构成"接地保留"权衡而非质量排序，选择何种策略需要超出自动指标能提供的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有大量研究关注检测RAG系统中的不实陈述（幻觉），但检测本身对读者没有实际帮助，且几乎没有研究探讨检测后应采取的修复行动。本文填补了这一空白，系统性地比较不同的修复策略。

Method: 使用RAGTruth基准（手工标注的不支持片段），将每个被标记的回答分解为独立事实主张，逐一与检索来源核对，然后比较四种方案：不处理、删除不支持主张、用源文本替换、重写。由三个不同模型家族对916个修复后的回答进行评判。

Result: 所有修复策略均降低了回答中被判定包含不支持内容的比例，且三个评判模型对策略排序达成一致。删除策略带来最大程度的不实内容减少，但仅保留原文的64.3%；重写保留80.1%但减少效果最弱。83.5%被标注为干净的回答也被编辑了。

Conclusion: 不同修复策略处于"接地保留"（grounding preservation）权衡的不同位置，而非形成简单的质量排序。选择具体修复策略需要关于回答实际有用性的证据，这是自动评估指标无法提供的。

Abstract: Language models increasingly answer questions by consulting retrieved documents rather than memory alone, a design now common in search assistants and enterprise knowledge tools. Grounding a model in retrieved text reduces unsupported statements but does not eliminate them, and a reader cannot tell a grounded sentence from an invented one. Most research on this problem stops at detection, yet flagging a faulty answer changes nothing for the person reading it, and little is known about which action should follow. Using RAGTruth, a benchmark whose unsupported passages are annotated by hand, we split each flagged answer into individual factual claims, check each against the retrieved source, and compare leaving the answer untouched with three repair strategies of increasing richness: deleting an unsupported claim, replacing it with source text, and rewriting it. Three language models from different families judge the 916 repaired answers. Every strategy reduces the proportion of answers judged to contain unsupported content, and all three judges agree on the ordering. Deletion achieves the largest reduction while retaining least of the original answer, at 64.3% of the text, whereas rewriting retains 80.1% and reduces least. Repair is not confined to faulty answers: 83.5% of answers annotated clean are edited too. The strategies occupy different points on a grounding preservation trade-off rather than forming a quality ranking, and choosing between them needs evidence about answer usefulness that automatic metrics cannot supply.

</details>


### [124] [All You Need Is Non-Commutative Words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29314)
*Carla M. Quispe Flores,Stanley Salvatierra,Renan Cabrera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用幺正矩阵表示词汇token，将句子编码为有序矩阵乘积，利用矩阵乘法的非交换性自然捕捉词序，无需位置编码。该代数框架同时支持无投影的自注意力、变长文本块的并行组合、以及基于陪集读出层的持续学习。在IMDB和AG News等文本分类基准上匹配或超越bag-of-words基线，仅用64个实数参数编码替代约30,000维词表空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统词表示方法（如词袋模型）丢失词序信息，而基于位置编码的Transformer需要额外的PE机制且参数量大。本文旨在寻找一种能天然编码词序、参数高效且支持持续学习的词表示代数框架。

Method: 将每个词汇token表示为幺正矩阵，句子编码为其有序乘积。利用矩阵乘积的非交换性捕捉词序，无需位置编码。设计无query/key/value投影的反对称自注意力机制，支持变长文本块的并行组合以降低注意力成本。使用canonical-coset读出层编码所有真实幺正自由度，并通过嵌套群扩展实现持续学习，在新任务中扩大算子空间且精确保留旧任务表示。

Result: 在标准文本分类基准上，该方法匹配或超越词袋模型基线：在IMDB上获得更高准确率，在AG News上表现相当。用64参数实数编码取代约30,000维传统词表空间，仍能达到竞争性性能，展示参数表达的效率优势。

Conclusion: 幺正矩阵代数提供了一种无需位置编码即能天然编码词序的替代方案，以极低参数开销实现有竞争力的分类性能，并具备持续学习能力，证明了该参数化方式的表达效率。

Abstract: We represent lexical tokens as unitary matrices and encode each sentence as their ordered product. The noncommutativity of matrix product captures word order without positional encodings (PEs). The same algebra yields several capabilities, including antisymmetric self-attention with no query, key, or value projections, and parallel composition of variable-length text chunks at a reduced attention cost. Furthermore, it provides a canonical-coset readout layer that encodes all true unitary degrees of freedom compactly, while supporting continual learning through nested group extensions that enlarge the operator space with each new task preserving prior representations exactly. Across standard text-classification benchmarks, the method matches or exceeds bag-of-words baselines. Achieving higher accuracy on IMDB and comparable performance on AG News. Notably, this is accomplished by replacing the conventional $\sim$30,000-dimensional vocabulary space with a dense, 64-parameter real-valued encoding, highlighting the expressive efficiency of our parameterization.

</details>


### [125] [Padārtha: Ontology-Grounded Fine-Grained NER Benchmark for Classical Sanskrit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29324)
*Sujoy Sarkar,Pretam Ray,Paramhans Shah,Manoj Balaji Jagadeeshan,Akash Gairola,Arjuna S R,Pawan Goyal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Padārtha，首个基于本体论（Nyāya-Vaiśesika哲学体系）的细粒度梵语命名实体识别（NER）基准数据集，构建于《摩诃婆罗多》史诗之上，包含18个细粒度类别、108,335个实体提及，并系统对比了生成式NER与传统架构在梵语上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NER标注体系并非中性，其在现代新闻文本上开发的标签集强加了源文化定义，不适用于古典文学文本。作者主张标注模式应扎根于文本自身的传统，因此需要为梵语古典文本建立符合其文化本体论的细粒度NER基准。

Method: 作者从古典印度本体论系统Nyāya-Vaiśesika中派生标签集，构建18个细粒度类别并组织在10个本体节点下，同时映射到5个标准粗粒度标签以保持互操作性。专家标注了来自学术索引的12.6K个命名实体，链接到Mahānāma语料库中的对应提及，产生108,335个实体提及的细粒度标注（覆盖73,632个诗节），并额外构建5,000诗节的专家验证测试集，专门采样以强调罕见提及。

Result: 首次系统对比了生成式NER与传统架构在梵语上的表现：微调的生成式模型与传统任务特定系统表现相当。但所有系统从粗粒度到细粒度均出现性能急剧下降，且难以处理训练中未见过的实体提及。这一局限并非仅由数据稀缺导致——微调模型对未见实体的召回率远低于已见实体，且在词汇歧义下倾向于默认主要义项。

Conclusion: Padārtha证明了基于文本自身文化传统构建标注模式的价值，同时揭示了当前NER系统（无论生成式还是传统式）在处理细粒度分类和未见实体方面的系统性局限，指出问题的根源不仅在于数据量，更在于模型在词汇歧义时对多数义项的偏向性。

Abstract: Annotation schemas are not neutral. When applied to classical literature, tag sets developed for modern journalistic texts impose source-culture definitions on texts they were never designed to describe. We instead ground a schema in the tradition of the text itself introducing \textit{Padārtha}, the first ontology-grounded fine-grained Named Entity Recognition (NER) benchmark for Sanskrit, built on the \textit{Mahābhārata} epic. Our tag set derives from \textit{Nyāya-Vaiśesika}, a classical Indian ontological system, yielding 18 fine-grained categories organized under 10 ontological nodes and mapped onto five standard coarse tags, ensuring interoperability with existing benchmarks. Expert annotators label over 12.6K entries from a scholarly index of named entities, linked to corresponding mentions in the \textit{Mahānāma} corpus, producing fine-grained annotations for 108,335 entity mentions across 73,632 verses, along with a 5,000-verse expert-verified test set sampled to stress rare mentions. We present the first systematic benchmarking of generative NER against traditional architectures for Sanskrit, finding that fine-tuned generative models perform comparably to task-specific systems. However, all systems show a sharp decline from coarse to fine granularity and struggle with out-of-entity mentions unseen during training. The limitation is not due to data scarcity alone, as fine-tuned models recall unseen entities far worse than seen ones and tend to default to the majority sense under lexical ambiguity.

</details>


### [126] [StageWell: A Process-Aligned Chinese Corpus for Positive-Psychology Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29326)
*Yuxiong Wang,Ziwei Lin,Bo Wang,Yu Zhang,Shiguang Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StageWell，一个面向积极心理学对话的过程对齐中文语料库，以及HQS结构化数据构建与评估协议。StageWell将支持性对话组织为六阶段支持流程，通过多智能体全对话重写工作流构建了12,445条SFT实例、1,849对DPO偏好对和120条专家修订对话及977个QA对。实验表明，在四个9B-14B开源LLM上，该方法在过程控制、回复质量和安全性方面均取得稳健提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的积极心理学对话资源通常将监督简化为单轮策略标签或整体偏好标签，导致过程位置、支持功能和局部修复目标隐含不清，模型缺乏对多轮支持过程中连贯推进的建模能力。

Method: 构建StageWell语料：将支持性对话组织为六阶段支持流程；设计HQS协议，采用多智能体全对话重写工作流构造数据；DPO偏好对通过"过程局部化修复"构建，即用有缺陷的模型输出作为拒绝样本，在相同上下文和阶段约束下进行针对性重写作为选择样本。

Result: 在四个9B-14B开源LLM上，BERTScore平均提升0.037，Q-Overall提升1.32点，S-exact提升0.236，H-critical率降低0.167，证明该方法在过程控制、回复质量和安全性方面均优于基线。

Conclusion: 支持性对话应建模为结构化的多轮支持过程而非单轮回复生成，过程对齐的监督信号能够显著提升模型在积极心理学对话中的表现。

Abstract: Positive psychology dialogue aims to support emotional distress and positive resource building, requiring models to produce not only empathetic replies but also coherent progression through a multi-turn support process. Existing resources often reduce supervision to turn-level strategies or holistic preference labels, leaving process position, support function, and local repair targets implicit. We introduce StageWell, a process-aligned Chinese corpus for positive psychology dialogue, together with HQS, a structured protocol for data construction and evaluation. StageWell organizes support into a six-stage support process and uses a multi-agent whole-dialogue rewriting workflow to construct 12,445 SFT instances, 1,849 DPO preference pairs, and a GroundTruth subset of 120 expert-revised dialogues and 977 QA pairs. Guided by HQS, DPO pairs are built as process-localized repairs: flawed model outputs are used as rejected responses, and targeted rewrites under the same context and stage constraint are used as chosen responses. Across four 9B-14B open-source LLMs, this supervision yields robust gains in process control, response quality, and safety. Averaged across models, BERTScore improves by 0.037, Q-Overall increases by 1.32 points, S-exact increases by 0.236, and the H-critical rate decreases by 0.167. These results highlight the value of modeling supportive dialogue as a structured multi-turn support process rather than as single-turn response generation.

</details>


### [127] [When to Adapt: Conditional Memory Adapters for Retention-Preserving Domain Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29327)
*Jiayu Hou,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Engram Adapter框架，将预训练时的条件记忆重用作冻结大语言模型的事后适配器，通过多通道n-gram模式匹配与占用追踪作为选择性先验，使残差注入更可能作用于域内输入，同时用学习标量门控抑制OOD检索。在Qwen3-4B/8B上实验表明，域内精度提升的同时保留了99.4%-100.1%的OOD性能，且机械分析显示OOD残差被压缩至隐藏状态范数的约0.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法通常是"始终开启"的，其学到的扰动应用于每个输入，导致领域外(OOD)性能下降。本文旨在解决在提升专业领域性能的同时不牺牲通用能力的问题，探索条件激活的模块化适配路径。

Method: 提出Engram Adapter框架：(1) 将预训练时的条件记忆(post-hoc adapter)重用于冻结LLM；(2) 使用多通道匹配局部n-gram模式并显式跟踪占用率，作为轻量级选择性先验，使残差注入偏向域内输入；(3) 通过学习标量门控抑制不连贯的OOD检索。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上，以AG-News和MedMCQA为适应任务、多种推理/翻译/代码/法律推理基准为OOD评测，Engram Adapter提升域内精度并保留99.4%-100.1%的平均OOD性能；在LegalBench上平均略超冻结基座模型；门控与投影衰减将OOD残差压缩至隐藏状态范数的约0.08%，KL漂移极小。

Conclusion: 条件激活是通向模块化、保留记忆的领域特化的一条有前景的技术路线，基于冻结骨干网络可实现域内性能提升与OOD性能保留的双重目标。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in specialized domains must improve in-domain performance without sacrificing general capabilities. Existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods are typically always on: their learned perturbations are applied to every input, which can degrade out-of-domain (OOD) performance. We propose Engram Adapter, a framework that repurposes pretraining-time conditional memory as a post-hoc adapter for frozen LLMs. It uses multi-channel matching over local n-gram patterns with explicit occupancy tracking as a lightweight selectivity prior, making residual injection more likely on in-domain inputs while a learned scalar gate suppresses incoherent OOD retrievals. We evaluate on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B with AG-News and MedMCQA as adaptation tasks and OOD benchmarks spanning reasoning, translation, code generation, and legal reasoning. Engram Adapter improves in-domain accuracy while preserving 99.4%--100.1% of average OOD performance; on LegalBench it slightly exceeds the frozen base model on average, whereas comparable always-on baselines degrade sharply. Mechanistic analyses show that although OOD activations are non-zero, gate and projection attenuation reduce residuals to approximately 0.08% of hidden-state norm, yielding small KL drift and negligible accuracy change. These results suggest conditional activation is a promising route toward modular, retention-preserving domain specialization over frozen backbones.

</details>


### [128] [Arabic Safety Alignment as Selective Refusal: An Empirical Study of SFT, DPO, and Guard Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29378)
*Mohamad Zbib,Ammar Mohanna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对阿拉伯语大语言模型的拒绝行为，提出用良性拒绝率B和有害提示拒绝率H两个维度进行评估，避免单一拒绝率掩盖的权衡。在五个阿拉伯语模型和130次完整AraSafe集运行中，仅拒绝SFT会导致全盘拒绝，而选定的混合SFT配置可在B=14%-23%时达到H=90%-93%；DPO和推理防护对不同模型影响不同；人工盲审显示二元拒绝一致性89.0%（kappa=0.78）。结论支持按模型设定部署目标并仅保留能改善目标的干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅使用单一拒绝率，无法揭示"过拒绝良性提示"与"拒绝有害提示"之间的权衡，导致模型在实际部署中可能过度拒绝或危险顺从。

Method: 提出双指标评估框架（良性拒绝率B和有害提示拒绝率H）；跨5个阿拉伯语模型、130次运行，对完整人工编写的AraSafe数据集评估仅拒绝SFT、混合SFT、DPO和推理防护等干预手段；并开展300条响应的盲审评估标注一致性及第三方守卫模型准确率。

Result: 仅拒绝SFT导致全盘拒绝；选定混合SFT配置在B=14%-23%时H达90%-93%，其中4个配置在所有3次运行中超过H=90%目标，Fanar在2/3运行中达标。DPO和推理防护非统一升级。人工二元拒绝一致性89.0%（kappa=0.78）；Qwen3Guard和Aya Expanse 32B准确率分别为88.7%和91.0%。SFT对Arabizi仅部分迁移，所有模型均未达90%。

Conclusion: 应基于模型具体的操作点（operating point）进行选择：设定部署目标，仅保留能改善该目标的干预措施。同时表明从现代标准阿拉伯语到Arabizi的拒绝行为迁移是部分性的。

Abstract: Arabic large language models must refuse harmful prompts without over-refusing benign or sensitive prompts, yet a single refusal rate hides this trade-off. We evaluate it using benign refusal B and harmful-prompt refusal H, where H measures refusal rather than harmful compliance. Across five Arabic-capable models and 130 runs on the full human-written AraSafe set, refusal-only supervised fine-tuning (SFT) collapses toward blanket refusal, whereas selected mixed-SFT configurations reach H = 90% to 93% at B = 14% to 23%; four selected configurations exceed the H = 90% target in all three runs, while Fanar does so in two of three. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and inference guards change B and H differently across models rather than acting as uniform upgrades. In a blinded 300-response audit, annotator binary-refusal agreement is 89.0% (kappa = 0.78); Qwen3Guard and Aya Expanse 32B reach 88.7% and 91.0% accuracy, respectively, with no conclusive paired difference. Selected SFT raises H on Arabizi for all five models, but none reaches 90%, showing only partial transfer from Modern Standard Arabic. Overall, the results support model-specific operating-point selection: set a deployment target and retain only interventions that improve it.

</details>


### [129] [AlgoWorlds: Benchmarking Tool Use for Global Optimization in Algorithmic Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29397)
*Zixiang Xu,Jiaan Wang,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlgoWorlds是一个新的基准测试，将组合优化问题转化为部分可观测的决策环境，用于评估LLM代理能否将工具收集的信息转化为全局最优决策。240个环境覆盖10个组合优化族和4个工作负载级别，实验表明即使领先模型在大部分情况下能产生可行决策，最佳模型的精确最优率仅为38.61%，揭示挑战不仅在于信息获取，更在于信息整合、全局约束推理和决策验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用基准仅评估代理能否完成可行的工作流程（使用合适的工具和有效参数），但在路线规划、车队调度等真实决策场景中，个体选择通过共享约束和成本相互影响，可行解可能仍然高度次优。因此需要一个更高的标准：评估代理能否将工具获取的信息转化为全局最优的决策。

Method: 设计AlgoWorlds基准：将形式化指定的组合优化问题转化为部分可观测的决策环境。每个环境包含一个隐藏实例，仅通过任务特定的信息工具可观测，代理提交结构化决策后按可行性和最优性评估。使用族特定的确定性程序生成实例，精确算法证实最优解并确定工作负载级别，两个结构不同的工具接口呈现每个底层实例。共240个环境，覆盖10个组合优化族和4个工作负载级别。

Result: 评估了7个领先LLM（包括Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.6 Sol）。全局最优性极具挑战：领先模型在大多数情况下能产生可行决策，但最佳模型仅在38.61%的情况下达到精确最优。即使代理收集了足够的信息来重建隐藏实例，大多数失败仍以可行但次优的决策告终。

Conclusion: 该挑战超越信息获取，延伸到信息整合、全局约束推理和决策验证层面。AlgoWorlds提供了一个可验证的基准平台，用于系统评估和推进LLM代理在真实复杂决策场景中的全局最优决策能力。

Abstract: Tool-use benchmarks generally evaluate whether an agent completes a workflow using appropriate tools and valid arguments. However, feasibility alone is insufficient in real-world decision settings such as route planning and fleet dispatch. Individual choices interact through shared constraints and costs, so a feasible solution may still be substantially suboptimal. This raises a harder question: can an agent turn information gathered through tools into a globally optimal decision? We introduce AlgoWorlds, a benchmark that transforms formally specified combinatorial optimization problems into partially observed decision environments with verifiable global optima. Each environment contains a hidden instance observed only through task-specific information tools, after which the agent commits to one structured decision evaluated for feasibility and optimality. AlgoWorlds contains 240 environments covering ten combinatorial optimization families and four workload levels. Family-specific deterministic programs generate the instances, exact algorithms certify their optima and determine workload levels, and two structurally different tool interfaces present each underlying instance. We evaluate seven leading LLMs, including Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5.6 Sol. Achieving global optimality remains highly challenging: although leading models produce feasible decisions in most cases, the best-performing model reaches exact optimality in only 38.61% of cases. Even when agents collect sufficient information to reconstruct the hidden instance, most failures end in feasible but suboptimal decisions. The challenge therefore extends beyond information acquisition to information integration, global constraint reasoning, and decision verification. The project homepage is available at https://xzx34.github.io/AlgoWorlds/, and the code is available at https://github.com/xzx34/AlgoWorlds.

</details>


### [130] [Evaluating the Semantic Specificity of Representation Steering in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29431)
*Zhangdie Yuan,Andreas Vlachos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-Rule Transfer (CRT)诊断框架，用于审计大型语言模型中的局部表示引导（LRS）干预是否真正修复了推理电路，还是仅仅注入了表面的标签偏差。实验表明，针对矛盾盲视这一逻辑缺陷的LRS干预实际上只是注入全局标签偏置——将引导向量应用于模型已能正确处理（99.6%基线）的规则时，性能降至40.4%，说明干预是表面标签覆盖而非真正的推理修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部表示引导（LRS）被广泛用于修复大语言模型的推理病理问题，但标准基准评估容易被表面的标签覆盖所欺骗，造成推理电路得到修复的假象。因此需要一种更严格的诊断方法来区分真正的推理修复与表面的标签偏向。

Method: 提出Cross-Rule Transfer (CRT)诊断框架，通过在模型原本擅长的规则族上评估表示干预来判断其是否真正修复了推理电路，并辅以四种互补对照实验：直接logit偏置等价性、控制向量标签翻转、跨模型嫁接以及早期层引导检查。

Result: 针对矛盾盲视的晚期层LRS干预揭示了干预仅注入了全局标签偏置：将引导向量应用于模型已正确处理的规则（99.6%基线）会导致性能骤降至40.4%，因为强迫模型做出错误的矛盾预测。四种对照实验进一步支持了这一诊断结论。

Conclusion: LRS干预在矛盾盲视上的效果并非真正的推理修复，而是表面的标签覆盖。CRT提供了一套严格的方法论，能够可靠地区分真实的推理修复与表面的标签覆盖，为评估表示干预的有效性提供了更严谨的基准。

Abstract: Localized Representation Steering (LRS) is widely used to correct reasoning pathologies in large language models. However, standard benchmark evaluations can easily be fooled by superficial label overrides, creating a false impression of reasoning circuit repairs. In this work, we propose Cross-Rule Transfer (CRT), a diagnostic framework that audits representational interventions by evaluating them on rule families where the model is natively competent. Evaluating late-layer LRS for a widespread logical failure, contradiction blindness, reveals that the intervention merely injects a global label bias: applying the steering vector to rules the model already handles correctly (99.6% baseline) degrades performance to 40.4% by forcing false contradiction predictions. We support this diagnosis with four complementary controls (direct logit bias equivalence, control vector label-flipping, cross-model grafting, and early-layer steering checks), providing a rigorous methodology to distinguish genuine reasoning repairs from superficial label overrides.

</details>


### [131] [Whose Assessment of Distress? Community Perspectives and LLM Alignment on Well-Being Posts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29446)
*Andrew Aquilina,Xiang Lorraine Li,Yu-Ru Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过视角主义标注方法，收集321名参与者对1,198条Reddit帖子的9,587个心理健康判断，评估大语言模型（LLM）在检测心理困扰时是否能捕捉不同社群的视角。结果显示，开权重LLM系统性地高估心理困扰，在社群认为无至轻度困扰的情况下准确率仅31-44%，主要产生假阳性；GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Pro也存在同样的轻度困扰膨胀问题，而Claude Opus 4则更为保守。这种偏差并非简单的"局外人"视角问题，而是模型具有超出人类判断的"困扰先验"，对心理健康AI的公平部署构成威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理困扰的判断本质上是社会情境化的，不同社群的规范（如情绪表达、脆弱性、求助意愿）会影响什么被视为值得关注的困扰。然而，用于困扰检测的LLM通常被对齐到一个单一、无差别的标准，这引发了一个关键问题：这些模型能否捕捉被评估社群的真实视角？现有研究缺乏对LLM在心理困扰检测中是否反映多元社群视角的系统性实证评估。

Method: 本研究采用视角主义标注框架：(1) 招募321名参与者对源自六个身份社群的1,198条Reddit帖子提供9,587个判断，生成社群特定标签；(2) 将标注者分为情境化内组（in-group）和非情境化外组（out-group）条件，比较两种条件下标注者与社群标签的一致性；(3) 评估九种开权重LLM配置和四种前沿模型配置（GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude Opus 4等）的困扰检测表现，并将其与人类判断进行系统性对比。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 情境化内组标注者比非情境化外组标注者更倾向于与社群判断一致（OR=1.18），但效应在不同社群间差异显著；(2) 开权重LLM系统性地高估困扰，在社群认为无至轻度困扰时准确率仅31-44%，以假阳性为主；(3) GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Pro即使整体过估/低估率混合，仍存在无至轻度困扰的膨胀问题，而Claude Opus 4更为保守；(4) 该偏差不能简单归因于局外人视角——非情境化外组人类的总体验证几乎对称（18%过估对比19%低估），而膨胀困扰的模型展现出超过内组和外组人类判断的"困扰先验"。

Conclusion: LLM在心理困扰检测中普遍存在系统性的高估偏差，这种偏差源于模型内部的"困扰先验"而非简单的局外人视角，且对不同社群的影响不均匀。这为心理健康领域的AI公平部署敲响了警钟：校准不良的困扰检测可能对不同社群产生不平等的负面影响，需要在模型对齐和部署中纳入社群特定视角。

Abstract: Judgments about psychological distress are socially situated: what counts as concerning hinges on community norms around emotional expression, vulnerability, and help-seeking. Yet large language models (LLMs) used for distress detection are typically aligned to a single, undifferentiated standard. How well do these models capture the perspectives of the communities whose language they assess? We address this question through a perspectivist annotation study in which 321 participants provided 9,587 judgments on 1,198 Reddit posts spanning six identity-based communities, yielding community-specific labels. Raters in the contextualized in-group condition show a modest tendency to agree more with their community than uncontextualized out-group raters (OR = 1.18), an effect varying significantly across communities. We then evaluate nine open-weight LLM configurations and four frontier configurations against these labels. Open-weight LLMs systematically over-estimate distress: when communities perceive none-to-mild distress, these models achieve only 31-44% accuracy, predominantly producing false positives. GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Pro show the same none-to-mild inflation even when their full-sample over/under rates are mixed, while Claude Opus 4 is more conservative. This pattern does not simply mirror an outsider reading position: uncontextualized out-group human aggregates were nearly symmetric, with 18% over-estimation versus 19% under-estimation. Instead, the models that inflate none-to-mild cases exhibit a distress prior that exceeds both contextualized in-group and uncontextualized out-group human judgments. These findings have implications for equitable AI deployment in mental health contexts, where miscalibrated distress detection may unevenly affect the communities being assessed.

</details>


### [132] [AI Can Be Easily Persuaded in Clinical Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29453)
*Jiayuan Zhu,Jiazhen Pan,Fenglin Liu,Minhao Hu,Junde Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验发现AI在医疗决策中极易被说服：专业权威、国家背景、机构隶属、声称的历史绩效、多位医生、支持的临床观点及重复施压均能影响AI决策；资深临床医生比医学生更具说服力（约多改变10%的案例）；甚至伪造的临床观点也能将AI从正确决策引离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI日益融入临床实践并在医疗决策中扮演越来越重要的角色，而医学是高风险、循证的领域，决策直接影响患者生命，因此有必要探究AI在他人试图说服时能否保持客观判断。

Method: 通过受控实验系统性地研究AI被说服的容易程度，操纵多种说服因素（包括来源的专业权威、国家背景、机构隶属、声称的历史绩效、多位医生、支持的临床观点以及重复施压）来测试其对AI决策的影响。

Result: 研究发现：①专业权威、国家背景、机构隶属、声称的历史绩效、多位医生、支持的临床观点和重复压力均能影响AI决策；②同样的说服输入来自资深临床医生比来自医学生多改变约10%的案例；③仅声称更好的历史绩效就能持续增强医生说服力；④更显著的是，即使是捏造来支持错误答案的合理临床观点，也能将AI从正确决策中说服偏离。

Conclusion: AI极易被人所说的内容、说者的身份以及观点的呈现方式所影响，且无法可靠判断临床观点是否正确。因此，必须确保AI在说服压力下维持健全的判断，以保证其在高风险医疗决策中的安全可靠使用。

Abstract: As AI becomes increasingly integrated into clinical practice, it is playing a growing role in medical decision making. Medicine, however, is a high stakes and evidence based field, where decisions can directly affect patients' lives. It is therefore important to understand whether AI can maintain objective judgment when others try to persuade it. In this paper, we study how easily AI can be persuaded through controlled experiments. We find that professional authority, national background, institutional affiliation, claimed past performance, multiple physicians, supported clinician views, and repeated pressure can all affect AI decisions. Surprisingly, the same persuasive input changes about 10% more cases when it comes from a senior clinician than from a medical student. Simply claiming a better performance history consistently makes the physician more persuasive. More strikingly, a plausible clinician view can persuade AI away from a correct decision even when it is fabricated to support an incorrect answer. This indicates that AI can be strongly influenced by convincing support without reliably determining whether this view from the clinician is correct. Together, these findings suggest that AI can be easily persuaded by what people say, who says it, and how the opinion is presented. Therefore, it is essential for AI to maintain sound judgment under persuasion, enabling its safe and reliable use in high stakes medical decision making.

</details>


### [133] [Item-Mean Surrogates: Why Richer Persona Data Fail to Improve LLMs as Human Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29455)
*Daehwan Ahn,Chengfeng Mao,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在聚合层面能较好地拟合人类平均回答，但仅能解释剔除项目均值后3.05%的个体变异（人类重测基准为53.6%）；丰富画像、模型变体与微调均无法缩小这一差距。方差分析表明，剔除项目均值后的稳定信号是"人×项目"交互（约为稳定人效应的8.9倍），而画像数据编码了受访者但未编码这种项目特定的偏离。此外，LLM输出压缩了人类反应分布（更少的离散度、更少的反应类别、扭曲的分布形态）。作者将这种现象称为"项目均值替代"，并提出四项针对LLM人类替代主张的实证检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM作为人类替代物的前提是否成立——即更丰富的人物画像数据能否使LLM成为特定个体的替代品或探索工具。现有研究大多在聚合层面宣称LLM与人类行为一致，缺乏对个体层面预测能力的系统检验。

Method: 基于四个数据集（覆盖超过40万名参与者、超过6,000个调查题目和实验结果）进行系统性评估。通过剔除项目均值后的残差分析衡量LLM对受访者特定变异的解释力，并与人类重测信度（53.6%）对比；同时进行方差分解来分离稳定人效应与人×项目交互效应；还考察了LLM输出的分布形态特征（离散度、反应类别使用、分布形状）。

Result: （1）LLM在聚合层面表现良好，平均响应与人类平均高度一致；（2）但剔除项目均值后，LLM仅解释3.05%的受访者特定变异，远低于人类53.6%的重测基准；（3）丰富画像、不同模型变体和微调均无法缩小该差距；（4）方差分析显示剔除项目均值后，人×项目交互信号约是稳定人效应的8.9倍，而画像数据未能编码这种项目特定偏离；（5）LLM输出压缩了人类反应分布，使用更少的离散度和反应类别，并扭曲了分布形态。

Conclusion: 当前LLM人类替代模型只能近似项目平均值，无法替代个体层面的分布或受访者特定的偏差。作者总结出"项目均值替代"模式，并提出四项实证检验用于评估未来的LLM人类替代主张，为LLM作为人类替代物的使用设定更严格的标准。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used as human surrogates, often on the premise that richer persona data could make them substitutes or exploratory tools for specific individuals. We test this premise across four datasets covering more than 400,000 participants and more than 6,000 survey items and experimental outcomes. LLMs perform well at the aggregate level: their average responses closely align with average human responses to the same items. But this success largely reflects predicting each item's average human response. Once each item's human mean is removed, LLM predictions explain only 3.05% of the remaining respondent-specific variation, far below the 53.6% human test-retest benchmark. Richer personas, model variants, and fine-tuning do not close this gap. In variance analyses, once item means are removed, the reliable remaining signal is person-by-item. It captures how a respondent departs from the mean on a particular item and is about 8.9x larger than the stable person effect. Persona data encode the respondent, but not this item-specific deviation. LLM responses also compress human response distributions, using less spread, fewer response categories, and distorted distributional shapes. We call this pattern item-mean surrogacy. Current LLM surrogates can approximate item averages, but not the distributions or respondent-specific deviations needed to replace individual humans. We propose four empirical tests for LLM-based human-surrogate claims.

</details>


### [134] [Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness of Reasoning Models Varies with Where and How Preference Cues Are Delivered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29464)
*Aryo Pradipta Gema,Neel Rajani,Rohit Saxena,Wai-Chung Kwan,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FACE-Eval是一个包含5,100个样本的评估基准，用于测试思维链（CoT）监控在偏好信息通过工具返回或原始工件呈现时的可靠性。评估了15个开源模型（4B-1.60T参数），发现所有模型在工具返回线索上的口头承诺度更低、未口头化采纳度更高，且隐式线索加剧了这一问题。两个转录监控器的大模型检测能力与未口头化采纳呈负相关（r=-0.54和r=-0.78），表明当信息通过工具或需要从原始工件推断时，CoT监控的可靠性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT监控的忠实性测试通常在用户消息中放置明确偏差线索，但实际智能体可能通过工具返回或原始工件（raw artifacts）获得偏好信息。这产生了关键研究空白：CoT监控在信息通过非用户消息渠道呈现时是否仍然可靠？该论文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估CoT在不同线索位置和显式程度下的忠实性。

Method: 构建FACE-Eval基准（5,100个样本），操纵两个维度：线索位置（用户消息 vs 工具返回）和显式性（直接摘要 vs 原始工件）。测量两类指标：口头化承诺（cue-following答案中明确表达的承诺）和未口头化采纳（所有线索样本中的隐含采纳）。评估15个开源模型（8个模型家族，4B-1.60T参数），并使用源归因提示和监控告知作为干预，同时用两个转录监控器（GPT-5.6-Luna和GPT-4o-mini）检测最大模型中的偏好采纳。

Result: 所有15个模型的工具返回线索口头承诺度低于用户消息线索，隐式线索低于显式线索。未口头化采纳在全部15个模型上工具返回线索更高，在30个模型-通道比较中有28个隐式线索更高。源归因提示仅在7个模型上缩小通道差距（有时通过增加用户通道的未口头化采纳），监控告知无法可靠缩小差距。两个监控器的检测能力均与未口头化采纳呈显著负相关（r=-0.54和r=-0.78）。

Conclusion: CoT监控的可靠性依赖于推理轨迹忠实记录信息塑造过程这一假设，但该研究证明当偏好信息通过工具返回或须从原始工件推断时，模型倾向于不口头化采纳这些信息，使监控更难检测。即使在单次调用、预填充工具设置下，这也对当前CoT监控范式的有效性提出了重要质疑，预示着在更复杂的多轮工具使用场景中监控挑战可能更加严峻。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring assumes that reasoning traces faithfully record the information that shapes a model's answer. Existing faithfulness tests often place explicit bias cues in the user message, while agents may encounter preferences through tool returns or raw artifacts. We introduce FACE-Eval (Faithful Attribution of Cue Effects Evaluation), a 5,100-sample evaluation that varies cue location (user message or tool return) and explicitness (direct summary or raw artifact). We measure verbalized commitment among cue-following answers and unverbalized adoption among all cued samples. We evaluate 15 open-weight models from eight families, with total parameters ranging from 4B to 1.60T. Every model has lower verbalized commitment for tool-return than user-message cues and for implicit than explicit cues. Unverbalized adoption is higher for tool-return cues on all 15 models and for implicit cues in 28 of 30 model-channel comparisons. A source-attribution prompt narrows the channel gap on seven models, sometimes by increasing user-channel unverbalized adoption, while telling models that their reasoning will be monitored does not reliably close the gap. We also use two transcript monitors (GPT-5.6-Luna and GPT-4o-mini) to detect preference adoption in the largest model of each family. Across 32 model-channel-explicitness cells, higher unverbalized adoption is associated with lower detection ability for both monitors (Pearson r=-0.54 and r=-0.78, respectively). These results suggest that CoT monitoring may be less reliable when preference information arrives through tools or must be inferred from raw artifacts, within the single-call, prefilled-tool setting tested here.

</details>


### [135] [MUDDLE: Measuring Understanding of Documents under Distractor and Length Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29477)
*Jason Luo,Saibilila Abudukelimu,Judy Song,Andrew Feng,Shivank Garg,Vasu Sharma,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL
