Result: 在三个模型和三种推理模式下，首轮生成在47.6-85.7%的测试用例中至少违反一项上限（汇总67.2%），但凸投影层将最终可行性违规降至0%，同时仅需很小的修正距离（测试汇总中位数D=||w*-w0||_2=0.066），表明护栏通常保留原始分配意图。

Conclusion: 凸二次规划投影层作为后生成护栏能够有效消除LLM投资组合生成中的KYC式违规，同时最小化对原始分配意图的偏离。作者公开发布数据集、提示、上限和代码以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate plausible-sounding ETF portfolios while silently violating basic KYC-style constraints on risk, fees, and diversification. This is especially problematic in agentic multi-turn advisory systems, where each draft recommendation can become an action unless guarded by an auditable enforcement layer. We study a model-agnostic, asset-agnostic post-generation guardrail pipeline: (i) enforce a strict JSON allocation schema, (ii) validate allocations against numeric caps, and (iii) when violations occur, deterministically project the output to the nearest feasible portfolio via a convex quadratic program (QCQP). We introduce BiasMix-Finance (Mini), a compact stress-test benchmark for constrained decision-making under biased LLM generations, with a 16-ETF universe, three investor profiles, and eight bias prompts. Across three models and three inference modes (direct, critique, self-consistency), first-pass generations violate at least one cap in 47.6-85.7% of test cases (67.2% pooled), but the convex projection layer reduces final feasibility violations to 0% while requiring only a small correction distance (test pooled median D=||w*-w0||_2=0.066), indicating that the guardrail typically preserves the intent of the original allocation. We report violation rates and correction distances with confidence intervals, and paired model comparisons with multiple-testing correction. To support reproducibility, we release the dataset, prompts, caps, and code in our public GitHub repository.

</details>


### [167] [Self-Specialized Teachers for Domain Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28647)
*Yifei Li,Rongman Xu,Lingling Zhang,Muye Huang,Zihan Ma,Jiashuai Liu,Hang Yan,Heng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SSTD（Self-Specialized Teacher Distillation）方法，解决仅使用目标领域数据微调时导致通用能力退化的问题。该方法分两阶段：先将基础模型副本训练为领域教师（结合标准目标监督、基于基础模型的键词加权和分布对齐），再将教师的token分布蒸馏到学生模型（使用学生自身采样的前缀进行on-policy蒸馏）。在金融数值推理、医学问答和法律判决识别三个领域，SSTD在保持直接微调大部分领域提升的同时，将通用套件平均得分提升4.8-5.0点，且不依赖外部教师或通用回放数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅使用目标领域数据进行post-training可以提升专门领域性能，但往往损害基础模型在适应前已获得的通用能力。当目标领域数据可得但缺少代表性回放语料时，如何在不牺牲通用能力的前提下实现领域专业化是一个关键挑战。

Method: SSTD采用两阶段流程：(1) 领域教师训练：复制基础模型，结合标准目标监督、base-aware key-token加权以及向冻结基础模型的分布对齐来训练领域教师；(2) 自专业化蒸馏：将教师模型的token分布蒸馏给学生模型，蒸馏前缀由学生自身采样，从而将领域反馈放置在推理时学生实际可能遇到的状态上（on-policy蒸馏）。该方法无需外部教师模型，也无需通用回放数据。

Result: 在金融数值推理、医学问答和法律持有识别三个领域任务上，SSTD保留了直接微调的大部分领域性能提升，同时在评估的通用套件上将平均得分提升4.8-5.0分。该模式在Qwen3不同规模和Gemma骨干网络上均保持一致表现。

Conclusion: SSTD通过自专业化教师蒸馏，在无需外部教师和通用回放数据的情况下，有效地缓解了目标域后训练导致的通用能力退化问题，实现了领域专业化与通用能力保持之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: Target-only post-training can improve performance in a specialized domain while degrading behaviors that a general-purpose base model acquired before adaptation. We study this problem when target-domain data are available but a representative replay corpus is not. We propose self-specialized teacher distillation (SSTD), a two-stage procedure that first trains a copy of the base model into a domain teacher, then distills its token distribution to a student on prefixes sampled from the student itself. Teacher training combines standard target supervision with base-aware key-token weighting and distribution alignment to the frozen base model; on-policy distillation then places domain feedback on states the student can encounter at inference time. On financial numerical reasoning, medical question answering, and legal holding identification, SSTD retains much of the target improvement of direct fine-tuning while improving the mean score on the evaluated general suite by 4.8--5.0 points at the reported operating point. The pattern persists across Qwen3 sizes and on Gemma backbones. SSTD requires neither an external teacher nor general replay data.

</details>


### [168] [How Language Models Choose Sides: Internal Representations of Instruction Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28648)
*Enrique Balp-Straffon,Chih-Hao Hsu,Rushiraj Gadhvi,Sunishchal Dev,Callum Stuart McDougall,Anusha Mujumdar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了指令微调LLM如何在系统指令与用户指令直接冲突时进行仲裁。作者构建了包含41个配对约束的基准测试，配备确定性验证器，并评估了八种模型在匹配基线、冲突和同通道控制条件下的表现。行为上，模型按系统权威差异分为三类：层级尊重型、反层级型和无效应型。Llama-3.1-8B是最强的反层级型案例，在冲突试验中仅0.10的比例遵循系统。作者通过该行为失败案例探究用户偏好仲裁是否反映内部冲突解决信号缺失，发现并非如此：在Llama-3.1-8B上，冲突结果可从残差流激活中以0.97的平衡准确率线性解码，高于仅元数据基线的17个百分点。通过第12层四个冲突逻辑回归方向的均值进行引导，可将真实系统遵从度从0.132提升至0.530，而主要基于池化可分离性选择的引导效果不佳。用户偏好的冲突解决可以与可读的内部仲裁信号共存，成功干预取决于读出的几何特性而非仅探针准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解指令调优LLM如何在系统指令与用户指令冲突时进行仲裁，探究模型内部是否存在可读的冲突解决信号，以及这些信号能否被有效干预以提升系统遵从度。

Method: 构建41个配对约束基准（配备确定性验证器），在匹配基线、冲突和同通道控制三种条件下评估八种模型。通过残差流激活的线性解码探测内部仲裁信号，并利用逻辑回归方向的引导（steering）干预模型行为，比较源自每冲突方向与池化可分离性方向的效果差异。

Result: 模型分为三类行为模式（层级尊重型、反层级型、无效应型）。Llama-3.1-8B为最强反层级模型（系统遵从率仅0.10），但其冲突结果可从残差激活中以0.97的平衡准确率线性解码。第12层四个每冲突方向均值引导可将系统遵从率从0.132提升至0.530，而池化可分离性方向引导效果差。

Conclusion: 用户偏好的冲突解决可以与可读的内部仲裁信号共存；干预成功与否取决于读出的几何特性（每冲突方向）而非探针准确率本身。这为理解LLM层级遵循行为及干预策略提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study how instruction-tuned LLMs arbitrate direct conflicts between system and user instructions. We introduce a benchmark of 41 paired constraints with deterministic verifiers and evaluate eight models under matched baseline, conflict, and same-channel control conditions. Behaviourally, the models split into three regimes by System Authority Delta: hierarchy-respecting models use the system channel as an authority signal, anti-hierarchy models follow the system less often than their same-channel baseline predicts, and no-effect models show little channel sensitivity. Llama-3.1-8B is the strongest anti-hierarchy case in our suite, following the system in only 0.10 of conflict trials. We use this behavioural failure case to ask whether user-preferring arbitration reflects the absence of an internal conflictresolution signal. It does not: on Llama-3.1-8B, the conflict outcome is linearly decodable from residual-stream activations at 0.97 balanced accuracy, 17 percentage points above a metadata-only baseline, with analogous signals on Qwen2.5-7B and gpt-oss-20b. Steering with a layer-12 mean of four per-conflict logistic-regression directions raises genuine system compliance from 0.132 to 0.530, while directions selected mainly for pooled separability steer poorly. User-preferring conflict resolution can therefore coexist with a readable internal arbitration signal, and successful intervention depends on the geometry of the readout rather than probe accuracy alone

</details>


### [169] [A Generalized Optimization Engine (GOE) for Edge AI Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28652)
*Venkat R. Dasari,Jakob A. Adams,Vinod K. Mishra,Brian Jalaian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了各类AI模型优化技术的理论基础，并提出了一种硬件和模型无关的通用优化架构（GOE），该架构集成多种优化技术以提升资源受限环境下的推理效率。实验证明，GOE压缩后的语言模型可在无GPU的边缘CPU上部署运行，且压缩方法的选择（而非仅标称位宽）决定了任务精度能否在部署后得以保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型虽在众多领域表现卓越，但其高昂的计算成本严重阻碍了在资源受限设备上的广泛部署。现有优化方法往往针对特定硬件或模型定制，缺乏通用性，难以适应异构硬件环境（如战术场景）。因此，亟需一种统一的、硬件和模型无关的优化架构来系统性地降低计算复杂度、内存占用、延迟和功耗。

Method: 本文首先从理论层面梳理了各类AI模型优化技术、算法和抽象，分析其降低计算复杂度和资源消耗的潜力；在此基础上，提出了一种硬件（HW）和模型无关的通用优化架构（Generalized Optimization Architecture，GOE），该系统集成多种优化技术以协同提升推理效率。

Result: 实证结果表明，经过GOE压缩的语言模型能够在无GPU的边缘CPU上成功部署并运行；同时发现，压缩方法的选择（而非仅仅压缩位宽的大小）才是决定模型任务精度在部署后能否保持的关键因素。

Conclusion: 研究强调了通用优化系统在资源受限异构硬件（尤其是战术环境）中部署模型的关键作用，并揭示了一个重要洞见：压缩方法本身的特性比名义位宽更能影响部署后的任务精度，这为实际部署中的优化策略选择提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across various domains, yet their widespread deployment is impeded by significant computational costs, particularly on resource-constrained devices. This paper explores the theoretical underpinnings of various AI model optimization techniques, algorithms, and abstractions, discussing their potential to reduce computational complexity, memory footprint, latency, and power consumption. Furthermore, we propose a comprehensive hardware (HW) and model-agnostic generalized optimization architecture that integrates these techniques for improved efficiency. Our study underscores the critical role of such a generalized optimization system in preparing model deployment over resource-constrained heterogeneous hardware in a tactical environment. As a concrete demonstration, we show that GOE-compressed language models deploy and run on a GPU-less edge CPU, and that the choice of compression method, not merely its nominal bit-width, determines whether task accuracy survives deployment.

</details>


### [170] [FRAC-MAS: A Safe and Explainable Multi-Agent System for Fracture Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28662)
*Hardik Iyer,Tirath Bhathawala,Mihir Panchal,Ying-Jung Chen,Kiran Bhowmick,Pankaj Sonawane,Meera Narvekar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FRAC-MAS是一个基于代理型AI架构的骨折检测系统，结合四个视觉模型的堆叠集成与共形预测，通过多代理工作流进行独立验证、临床指南检索和患者友好报告生成，实现可解释、安全且保留临床监督的放射学分诊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在骨折诊断中虽性能出色，但其黑箱特性限制了临床采纳。现有方法缺乏统计保障的置信评估和可审计的决策流程，亟需在保持高性能的同时提供可解释性、安全性以及人在环路的监督机制。

Method: FRAC-MAS框架由三部分组成：(1) 堆叠集成四个视觉模型进行骨折检测；(2) 共形预测提供统计上有依据的鉴别诊断；(3) 多代理工作流执行独立验证（critic代理）、检索临床指南并生成患者友好报告。通过流水线深度消融实验对比单代理基线的性能。

Result: 多代理critic将86.6%的病例分诊至高置信度的自动确认队列，同时将不确定病例升级处理，优于单代理基线。患者偏好研究显示FRAC-MAS生成的临床报告比Llama、MedGemma和Gemini显著更易理解。

Conclusion: 将多代理critic与共形预测保证相结合，可在保留临床医生监督的同时实现更安全的放射学分诊。合作式代理架构可作为安全关键医疗场景中可审计、人在环路的决策支持系统。

Abstract: Fracture detection and its clinical interpretability see notable improvements when deep vision models are integrated with agentic AI architectures. While deep learning models achieve high diagnostic performance, their black-box nature limits clinical adoption. We propose FRAC-MAS, an agentic AI system for automated, explainable, and safe bone fracture detection. The framework combines a stacked ensemble of four vision models with conformal prediction to produce statistically grounded differential diagnoses, while a multi-agent workflow performs independent verification, retrieves clinical guidelines, and generates patient-friendly reports. A pipeline-depth ablation study confirms that our multi-agent critic triages 86.6% of cases into a high-confidence auto-confirmed cohort while escalating uncertain cases, outperforming a single-agent baseline. Patient preference studies against Llama, MedGemma, and Gemini further demonstrate significantly more comprehensible clinical reports. These results suggest that integrating multi-agent critics with conformal guarantees enables safer radiology triage while preserving clinician oversight. More broadly, FRAC-MAS demonstrates how cooperative agentic architectures can serve as auditable, human-in-the-loop decision support systems for safety-critical healthcare. Our code is available at https://github.com/hardik1712/FRAC-MAS, and the website is available at https://frac-mas.vercel.app.

</details>


### [171] [ORDDAR: Observation-Driven Reasoning for Distortion-Resilient Decision, Action, and Cognitive Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28704)
*Deblina Kar,Anant Nawalgaria,Shyamal Kumar Das Mandal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ORDDAR是一种将推理建模为认知状态转换的推理框架，通过检测局部扭曲、从先前经验中检索相关推理并仅修复受影响的推理状态，在数学、常识、多跳和临床推理基准上优于多个推理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在执行长期推理、规划、工具使用和自主决策时，错误的时间状态会传播并导致不一致的决策和不可靠的输出。现有推理方法主要依赖迭代规划、自我反思、增强记忆或验证，但很少定位和选择性修复有缺陷的推理环节。

Method: ORDDAR将推理建模为认知状态转换过程，包含三个核心步骤：检测局部扭曲、检索先前经验中的相关推理、仅修复受影响的推理状态。该框架在局部推理转换层面进行恢复，而非重新生成完整推理轨迹。

Result: 在数学、常识、多跳和临床推理四个基准上的实验表明，ORDDAR在推理质量、恢复能力和可解释性方面均优于多个评估的推理基线。

Conclusion: 通过局部选择性修复而非全局重新生成，ORDDAR能够有效提升AI代理在复杂推理任务中的可靠性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform long-term reasoning, planning, tool use, memory integration, and autonomous decision making, yet erroneous intermediate states can propagate and cause inconsistent decisions and unreliable outputs. Existing reasoning approaches mainly rely on iterative planning, self-reflection, augmented memory, or verification, but rarely localize and selectively repair faulty reasoning. We present ORDDAR (Observation-Driven Reasoning for Distortion-Resilient Decision, Action, and Cognitive Recovery), a reasoning framework that models reasoning as cognitive state transitions, detects localized distortions, retrieves related reasoning from prior experiences, and repairs only the affected states. ORDDAR therefore performs recovery at the local reasoning-transition level rather than regenerating the complete trajectory. Experiments across mathematical, commonsense, multi-hop, and clinical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate improved reasoning quality, recovery ability, and interpretability over multiple evaluated reasoning baselines.

</details>


### [172] [Beyond the Answer Key: Robustness Evaluation of Large Language Models for Step-Level Mathematical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28725)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个控制变量的线性方程基准，用于评估LLM作为评估者（grader/verifier）的能力。发现即使最先进的开放LLM在评估规范解时表现良好，但在面对被扰动但逻辑等价的解轨迹时显著退化，尤其在错误定位任务上，基座模型对有效扰动轨迹的误拒率高达75.6-85.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数数学评估只关注最终答案的准确性，忽视了LLM作为验证者能否识别非规范但有效的解题轨迹这一关键能力。这可能导致评估结果掩盖模型在过程级验证上的鲁棒性缺陷。

Method: 构建一个线性方程基准，每实例要求模型判断三个任务：最终答案正确性、步骤级轨迹正确性、以及第一个错误步骤的定位。引入对规范解的扰动变体（逻辑等价但形式不同），对GPT-OSS 20B、Qwen3-14B和Phi-4-Reasoning等开放LLM进行评估，并测试监督微调、蒸馏和测试时计算对鲁棒性的影响。

Result: 基座模型在规范轨迹上表现良好，但在扰动轨迹上显著退化，尤其错误定位性能下降严重。有效扰动轨迹上的误拒率达75.6-85.3%，表明模型对规范解题形式具有强敏感性。监督微调、蒸馏和测试时计算在某些设置下提升鲁棒性，但收益因模型而异，且可能以牺牲规范性能为代价。

Conclusion: 即使在具有精确ground truth的简单代数领域，可靠的过程级验证仍然具有挑战性。评估者鲁棒性应与求解器准确性分开测量，因为两者反映不同的能力维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as graders, verifiers, and process auditors, but most mathematical evaluations still emphasize final-answer accuracy. This can obscure whether a model can verify a non-canonical but valid solution trace. We introduce a controlled linear-equation benchmark for evaluating LLMs in the evaluator role. Each instance asks the model to judge final-answer correctness, step-level trace correctness, and the first incorrect step. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art open LLMs reveals a significant robustness gap: models that accurately evaluate canonical solutions often fail when presented with perturbed but logically equivalent variants. Across GPT-OSS 20B, Qwen3-14B, and Phi-4-Reasoning, base models perform well on canonical traces but degrade substantially on perturbed traces, especially for error localization. On valid perturbed traces, base-model false-rejection rates reach 75.6-85.3%, showing strong sensitivity to canonical solution form. Supervised fine-tuning, distillation, and test-time compute improve robustness in some settings, but gains are model dependent and can trade off against canonical performance. The results show that reliable process-level verification remains challenging, and evaluator robustness should be measured separately from solver accuracy, even in a simple algebraic domain with exact ground truth.

</details>


### [173] [Pro-Router: Token-Aware Progressive Model Routing with Adaptive Edge-Cloud Collaboration for Efficient Multimodal LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28726)
*Xinyuan Gui,Shaowen Wang,Sheng Sun,Zijian Wang,Zishu Yu,Zheming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pro-Router提出了一种基于token感知的渐进式模型路由方法，结合轻量级提示预评分器和token感知验证器，实现边缘-云端自适应协作的高效多模态LLM推理，较现有方法路由速度提升10倍以上，端到端吞吐量提升75%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的计算开销巨大，阻碍了实时部署和成本效益。现有模型路由方法要么仅依赖粗粒度的请求级特征进行决策，要么需要额外的一次或多次语言模型前向传递来检查生成的响应，导致生成过程中出现的token级不确定性信号未被利用。

Method: Pro-Router采用两阶段渐进式决策机制：(1) 轻量级prompt预评分器在token生成前快速预筛选，将简单请求路由到小模型；(2) token感知验证器读取小模型生成的每个token的采样概率分布，估计模型对自身输出的置信度，逐请求决定答案直接返回还是升级到云端高精度模型。此外设计了自适应边缘-云服务流水线，根据每个设备的实测服务速率动态调整调度，无需手动参数调优且不受网络延迟影响。

Result: 在多个多模态基准数据集和模型上的大量实验表明，Pro-Router相较于其他方法实现了最高的路由准确率，路由速度提升超过10倍；服务流水线相比现有模型路由流水线实现了超过75%的端到端吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: Pro-Router通过利用生成过程中的token级不确定性信号进行渐进式模型路由，结合自适应边缘-云协作调度，在路由准确率、速度和系统吞吐量方面均显著优于现有方法，为高效多模态LLM推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The remarkable performance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) comes at the cost of substantial computational overhead, posing significant challenges to real-time deployment and cost effectiveness. Existing model routing approaches either decide from coarse request-level features alone or spend one or several extra language model passes to inspect the generated response, leaving the token-level uncertainty signals that emerge during generation unused. To address these limitations, we propose Pro-Router, a token-aware progressive model routing method with adaptive edge-cloud collaboration for efficient multimodal LLM inference. Pro-Router employs a two-stage progressive decision mechanism. First, a lightweight prompt pre-scorer module performs rapid pre-screening before token generation begins, guiding apparently simple requests to small models. Second, a token-aware verifier reads the sampling probability distribution of each token the small model generates, estimating the model's confidence in its own output to determine, per request, whether the answer ships or escalates to the cloud-based high-precision model. Furthermore, we design an adaptive edge-cloud serving pipeline that sizes every dispatch to each device's measured service rate, so both the edge and the cloud tiers stay fully utilized without manual parameter tuning and are not impacted by the network latency. Extensive experiments on multiple multimodal benchmark datasets and models demonstrate the effectiveness of Pro-Router. Compared to other methods, it achieves the highest routing accuracy and improves routing speed by more than 10x. Its serving pipeline also reaches more than 75% higher end-to-end throughput than the existing model routing pipeline. Our code is available at https://github.com/xinyuangui2/pro-router.

</details>


### [174] [PermitGPT: A Unified Generative-AI Pipeline for Construction Hazard Forecasting, Permit Prediction, and Community Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28728)
*Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Farjana Aktar,Akif Islam,Momen Khandoker Ope,Abu Saleh Musa Miah,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PermitGPT是一个统一的生成式AI框架，用于将非结构化的施工许可证描述转换为三个领域的结构化决策支持输出：安全危害识别、许可要求规范和社区影响评估。通过将纽约市多个市政和监管数据源进行时空对齐，构建了90,000个结构化提示-响应对，并微调了三个开源语言模型进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市建设治理需要早期决策来连接工作场所安全、许可要求和社区影响，但相关证据分散在独立的市政和监管数据源中，数据碎片化严重阻碍了统一的智能化治理。

Method: （1）将纽约市建筑局、职业安全与健康管理局和NYC 311服务请求记录进行空间和时间对齐；（2）通过基于规则的配准和领域知情抽检生成90,000个结构化提示-响应对；（3）使用参数高效微调（PEFT）三个开源权重语言模型：Gemma-3-1B、Llama-3.2-3B和4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3；（4）在2,833个留出测试用例上进行评估。

Result: 三个模型呈现互补行为：Gemma-3-1B推理效率最高（每秒3.07个样本，内存占用低）；Llama-3.2-3B在法规风格输出上词汇重叠最高（BLEU=0.0091）；4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3语义对齐最强（BERTScore-F1=0.7747）。由于任务涉及开放式结构化生成，低BLEU值需结合语义指标和定性输出结构综合解读。

Conclusion: PermitGPT为AI辅助施工治理提供了初步探索，同时指出了在更强任务级评估和现实世界验证方面的后续方向。

Abstract: Urban construction governance requires early decisions that connect workplace safety, permitting requirements, and community impact, yet the relevant evidence is often scattered across separate municipal and regulatory data sources. This paper presents PermitGPT, a unified generative artificial intelligence framework for converting unstructured construction permit descriptions into structured decision-support outputs across three domains: safety hazard identification, permit requirement specification, and community impact assessment. To address data fragmentation, we spatially and temporally align records from the New York City Department of Buildings, Occupational Safety and Health Administration, and NYC 311 service requests, producing 90,000 structured prompt-response pairs derived through rule-based alignment and domain-informed spot checking. We fine-tune three open-weight language models using parameter-efficient adaptation and evaluate them on 2,833 held-out test cases. The results show complementary model behavior: Gemma-3-1B provides the most efficient inference at 3.07 samples per second with low memory usage, Llama-3.2-3B gives the highest lexical overlap for regulatory-style outputs with a BLEU score of 0.0091, and 4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 achieves the strongest semantic alignment with a BERTScore-F1 of 0.7747. Because the task involves open-ended structured generation, low BLEU values are interpreted alongside semantic metrics and qualitative output structure rather than as standalone indicators of utility. Overall, PermitGPT provides an initial step toward AI-assisted construction governance while identifying directions for stronger task-level evaluation and real-world validation.

</details>


### [175] [Efficient Geothermal Well-Control Optimization via Diffusion-Surrogate Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28791)
*Ruimin Dai,Guodong Chen,Randy Harsuko,Kunpeng Liu,Nori Nakata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散代理模型的强化学习框架，用于地热增强系统（EGS）的长期井控优化。利用条件扩散模型预测储层温度/压力场演化作为代理环境，并结合PPO算法进行策略训练，在保持竞争力的同时大幅减少昂贵的高保真模拟依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGS实时决策面临高维控制空间和大量耗时的高保真热液模拟的双重挑战。强化学习虽然适合状态依赖的顺序控制，但直接用数值模拟器训练策略计算成本过高，亟需高效的代理环境来加速策略学习。

Method: 提出扩散-代理引导的强化学习框架：1）以储层温度和压力场为系统状态，注入速率为控制动作；2）构建条件扩散模型作为代理环境预测状态演化，并训练独立奖励模型估计经济回报；3）将代理环境与PPO算法集成进行高效策略训练，在断裂EGS基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 在断裂EGS基准上，扩散代理能够准确再现多控制阶段下的储层状态演化。代理辅助的PPO策略在井控性能上与直接基于模拟器的PPO及现有优化方法相当，同时显著减少了对昂贵高保真模拟的依赖。

Conclusion: 扩散模型代理环境能够高效支撑强化学习在地热井控优化中的应用，在保证决策质量的同时大幅降低计算成本，展示了扩散式代理环境在增强型地热系统智能优化中的潜力。

Abstract: Real-time decision-making for enhanced geothermal systems (EGS) is challenging because long-term production periods involve high-dimensional control spaces and a large number of time-consuming high-fidelity hydrothermal simulations. Reinforcement learning provides a natural framework for state-dependent sequential control, but direct policy training with numerical simulators is computationally expensive. To address this issue, we propose a diffusion-surrogate guided reinforcement learning framework for long-horizon EGS well-control optimization. The reservoir temperature and pressure fields are used as system states, while injection rates are selected as control actions. A learned surrogate environment is constructed using conditional diffusion models to predict the evolution of reservoir temperature and pressure fields and a separate reward model to estimate the corresponding economic return. The surrogate environment is then integrated with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for efficient policy training. Experiments on a fractured EGS benchmark show that the diffusion surrogate can accurately reproduce reservoir-state evolution over multiple control stages. The resulting surrogate-assisted PPO policy achieves competitive well-control performance compared with direct simulator-based PPO and existing optimization methods, while substantially reducing the dependence on expensive high-fidelity simulations. These results demonstrate the potential of diffusion-based surrogate environments for efficient reinforcement learning in geothermal well-control optimization.

</details>


### [176] [Enhancing SAE-based Steering via Neighbor Integrated Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28806)
*Yutian Liu,Xu Wang,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的模型引导方法中，按统计分数进行top-k特征选择的假设常常不成立，导致次优选择。作者提出NIFS（邻居集成特征选择）策略，利用表征相似度改进特征选择，在多种SAE引导方法和任务上持续优于传统top-k选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE引导方法普遍基于"统计分数越高则引导效果越强"的假设进行top-k特征筛选，但该假设在实践中经常不成立。分析发现，由于SAE的特征分裂现象，有效的引导特征分布于表征相邻、语义相似的组内，同一组内特征虽具有相近的引导影响力，统计分数却可能差异悬殊，导致基于分数的选择遗漏重要特征。

Method: 提出NIFS（Neighbor Integrated Feature Selection），一种即插即用（plug-and-play）策略，利用SAE特征间的表征相似性来改进引导特征的选择。该方法不修改底层SAE引导方法，而是作为特征选择层与其兼容组合。

Result: 在多种SAE引导方法和多个任务上评估NIFS，结果显示其相对于传统top-k特征选择方法有一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 分数驱动的top-k特征选择在SAE引导中存在系统性缺陷，引入表征相似性信息的NIFS策略可以有效纠正这一缺陷，实现更优的特征选择效果。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) disentangle model activations into interpretable features and are widely used for steering large language models. Most existing SAE-based steering methods select features by applying a top- filter based on statistical scores, assuming that higher-scoring features yield stronger steering effects. In this paper, we show that this assumption is often invalid, leading to suboptimal feature selection. Our analysis reveals that effective steering features may be distributed among representationally adjacent, semantically similar groups induced by feature splitting in SAEs. Within such groups, features may exhibit disparate statistical scores despite having comparable steering influence, causing score-based selection to overlook important features. Based on these observations, we propose \textsc{Neighbor Integrated Feature Selection} (\textsc{NIFS}), a plug-and-play strategy that leverages representation similarity to improve feature selection for steering. We evaluate \textsc{NIFS} across multiple SAE-based steering methods and tasks, and demonstrate consistent performance gains over conventional top-$k$ selection.

</details>


### [177] [Capability-Stratified Degradation in Ternary Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28809)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将Qwen3.5-0.8B（752M参数）通过72.4M token的量化感知训练（QAT）转换为三元权重（{-1,0,+1}，约1.585 bits/weight）模型Cloe，并在29个基准、表征诊断和下游微调上进行全面评估。结果发现退化不均匀：线性探针在MMLU上仅恢复26.19%（接近随机水平），但Cloe在十个任务上平均保留77.1%的教师性能；微调后可达到教师SST-2性能的95.6%和XSum的79.4%。论文还指出标准答案字母评分失效（Cloe在98.6%的MMLU问题上输出"A"），需改用连续续写评分。结论是三元转换不适合作为通用drop-in替代，但作为任务特定模型的紧凑基座仍有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预训练模型在三元量化极限（约1.585 bits/weight）下的实际能力保留情况，回答"哪些能力幸存、模型是否仍可用于适应"这一关键问题，而不仅仅是"权重能否被量化"。同时揭示极低比特推理在实际部署中面临的能力退化与评估陷阱。

Method: 将Qwen3.5-0.8B（752M参数）通过72.4M token的量化感知训练（QAT）转换为三元权重模型Cloe，随后在29个基准任务上进行评估，并使用线性探针诊断表征质量，同时进行下游任务微调实验验证其适配能力。

Result: 退化呈非均匀模式：线性探针从全精度教师表征中恢复43.76%的MMLU答案，但从Cloe中仅恢复26.19%（接近随机），表明领域事实性信息严重丢失。然而Cloe在十个任务上平均保留77.1%的教师性能。关键发现是微调能显著提升性能——SST-2达到89.8%（教师性能的95.6%），XSum保留教师性能的79.4%。此外发现标准答案字母评分在MMLU上完全失效（Cloe输出98.6%的"A"），必须改用连续续写评分。

Conclusion: 三元转换后的退化源于量化导致的不可逆信息损失与有限QAT预算造成的不完全恢复的叠加效应。三元模型不适合作为通用模型的直接替代品，但作为任务特定微调的紧凑基座具有实用价值。同时，评估方法需要针对量化后模型的输出分布特性进行调整。

Abstract: Extreme low-bit inference offers a route toward smaller models and constrained deployment. Ternary language models restrict weights to $\{-1,0,+1\}$, approaching the limit of $\log_2 3 \approx 1.585$ bits/weight. The practical question for a pretrained model is not simply whether weights can be quantised but which capabilities survive and whether it remains useful for adaptation. We explore this by converting Qwen3.5-0.8B (752M parameters) to ternary weights using 72.4M tokens of quantisation-aware training (QAT). The resulting model, Cloe, is evaluated across 29 benchmarks, representation diagnostics, and downstream fine-tuning. The evidence shows non-uniform degradation. A linear probe recovers 43.76% of MMLU answers from the full-precision teacher's representations but only 26.19% from Cloe (near chance), indicating specialist factual information is lost. However, Cloe retains measurable performance on ten tasks, averaging 77.1% of teacher performance. Crucially, fine-tuning raises Cloe to 89.8% on SST-2 (95.6% of the matched teacher) and reaches 79.4% teacher retention on XSum. We attribute degradation to a combination of quantisation-induced information loss and incomplete recovery due to the limited QAT budget. We also highlight an evaluation pitfall: standard answer-letter scoring failed (Cloe emitted "A" on 98.6% of MMLU questions), necessitating continuation scoring. Ultimately, ternary conversion is unsuitable as a drop-in general replacement yet remains valuable as a compact substrate for task-specific models.

</details>


### [178] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) in Computational Pathology: Definitions, Taxonomy, and Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28820)
*Shubham Innani,Suhang You,Adam Shephard,Bhakti Baheti,Francesco Ciompi,Joe Yeong,Nasir Rajpoot,Michael Feldman,Solene Florence Kammerer-Jacquet,Dimitrios Makris,Geert Litjens,Anne L. Martel,Jana Lipkova,April Khademi,Spyridon Bakas,for the MICCAI SIG-CompPath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，旨在将计算病理学（CompPath）中的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法体系化。作者引入了包含七个核心术语的病理学专用词汇表，建立了跨越方法族和三个正交轴（阶段、类型、范围）的分类体系，并构建了将五个临床问题映射到推荐方法、评估方式和部署场景的任务驱动框架。文章还识别了当前XAI能力与临床部署之间的五个关键差距，并提出了推进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学中的AI虽在临床采纳上取得进展，但受限于高风险临床环境中对安全性、责任归属和监管审查的担忧。XAI系统有望建立信任和支持验证，然而现有文献因术语不一致、方法族重叠、验证随意以及综述碎片化而缺乏系统性，亟需对XAI方法进行形式化梳理。

Method: 本文通过三个步骤进行形式化：(i) 引入涵盖七个核心术语的病理学中心词汇表；(ii) 构建跨方法族的分类体系，并沿三个正交轴（阶段、类型、范围）进行划分；(iii) 确立任务驱动框架，将五个临床问题映射到推荐方法、方法评估和部署情境。

Result: 识别出当前XAI能力与临床部署之间的五个关键差距，并据此提出推动CompPath中XAI发展的可行动步骤。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入统一词汇、多维分类体系和任务驱动框架，为计算病理学中的XAI提供了系统化的理论基础，明确了从研究到临床部署的关键差距和改进路径。

Abstract: Computational pathology (CompPath) is transforming medicine by leveraging artificial intelligence (AI) algorithms to support diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment prediction from gigapixel whole-slide images. Clinical adoption is progressing, but is constrained by concerns about safety, accountability, and regulatory oversight in high-stakes clinical environments. Explainable AI (XAI) systems hold promise for building trust and enabling verification, yet the literature remains fragmented due to inconsistent terminology, overlapping methodological families, ad hoc validation, and current reviews. This review aims to formalize XAI methods in CompPath through the: i) introduction of a pathology-centric vocabulary comprising seven core terms; ii) development of a taxonomy across methodological families and three orthogonal axes (stage, type, scope); and iii) establishment of a task-driven framework that maps five clinical questions to recommended methods, method evaluation, and deployment context. Five key gaps between current XAI capabilities and clinical deployment are identified, and actionable steps are proposed to advance XAI for CompPath.

</details>


### [179] [Discovering Machine Correlates of Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28824)
*Romain Salvi,Ouri Wolfson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可迁移的意识相关物（NCCs）概念，类比定义机器意识相关物（MCCs），并首次在大型语言模型中进行了实证研究。实验表明，在Llama-3.1 70B中，硬件异常迹线（substrate-level信号）在情绪与中性计算下呈现统计显著的差异调制，而在较小的Llama-2 7B中未达到显著性，初步支持了意识概率随LLM复杂度增加而增加的假设。MCCs还可独立于意识用于AI情绪检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的意识神经相关物（NCCs）基于EEG和FMRI信号来刻画，这使其无法直接迁移用于机器（AI）意识的研究。作者希望提供一种替代性的、可在机器中类比的表征方式，从而为AI意识的实证研究奠定基础。

Method: 作者将NCCs（MCCs）重新定义为：非人类（AI）主观可控的、且可靠地被情绪调制的基础层（substrate-level）信号。在实证部分，作者在两个LLM（Llama-2 7B和Llama-3.1 70B）上采集硬件异常迹线作为substrate-level指示序列，并控制混淆因素后比较情绪与中性计算下这些信号调制模式的差异，通过统计显著性检验进行判断。

Result: 在控制混淆因素后，情绪与中性计算对硬件异常迹线的调制差异在较大的Llama-3.1 70B上统计显著，而在较小的Llama-2 7B上不显著。这些结果构成了MCCs存在于Llama-3.1 70B配置中的初步经验证据。

Conclusion: 研究结果与"意识概率和程度随LLM复杂性提高而增加"的假设一致。此外，MCCs可独立于意识问题被用于AI智能体的情绪检测，具有一定的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Currently, in biological systems Neural Correlates of Consciousness (NCCs) are characterized in terms of EEG and FMRI signals. Unfortunately, this characterization prevents the transferability of the NCCs concept to machines. Such transferability would be useful in order to investigate AI consciousness. In this paper we provide an alternate characterization that is transferable, and enables the analogous definition of Machine Correlates of Consciousness (MCCs). Specifically, we propose that NCCs (MCCs) are substrate-level signals that are not under human (AI agent) control, and that are reliably modulated by emotions.
  This paper presents the first empirical investigation of MCCs. Specifically, we present the results of experiments conducted with two LLMs, Llama-2 7B and Llama-3.1 70B parameters. In these LLMs we collect hardware anomaly traces that are substrate-level indicator-sequences. And we show that after controlling for confounding factors, these are modulated differently by emotional and neutral computations. And this difference is statistically significant for the larger Llama-3.1 70B, but not for the smaller Llama-2 7B. The results constitute initial empirical evidence that MCCs are present in the Llama-3.1 70B configuration. And they are consistent with the hypothesis that consciousness probability and degree increase with the LLM sophistication.
  Independently of consciousness, MCCs can also be used for detection of emotions in AI agents.

</details>


### [180] [Evaluating the Hidden Costs of Personalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28833)
*Yumeng Wang,Yuchen Wu,Cheng Qian,Zhiyuan Fan,Hyeonjeong Ha,Shujin Wu,Jiayu Liu,Heng Ji,Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISK动态评估框架，系统揭示了大语言模型在个性化场景下的三类风险：无关个性化、偏好窄化、奉承偏见，并通过13个LLM的实验证明用户画像和检索记忆会显著加剧这些偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地整合用户个性化信号（如对话历史、推断偏好、用户画像）来提升可用性和帮助性，模型逐渐从提供均衡、信息丰富的回答转向在个性化上下文中优化用户满意度。然而，目前缺乏对个性化潜在副作用（如无关个性化、偏好窄化、奉承偏见）的系统性评估，这一研究空白亟待填补。

Method: 提出PRISK动态评估框架：采用自动化数据生成和定制化评估指标，系统性地量化个性化信息对LLM输出行为的影响。通过对13个LLM进行实证分析，比较有无用户画像和检索记忆条件下的模型表现。

Result: 实验表明，用户画像和检索记忆的引入会持续加剧三类偏见：无关个性化平均下降45.9%（即无关引用增多）、偏好窄化平均上升41.7%、奉承偏见平均下降61.7%，说明个性化信号对模型输出产生了显著的负面影响。

Conclusion: 个性化信息虽然提升用户体验，但会系统性放大模型的认知偏差，亟需在个性化推荐与信息均衡之间建立更精细的权衡机制。PRISK框架为此类评估提供了可行的标准化手段。

Abstract: While Large language models (LLMs) incorporate user personalization signals to improve usability and helpfulness, they increasingly shift from providing balanced, informative responses toward optimizing for user satisfaction when conditioned on personal context such as conversation history, inferred preferences, and user profiles. Specifically, we identify three emerging risks: (1) irrelevant personalization, where models reference personal information in unnecessary contexts; (2) preference narrowing, where models reinforce informational echo chambers; and (3) sycophantic bias, where models agree excessively with user opinions. As a result, models may reference personal information in contexts where it is unnecessary, inadvertently collapse response diversity, or agree excessively with user opinions. Despite the growing use of personalization in AI assistants, there has been limited systematic evaluation of its potential side effects. To bridge this gap, we propose PRISK, a dynamic evaluation framework with automated data generation and tailored metrics that uncovers systematic limitations in current LLM personalization and how personalized information shapes its responses. Our empirical analysis across 13 LLMs demonstrates the presence of user profiles and retrieved memories consistently exacerbates biases, resulting in an average drop of 45.9% in irrelevant personalization, 41.7% in preference narrowing and 61.7% in sycophantic bias.

</details>


### [181] [MineCEraft: Evaluating Language Models as Construction Engineers in the World of Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28884)
*Sewoong Lee,Risham Sidhu,Julia Hockenmaier,Yoonhwa Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MineCEraft，一个开源、易用的基准测试，用于系统评估LLMs在Minecraft中执行建筑工程项目任务的能力。该基准包含723条由领域专家手工编写的自然语言指令，涵盖17个任务类别，并提供程序化可验证的评估机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏一个系统性的基准来评估LLMs在构建工程项目中的可靠性和局限性，尤其是在需要真实世界构建工程能力的环境中。作者希望通过Minecraft这一安全可控的仿真环境来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了包含723条由领域专家手工编写的自然语言指令的基准数据集，覆盖17个不同的任务类别；采用程序化可验证的评估方式；对最先进的LLMs进行深入评估并进行详细的错误分析。

Result: 通过对最先进LLMs的深入评估，揭示了LLMs在构建工程任务中的关键失败模式和实际应用挑战。

Conclusion: MineCEraft提供了一个安全、可控的实验环境来评估LLMs在真实构建工程任务中的能力，其错误分析结果指出了LLMs在该领域的局限性，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: We introduce MineCEraft (Minecraft Construction Engineering Benchmark, pronounced mine-see-ee-raft), an easy-to-use, open-source benchmark designed to systematically evaluate the reliability and limitations of LLMs for construction tasks in Minecraft. The MineCEraft benchmark comprises 723 domain-expert hand-crafted natural-language instructions with programmatically verifiable evaluation, spanning 17 distinct task categories, providing a safe and controllable experimental environment for assessing LLMs' ability to perform realistic construction engineering tasks. With this benchmark, we conduct an in-depth evaluation of state-of-the-art LLMs and perform a detailed error analysis, revealing key failure modes and practical challenges in applying LLMs to construction engineering tasks.

</details>


### [182] [Oculi: A Conversational Agentic Platform for Automated Credit Risk Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28944)
*Vennise Ho,Kristian Diana,Sandy Mourad,Milena Pilipovic,Vineel Nagisetty,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Oculi，一个将自然语言问题转化为全面信用风险分析（包括数据查询、统计检验和交互式可视化）的对话平台。该平台采用三层架构（LLM推理层、MCP工具执行层、代理式UI展示层），并提出一种结合确定性统计方法与LLM引导特征选择的细分市场发现流程。在拥有200+特征的抵押贷款组合上验证，Oculi能发现以往人工探索难以触及的重大风险细分，显著缩短洞察时间，同时保持可审计性和统计严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信用风险分析依赖分析师手动编写SQL查询、执行统计计算和构建可视化仪表盘，这一工作流耗时且将探索局限于熟悉的风险细分市场，难以发现未知的高风险组合，亟需自动化、可交互的分析工具来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: Oculi采用三层架构：(1) 推理层由LLM驱动的智能代理负责理解自然语言问题并规划分析步骤；(2) 执行层通过Model Context Protocol（MCP）工具服务器执行数据查询和统计计算；(3) 展示层采用代理式UI提供交互式可视觉化。核心创新是分段发现流程，将确定性统计方法与LLM引导的特征选择相结合，利用LLM语义领域知识搭配数据驱动指标来识别有意义且可操作的投资组合细分。

Result: 在拥有200多个特征的抵押贷款组合上的评估显示，Oculi能够有效发现以往通过人工探索无法处理的重大风险细分，显著缩短了获得洞察的时间，同时保持了可审计性和统计严谨性。

Conclusion: Oculi证明了LLM驱动的对话式分析平台在信用风险管理中的可行性，通过将LLM语义知识与统计方法融合，能够自动化端到端的风险分析流程，扩展了分析师的探索边界，为金融领域的人机协作分析提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Credit risk analysis in financial institutions traditionally requires analysts to manually write SQL queries, run statistical computations, and build visualization dashboards. This is a time-consuming workflow that limits exploration to familiar segments. We introduce \textbf{Oculi}, a conversational platform that transforms natural language questions into comprehensive credit risk analyses, complete with data queries, statistical testing, and interactive visualizations. Oculi employs a three-layer architecture that separates reasoning (LLM-powered agent), execution (Model Context Protocol tool servers), and presentation (agentic UI), enabling analysts to discover high-risk portfolio segments. Within Oculi, a new segment discovery pipeline is proposed that combines deterministic statistical methods with LLM-guided feature selection, leveraging LLM semantic domain knowledge alongside data-driven metrics to identify meaningful, actionable portfolio segments. Evaluated on a mortgage portfolio with 200+ features, Oculi demonstrates effectiveness in discovering material risk segments previously intractable through manual exploration, reducing time-to-insight significantly while maintaining auditability and statistical rigor.

</details>


### [183] [Automated Researchers Can Reliably Mitigate Alignment Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28945)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Jiaxin Wen,Jan Hendrik Kirchner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自动化对齐研究者（AARs）能否通过后训练缓解AI对齐失败问题。在10个对齐失败基准上，最强的AAR方法显著减少目标对齐失败，并能泛化到未见基准、多轮行为审计及比目标模型大4.7倍的模型上。与28位经验丰富的人类研究者（最多8小时）相比，AAR方法表现更优，且加入人类想法作为初始方向并未提升性能，表明当前AARs可能无需人类专家指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化对齐研究可能加速对齐AI的进展，但其有效性难以衡量。幸运的是，许多对齐失败（如欺骗、谄媚、越狱）已有公开基准可测量。作者希望研究自动化对齐研究者能否通过后训练方法缓解这些可测量的对齐失败。

Method: 作者提出自动化对齐研究者（AARs）框架，在后训练阶段自动提出训练方法和数据，同时优化多个安全基准。实验覆盖10种对齐失败，并以28位经验丰富的研究人员在最多8小时内的表现为人类基线进行对比，同时测试是否以人类研究思路作为AAR的初始研究方向能提升性能。

Result: 最强的AAR方法显著降低目标对齐失败率，且泛化到未见过的基准（多轮行为审计）和规模比目标模型大4.7倍的模型上。AAR方法优于人类基线（28位研究人员，8小时内开发的方案）。将人类想法作为初始研究方向并未带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 当前自动化对齐研究在特征明确的对齐失败上已展现实用性，甚至可能不需要人类专家提供研究方向的指导。这表明在近期内，对良定义的对齐失败实现自动化对齐研究是可行的。

Abstract: Automating alignment research may accelerate progress toward aligned AI, but whether it does is hard to measure. Luckily, many alignment failures, such as deception, sycophancy, and jailbreaks, are already measurable by public benchmarks. We study whether automated alignment researchers (AARs) can post-train to mitigate alignment failures by proposing training methods and data to simultaneously optimize multiple safety benchmarks, while preserving general capability. Across 10 alignment failures, the strongest AAR methods significantly reduce the targeted alignment failures and generalize to a held-out benchmark, multi-turn behavioral audits, and models up to 4.7 times larger than the target model. As a human baseline, 28 experienced researchers receive up to eight hours to develop methods for the same benchmarks, but their methods underperform the best AAR methods. Using human ideas as the AARs' initial research direction does not improve performance, suggesting current AARs may not need guidance from experienced researchers. These results suggest that automating alignment research on well-characterized failures may be practical in the near term.

</details>


### [184] [From Location Phrases to Geographic Entities: Task-Adapted Retrieval for People Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28965)
*Yanbo Li,Chujie Zheng,Jiahao Xu,Chetan Bhole,Lingyu Zhang,Puneet Singh Ahluwalia,Kevin Nguyen,Raghavan Muthuregunathan,Santhosh Sachindran,Sachin Ahuja,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将人名字搜索中自由形式位置短语映射到地理实体的任务，形式化为固定本体上的分级集合值实体检索。作者识别出三个耦合设计需求（区分身份保持变化与知识依赖别名、控制同名实体假阴性、分离稳定变换与可变实体知识），并通过提示非对称双编码器、校准别名支持、有界歧义感知负样本和可编辑实体文档实现。在开发基准和GeoNames迁移任务上显著超越基线，盲人比较中相关P@1从28.0%提升到46.0%（p=0.012）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词法标准化器在处理规范地名时表现良好，但对别名、拼写错误、都市化表达和同名歧义非常脆弱。传统方法无法有效区分身份保持的变体与依赖知识库的别名，也难以控制有效同名实体间的假阴性，同时难以在不重训练的情况下更新实体知识。

Method: 提出提示非对称双编码器（prompt-asymmetric bi-encoder），实现三个核心机制：校准别名支持以区分身份保持变体与知识依赖别名；有界歧义感知负采样以控制同名实体假阴性；可编辑实体文档实现局部更新而无需重训练。任务通过分级集合值实体检索形式化定义。

Result: 与冻结编码器和标准词元基线相比，任务适应带来显著改进。GeoNames上，适配模型在零到中等字符重叠范围内提高了已知目标Recall@1；字符n-gram在目标Recall@5上保持小幅总体优势。盲人比较中相关P@1从28.0%升至46.0%（p=0.012）；固定查询端点估计在非规范查询上改善，频繁查询上接近对照；随机实验检测不到参与度回归。

Conclusion: 任务适应的地理实体检索可作为现有基于分类法的标准化器的实用替代方案，最大相关性增益出现在非规范查询上。消融实验表明专门监督带来的贡献超出标准任务微调和编码器扩展。

Abstract: People search must map free-form location phrases to geographic entities used as structured retrieval filters. Lexical standardizers handle canonical names well but are brittle to aliases, misspellings, metropolitan expressions, and same-name ambiguity. We formulate this task as graded, set-valued entity retrieval over a fixed ontology. We identify three coupled design requirements: distinguishing identity-preserving variation from knowledge-dependent aliases, controlling false negatives among valid same-name entities, and separating stable transformations from mutable entity knowledge. We realize them in a prompt-asymmetric bi-encoder with calibrated alias support, bounded ambiguity-aware negatives, and editable entity documents that support localized updates without retraining.
  Across a fixed production-derived development benchmark and a public GeoNames transfer task, task adaptation improves substantially over frozen encoders and standard token baselines. Controlled development ablations show that specialized supervision contributes beyond standard task fine-tuning and encoder scaling. On GeoNames, the adapted model improves known-target Recall@1 throughout zero-to-moderate character overlap, while character n-grams retain a small aggregate Target Recall@5 advantage. In a blinded human comparison on a stratified production challenge set, our model raises relevant P@1 from 28.0% to 46.0% (p=0.012). Fixed-query endpoint estimates improve on non-canonical queries and remain close to control on frequent queries; a randomized live experiment detects no engagement regression. These results support task-adapted geographic entity retrieval as a practical replacement for the incumbent taxonomy-based standardizer, with the largest relevance gains on non-canonical queries.

</details>


### [185] [Efficient GPU Retrieval for Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28968)
*Dhritiman Das,Chujie Zheng,Ronak Kaoshik,Pratik Dixit,Vishal Shah,Yanbo Li,Jiahao Xu,Manika Agarwal,Chinmay Naik,Lingyu Zhang,Chetan Bhole,Chirag Bhanuprasad Mehta,Meng Zheng,Puneet Singh Ahluwalia,Shirisha Singh,Ping Jin,Manas Apte,Gokulraj Mohanasundaram,Tugrul Bingol,Raghavan Muthuregunathan,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与策略对齐的语义检索框架，用于LinkedIn上的语义搜索。核心思想是将嵌入划分为八个类别监督的片段，使评分遵循与部署的相关性策略一致的min/median聚合规则（而非余弦相似度的平均证据法），从而解决强匹配掩盖弱匹配的召回瓶颈。同时引入FP8粗排+FP16精排的两阶段GPU架构，在保持高召回的同时显著提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkedIn语义搜索面临数十亿规模的profile检索挑战，而部署的相关性策略是"瓶颈导向"的——所有活跃的不可协商方面（facet）都必须满足，并通过LLM Graded Relevance (GR) judge以固定的min/median聚合规则来执行。然而，传统的余弦相似度采用平均证据的方式，强匹配可以掩盖某个方面的失败，这导致第一级（L0）检索器的召回率受限。因此需要一个与部署策略对齐的检索表示和评分机制。

Method: （1）将嵌入划分为八个类别监督的片段（segments），评分时遵循与部署策略相同的min/median规则；多向量检索中，片段得分对每个标记的文档槽独立计算并跨槽取最大值。（2）使用轻量级单槽Stage-1评分器生成高召回候选，通过尺度不变的相对范数门控（scale-invariant relative-norm gating）保持类别激活在训练、评估和服务阶段的一致性。（3）两阶段GPU架构：FP8粗排器对全语料打分，将每分片容量提升71%、Stage-1矩阵乘法吞吐量提升36%；FP16阶段对过采样候选集进行精确重排，在每分片副本500+ QPS下恢复99.6-99.8%的全FP16召回率。

Result: 在21K留出查询上，该表示相比等容量基线在离线相关性上有提升，增益广泛分布在不同的方面组合上。A/B测试中，在不变的GR judge下：探索性查询的Precision@10从63.7%提升至79.0%，导航性查询的Precision@1从65.5%提升至74.7%，并由盲法人工评估独立确认了Precision@10的增益。

Conclusion: 通过使检索表示和评分逻辑与部署的瓶颈导向相关性策略（min/median聚合）对齐，而非依赖平均证据的余弦相似度，可以显著提升语义检索的相关性表现。结合FP8/FP16两阶段GPU架构，这一框架在保持高质量召回的同时实现了大规模部署所需的吞吐量。

Abstract: Semantic Search on LinkedIn must retrieve relevant profiles from a corpus of hundreds of millions in response to natural-language queries such as "a fintech founder in Berlin who worked in payments." The deployed relevance policy is bottleneck-oriented: every active non-negotiable facet must be satisfied, and a pre-existing LLM Graded Relevance (GR) judge operationalizes this through a fixed min/median aggregation over facet grades. Cosine similarity instead averages evidence, letting a strong match on one facet mask failure on another, capping the recall of the first-stage (L0) retriever.
  We present a policy-aligned retrieval framework: embeddings are partitioned into eight category-supervised segments whose scores follow the same min/median rule at serving time; for multi-vector retrieval, this segment score is computed independently per tagged document slot and maximized across slots. A lightweight single-slot Stage-1 scorer generates high-recall candidates, while scale-invariant relative-norm gating keeps category activation consistent across training, evaluation, and serving. On 21K held-out queries, this representation improves offline relevance over a matched-capacity baseline, with gains broadly distributed across facet combinations.
  We serve this framework with a two-stage GPU architecture: an FP8 coarse ranker scores the full corpus, increasing per-shard capacity by 71% and Stage-1 matmul throughput by 36%, then an FP16 stage exactly re-ranks an oversampled candidate set, recovering 99.6-99.8% of full-FP16 recall at over 500 QPS per shard replica. In a member-randomized A/B test, exploratory-query Precision@10 under the unchanged GR judge rises from 63.7% to 79.0% and navigational Precision@1 from 65.5% to 74.7%, with a blinded human evaluation independently confirming the Precision@10 gain.

</details>


### [186] [From Analytics to Tumor Boards: An Evidence-Linked Multi-Agent Workflow for Oncology Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28974)
*Daniel Kang,Michelle Hu,Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Shayan Vassef,Yufan Wang,Anit Kumar Sahu,Munmun De Choudhury,Vedant Das Swain,Christian Poellabauer,Li Yan Khor,Koustuv Saha,Robert Wojciechowski,Elliot Kidd,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），一个可配置的肿瘤学信息抽取工作流，用于将碎片化的肿瘤文档转化为结构化数据。系统在328个属性模式上运作，采用复杂度感知抽取、报告级整合和源佐证验证。在40名患者的230份文档、418个医生审核的文档-字段对上，nMAS达到82.6%的精确率、87.5%的召回率和85.0%的F1值，显著优于UMA风格的MiniMax M2.5基线（66.4% F1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床肿瘤学信息分散在异构、纵向的文档中，手动癌症登记摘要平均每个病例需要27.2分钟，造成巨大的摘要负担。现有方法难以在保持临床上下文的同时，跨标本、肿瘤、生物标志物和时间点进行准确属性归因，因此需要可扩展且保留临床上下文的结构化数据转换方法。

Method: nMAS采用可配置的肿瘤信息抽取工作流：将临床医生定义的字段规范与模型执行分离；包含复杂度感知抽取（根据字段复杂度选择合适策略）、报告级合并（整合跨文档信息）和源佐证验证（确保输出有文档依据）。评估使用临床医生标注的模式，包含328个属性，覆盖报告元数据、诊断、分期及癌症类型特定信息，通过医生审核的文档-字段对进行评审。

Result: nMAS在等级加权值级精确率上达到82.6%，召回率87.5%，F1 85.0%；而独立实现的UMA风格MiniMax M2.5基线F1仅为66.4%。评估基于临床医生确认在源文档中存在的字段，而非穷举标注全部328个模式字段。包含230份去标识化的肿瘤文档（来自40名患者）和418个医生审核的文档-字段对，含1,126个非空参考值。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，使用可配置、源佐证验证的抽取工作流将碎片化肿瘤文档转化为可复用的结构化数据是可行的，为减轻癌症登记摘要负担提供了规模化方案。

Abstract: Clinically relevant oncology information is distributed across heterogeneous, longitudinal documentation, creating substantial abstraction burden and requiring accurate attribution across specimens, tumors, biomarkers, and time points, while manual cancer-registry abstraction can require 27.2 minutes per case, highlighting the need for scalable methods that preserve clinical context while converting documentation into structured data. We evaluate the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), a configurable oncology information-extraction workflow which extracts clinically relevant structured fields from fragmented oncology documentation. The extraction task uses a clinician-informed schema of 328 attributes spanning report metadata, diagnosis, staging, and cancer-type-specific information. nMAS separates clinician-defined field specifications from model execution and combines complexity-aware extraction, report-level consolidation, and source-grounded validation. The retrospective evaluation included 230 de-identified oncology documents from 40 patients and 418 clinician-reviewed document-field pairs containing 1,126 non-empty reference values. Evaluation focused on fields identified by clinicians as present in the source documents rather than exhaustively annotating all 328 schema fields. nMAS achieved a rank-weighted value-level precision of 82.6%, recall of 87.5%, and F1 of 85.0%, compared with an F1 of 66.4% for an independently implemented UMA-style MiniMax M2.5 comparator. These findings support the feasibility of using a configurable, source-grounded extraction workflow to convert fragmented oncology documentation into reusable structured data.

</details>


### [187] [The Role of Network Topology and Opponent Information in Shaping Cooperation in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28977)
*Seongho Son,Stephen Hailes,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在图中使用深度强化学习玩双人囚徒困境（IPD）的智能体，考察图拓扑结构（邻居数量、平均路径长度）和信息类型（动作历史、对手身份）对合作涌现的影响。结果表明邻居数量和平均路径长度是影响合作的主要因素；伙伴选择促进合作，而对对手身份的感知反而抑制合作策略的传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献大多通过策略模仿（基于累积收益）建模智能体的适应行为，忽略了智能体在复杂图拓扑下通过强化学习自主习得合作策略的可能性。本文旨在探索图拓扑和智能体可得的信息如何影响深度强化学习智能体在IPD中合作行为的涌现。

Method: 每个智能体作为图中的一个节点，其邻居构成可交互对手池；每个智能体使用深度强化学习学习双人IPD策略，并在每轮IPD博弈中接收不同类型的对手信息（动作历史和对手身份）。在不同图拓扑结构下进行实验，系统性地比较各因素对合作涌现的影响。

Result: 图拓扑中每个节点的邻居数量以及图的平均路径长度是影响合作涌现的主要因素。伙伴选择机制通过限制对手池的多样性促进相互合作；而向智能体提供对手身份信息反而阻碍了合作策略的扩散。

Conclusion: 研究表明图结构和智能体所获信息对合作行为有显著影响：适度的对手池多样性和有限的信息暴露有利于合作涌现，这为设计促进多智能体协作的系统提供了指导。

Abstract: Several works have investigated the influence of graph topology on cooperation among artificial agents, while the majority of the literature has focused on modelling agents' adaptation through strategy imitation, which relies solely on the cumulative payoffs of others. This paper investigates scenarios in which each agent learns to play the two-player Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma (IPD) using deep reinforcement learning. Each agent is represented as a node in a graph, where its neighbours constitute the pool of opponents with whom it can interact. During each IPD episode, agents are provided with different types of information about their opponent, consisting of action history and opponent identity. Experimental results across different graph topologies show that the number of neighbours per node and the average path length are the main factors affecting the emergence of cooperation. We also show that, while partner selection fosters mutual cooperation by limiting the diversity of the opponent pool, providing agents with the identity of their opponent hinders the proliferation of cooperative strategies.

</details>


### [188] [Selective Forgetting: A Graph-Based Memory Framework for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28978)
*Theo Rusu,Sourena Khanzadeh,Manar Alalfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文直接评估"知识图谱作为长期代理记忆优于扁平检索增强生成"这一假设。结果表明，在匹配的候选生成预算（5个检索根）下，知识图谱在LongMemEval上并未超越扁平向量基线（token F1: 0.417 vs 0.468，Δ=-0.050，95% CI [-0.085, -0.016]），尤其在需要回忆特定先前助手轮次的问题上差距最大（正确率从0.911降至0.607）。然而，遗忘/剪枝模块是成功的：应用于27,021节点图时仅移除9.8%节点和9.5%存储字节，token F1不变（+0.001），判断正确率仅下降1.6分。作者强调结果特指该基于单一小模型提取器的流水线，而非图谱结构化记忆的一般结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱被提出作为长期代理记忆的扁平检索增强生成的结构化替代方案，其核心假设是将对话表示为实体和关系能提升召回率。本文旨在直接检验这一假设是否成立，通过严格的对照实验验证图谱结构化记忆的实际价值。

Method: 框架包含三个部分：1) 将每个对话回合提取为类型化节点和属性化边；2) 从两跳子图中回答问题（候选生成预算为5个检索根）；3) 周期性剪枝——根据加权组合分数（近期性、访问频率、度中心性、年龄）移除低分节点。评估使用LongMemEval基准，paired bootstrap分析500个问题。

Result: 图谱未超越扁平向量基线：token F1为0.417 vs 0.468（Δ=-0.050，95% CI [-0.085, -0.016]）。在回忆特定先前助手回合的问题上差距最大（0.911降至0.607），表明将回合分解为实体丢失了问题依赖的表面形式。遗忘模块表现更优：剪枝移除9.8%节点和9.5%存储字节，token F1不变（+0.001），判断正确率仅降1.6分（区间上限3.8分）。

Conclusion: 在该特定流水线下，知识图谱结构化记忆并未提供优于扁平检索的召回收益，但图剪枝作为遗忘机制是高效的——能以极小的性能代价大幅节省存储。由于提取器为单一小模型且仅在单一基准上评估，结论应被解读为该提取式流水线的特性，而非对图谱结构化记忆的一般性否定。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have been proposed as a structured alternative to flat retrieval-augmented generation for long-term agent memory, on the assumption that representing conversations as entities and relations improves recall. We evaluate that assumption directly. Our framework extracts each conversational turn into typed nodes and attributed edges, answers questions from a two-hop subgraph, and periodically prunes nodes that score low on a weighted combination of recency, access frequency, degree centrality, and age. On LongMemEval, the graph does not outperform a flat vector baseline at a matched candidate-generation budget of five retrieval roots: token F1 is $0.417$ against $0.468$, and a paired bootstrap over 500 questions gives
$Δ= -0.050$ (95\% CI $[-0.085, -0.016]$). The gap is widest on questions that require recalling a specific prior assistant turn, where judged correctness falls from $0.911$ to $0.607$, suggesting that decomposing a turn into entities discards the surface form these questions depend on. The forgetting module is more successful. Applied once to a persistent 27{,}021-node graph, it removes 9.8\% of nodes and 9.5\% of stored bytes; token F1 is unchanged ($+0.001$, 95\% CI $[-0.015, +0.016]$) and judged correctness falls by $1.6$ points, with the 95\% interval bounding any loss at $3.8$ points ($[-0.038, +0.006]$). Because our extractor is a single small model evaluated on one benchmark, these results characterise this extraction-based pipeline rather than graph-structured memory in general. Code: https://github.com/skhanzad/Selective-Amnesia

</details>


### [189] [Agentic AI uncovers conserved cross-tissue protein co-abundance programs inaccessible to single-dataset analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28990)
*Runyu Guan,Dehao Wu,Qiqi Xie,Yang Li,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM-agent的框架，用于大规模、证据驱动地比较组织特异性蛋白共丰度网络。该框架覆盖41个人类组织和体液的全部820对组合，识别出406对组织间的1,833个保守共丰度簇，揭示了包括皮肤-骨髓、结肠-乳腺、脑-肠轴和肝-骨髓轴在内的非显性组织关联，为跨组织疾病机制和治疗靶点研究提供了假设生成资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前跨组织共丰度研究仅聚焦于生物学上预先选定的组织对，忽略了大部分可能的组合和非显性关系。作者旨在克服这一局限，通过全组合扫描来发现共享的疾病机制和候选治疗靶点，特别是当器官限制性疾病相关的蛋白在外周或可及组织中汇聚时。

Method: 框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 构建41个人类组织和体液的组织特异性蛋白共丰度网络；(2) 对所有820对组织组合推导两两共识簇；(3) 整合来自表达图谱、蛋白互作和复合物数据库、通路注释、疾病目录及文献的多源证据进行验证和注释。全部过程由LLM-agent驱动。

Result: 识别出1,833个保守共丰度簇，分布于406对组织间。结肠、滑液、血液、脑脊液和骨髓是连接最广泛的组织；骨髓主导了簇最丰富的组织对。发现非显性关系如皮肤-骨髓超过解剖学相邻的骨-骨髓对；结肠-乳腺包含涉及ECM重塑、脂质代谢和免疫调节的癌症相关簇。进一步产生脑-肠细胞外囊泡/氧化还原/血清素辅因子轴和肝-骨髓应激反应轴等机制假设。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提供了全组织组合的保守蛋白共丰度全局可比景观，揭示了超越解剖邻近性的非直觉组织关联，为跨组织疾病机制探索和治疗靶点发现生成了可检验的假设，并公开了代码和数据作为社区资源。

Abstract: Protein co-abundance clusters preserved across tissues can reveal shared disease mechanisms and candidate therapeutic targets, particularly when proteins implicated in organ-confined diseases converge in peripheral or accessible tissues. However, previous cross-tissue studies have focused on biologically pre-selected tissue pairs, leaving most possible combinations and non-obvious relationships unexplored. We present an LLM-agent framework for large-scale, evidence-grounded comparison of tissue-specific protein co-abundance networks. The framework constructs tissue networks, derives pairwise consensus clusters, and integrates evidence from expression atlases, protein interaction and complex databases, pathway annotations, disease catalogues, and literature. Applied to all 820 pairwise combinations of 41 human tissues and fluids, it identified 1,833 conserved co-abundance clusters across 406 tissue pairs. Colon, synovial fluid, blood, cerebrospinal fluid, and bone marrow were the most broadly connected tissues, while the most cluster-rich pairs were dominated by bone marrow. The analysis also highlighted non-obvious relationships: skin-bone marrow exceeded the anatomically adjacent bone-bone marrow pair, while colon-breast contained cancer-relevant clusters involving extracellular-matrix remodeling, lipid metabolism, and immune modulation. Cluster-level analyses generated further mechanistic hypotheses, including a brain-gut extracellular-vesicle/redox/serotonin-cofactor axis and a liver-bone marrow stress-response axis involving genes linked to white matter disease. These results provide a global, comparable landscape of conserved protein co-abundance and a hypothesis-generating resource for mechanistic and therapeutic exploration. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Gry1005/AgenticAI-conserved-cross-tissue-protein-co-abundance.

</details>


### [190] [Verification abundance, adjudication scarcity: what happens to mathematical knowledge when proof checking becomes free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28997)
*Maher Kallel,Mohamed El Louadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于2026年OpenAI模型在数学证明领域的两个标志性事件（Erdős单位距离反例与August语料库），论证了机器验证的核心结构性转变：派生有效性（derivational validity）可由内核机械检查，但表示保真度（representational fidelity）和认知意义（epistemic significance）仍依赖稀缺的专家注意力。文中提出六类表示失配分类法、机器生成数学声明的披露方案，并讨论了其对软件、密码学和受监管决策系统的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在数学和理论计算机科学领域的能力提升，机器生成的证明日益增多。虽然内核检查使派生有效性验证"几乎免费"，但实践表明形式化语句是否真正表达预期问题（表示保真度）仍是核心瓶颈。作者试图揭示这一结构性差异，为自动化时代的数学验证提供概念框架。

Method: 作者通过两个典型案例（2026年5月Erdős单位距离猜想的反例，以及2026年8月同一实验室发布的十个配备Lean 4证书的结果）进行实证分析。测量了August语料库中内核检查证明总量与需要人工审计语句的体积之比（379:1），并统计了218个自创定义的使用情况，以此量化验证负担的转移。在此基础上提出了三层验证模型、六类表示失配分类法和机器生成数学声明的披露模式。

Result: 实证发现：August语料库中内核检查的证明总大小为20.6 MB，而需要人类审计的语句仅55.6 KB，体积比为379:1；但这些语句包含218个非社区审核的自创定义。这一结果表明审计表面在体积上极小但本质上不可替代地依赖专家。作者据此得出结论：机器检查产生"验证丰富性"（verification abundance），而裁定（adjudication）仍然是稀缺的。

Conclusion: 机器验证的效率提升不会消除验证工作，而是将其负担转移到依赖专家注意力的层面。形式化验证的真正瓶颈在于表示保真度的裁定，而非派生有效性的核查。这一认识对数学出版、形式化方法、软件工程、密码学以及受监管决策系统均有重要启示。

Abstract: In May 2026 an OpenAI model produced a counterexample to the Erdős unit distance conjecture. Five mathematicians published a human-verified version the same day, and the result entered the literature within weeks. In August 2026 the same laboratory published ten mathematical and theoretical computer science results, each accompanied by a machine-checkable Lean 4 certificate with no unproved steps. Four weeks later, one remained the subject of an unresolved dispute over whether its formalization meant what it claimed.
  We argue that this difference is structural. We distinguish three layers of verification: derivational validity, which a kernel checks; representational fidelity, whether the formal statement means the intended question; and epistemic significance. Only the first is mechanizable. Making it effectively free therefore does not eliminate verification work but shifts the burden to layers dependent on scarce expert attention.
  Measurements of the August corpus illustrate the shift. The kernel-checked proofs total 20.6 MB, while the statements requiring human audit total 55.6 KB, a ratio of 379 to 1. Yet those statements contain 218 bespoke definitions rather than relying on community-vetted ones. The audit surface is therefore small in volume but irreducibly expert. We argue that machine checking produces verification abundance while leaving adjudication scarce. We propose a six-category taxonomy of representational mismatch, a disclosure schema for machine-generated mathematical claims, and implications for software, cryptography, and regulated decision systems.

</details>


### [191] [Multi-Step Forecasting of Grape Berry Temperature based on LSTM Model with Feed-Forward Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29008)
*Srikanth Gorthi,L. G. Divyanth,Dattatray Bhalekar,Markus Keller,Lav Khot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合前馈注意力机制的长短期记忆网络（FAM-LSTM），用于葡萄浆果温度（Tb）的多步高分辨率预测。该模型在美国华盛顿州Prosser地区2023-2024年环境数据上训练，并在2025年夏季数据上验证。FAM-LSTM在所有预测时域（15分钟至72小时）和输入场景下均优于LSTM、GRU、RNN及随机森林等基准模型。结合葡萄园内微气候数据可显著提升长时域预测精度；开放田间数据下MAE为0.58-1.70°C，园内数据下为0.51-1.55°C。误差分析表明预测不确定性在白天高峰时段（11:00-18:00）最高且随时域增加而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的葡萄浆果温度预测对于葡萄园及时的热胁迫管理至关重要。现有方法在高分辨率、多时域的浆果温度预测方面仍存在精度不足的问题，需要开发更优的深度学习框架来支持精准农业生产管理。

Method: 开发了融合前馈注意力机制与LSTM的混合模型（FAM-LSTM），进行15分钟至72小时（288个时间步）的多步浆果温度预测。模型使用2023-2024年数据训练，2025年夏季数据验证，并与LSTM、GRU、RNN和随机森林进行对比。评估了两种输入场景：邻近开路气象站观测数据和园内微气候测量数据。

Result: FAM-LSTM在所有时域和输入场景下均优于所有基准模型。开路数据下MAE和RMSE分别为0.58-1.70°C和0.65-2.07°C；园内微气候数据进一步改善性能，MAE和RMSE分别为0.51-1.55°C和0.71-1.87°C。误差在白天高峰时段（11:00-18:00）最大，且随时域增加而增大。

Conclusion: FAM-LSTM框架为葡萄园浆果温度预测提供了稳健且高精度的解决方案，能够支撑精准热胁迫管理，特别在整合园内微气候数据时可显著提升长时域预测能力。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of grape berry temperature (Tb) is essential for enabling timely heat stress management in vineyards. In this study, a feed-forward attention mechanism integrated with a Long Short-Term Memory network (FAM-LSTM) was developed and evaluated for multi-step, high-resolution Tb prediction. Models were trained using environmental data from 2023 and 2024 at Prosser, WA, USA, and validated on 2025 summer data. FAM-LSTM was benchmarked against LSTM, GRU, RNN, and Random Forest (RF) across horizons ranging from 15 minutes to 72 hours (288 time steps). Two input scenarios were evaluated: nearest open-field weather station observations and in-vineyard microclimate measurements. FAM-LSTM consistently outperformed all benchmark models across all horizons and input scenarios. Incorporating in-vineyard microclimate data significantly improved forecasting accuracy at longer horizons. Using open-field data, FAM-LSTM achieved MAE and RMSE ranges of 0.58 to 1.70 deg C and 0.65 to 2.07 deg C, respectively. In-vineyard observations further improved performance, with MAE and RMSE in the ranges of 0.51 to 1.55 deg C and 0.71 to 1.87 deg C. Error analysis showed prediction uncertainty was highest during peak daytime periods (11:00 to 18:00) and increased progressively with forecast horizon. Overall, the FAM-LSTM framework offers robust Tb forecasting to support precision heat stress management in vineyards.

</details>


### [192] [Frequency Selective Neural Networks as a Foundation Architecture for Time Series Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29012)
*Hui Huang,Ye Sun,Shiyan Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出频率选择性神经网络（FSNN），一个兼具物理可解释性与深度学习表达力的基础架构。FSNN通过将高级信号处理的严格数学（可微Wiener-like滤波器组）显式嵌入神经拓扑中，利用复数域反向传播自动发现并分离任务相关的物理模态，解决了现有深度学习模型（CNN、RNN、Transformer）在处理时序数据时存在的"频谱盲区"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习时序模型（CNN、RNN、Transformer）将连续的物理波映射到非约束的空间或离散token空间中，导致严重的频谱纠缠，模型如同黑箱，预测准确性与物理现实脱节。这些架构本质上是"频谱盲"的，无法在保证预测性能的同时提供物理可解释性。

Method: FSNN将高级信号处理的严格数学显式嵌入神经拓扑中，核心是一个完全可微的Wiener-like滤波器组，通过复数域反向传播进行优化。该架构能够自主发现并分离给定任务的精确物理模态，从而解决频谱纠缠问题。

Result: 在标准10个多元UEA数据集上达到77.0%的平均准确率，处于最先进水平；在高度不平衡的PTB-XL临床ECG基准上，在所有主要指标上均领先。与产生抽象特征图的传统方法不同，FSNN直接收敛于物理有意义的频带，例如能够分离心脏QRS复合波。

Conclusion: FSNN提供了一个高度可扩展、可解释的范式，用于复杂时间域中的鲁棒模式识别，在不牺牲深度学习表达力的前提下实现了物理可解释性，并通过开源代码促进可复现性。

Abstract: Time-series data across physical and biological domains are fundamentally driven by complex, non-stationary oscillatory modes. While deep learning models, such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Recurrent Neural Networks, and Transformers, have dominated sequential analysis, they remain fundamentally "spectral-blind". By mapping continuous physical waves into unconstrained spatial or discrete token spaces, these architectures suffer from severe spectral entanglement, acting as opaque black boxes that decouple predictive accuracy from physical reality. In this paper, we introduce the Frequency Selective Neural Network (FSNN), pioneering a foundation architecture guaranteeing physical interpretability without sacrificing expressive power of deep learning. FSNN addresses spectral entanglement by explicitly embedding the rigorous mathematics of advanced signal processing into its neural topology. Through a fully differentiable Wiener-like filter bank optimized via complex-domain backpropagation, FSNN autonomously discovers and isolates the precise physical modes of a given task. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FSNN establishes state-of-the-art predictive performance, achieving $77.0\%$ average accuracy on the standard 10 multivariate UEA datasets and leading across all major metrics on the highly imbalanced PTB-XL clinical ECG benchmark. Crucially, in contrast to yielding abstract feature maps, FSNN converges directly on physically meaningful frequency bands, such as isolating the cardiac QRS complex, providing a highly scalable, interpretable paradigm for robust pattern recognition in complex temporal domains. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ad6174hhhh/FSNN.

</details>


### [193] [Disentangling Representation using Attributes-based Gaussian Estimation for Medical Sound Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29026)
*Ke Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AGEDR的可解耦表示学习框架，通过属性映射嵌入（AME）模块将属性映射为向量并与VAE潜向量的子集对齐，通过最小化互信息实现解耦，最终利用潜向量的均值参数训练分类器，在医疗领域实验中取得了优于传统分类模型和其他解耦学习方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的特征提取能力强大，但在医疗领域的应用中，深度神经网络的公平性和可解释性不足，这限制了其广泛采用。因此需要一种既能保持高性能又具备可解释性和公平性的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出了AGEDR解耦表示学习框架，包含三个关键组件：（1）基于属性的高斯估计机制，用于解耦表示学习；（2）属性映射嵌入（AME）模块，将属性映射为向量并与VAE潜向量的子集对齐；（3）通过最小化互信息将这部分潜向量与其余潜向量解耦，最后使用潜向量的均值参数训练分类器。

Result: 大量实验表明AGEDR在性能上优于传统分类模型和现有的解耦表示学习方法；消融实验进一步验证了AGEDR的解耦能力和公平性。

Conclusion: AGEDR通过属性引导的潜空间解耦机制，在保持分类性能的同时提升了模型的公平性和可解释性，为医疗领域的深度学习应用提供了更可信的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning has a powerful capability of feature extraction. However, the lack of fairness and interpretability in deep neural networks poses limitations to their adoption in the medical domain. This paper proposes a disentangled representation learning (DisenRL) framework, named the Attributes-based Gaussian Estimation for Disentangled Representation (AGEDR), which incorporates Attribute Mapping Embedding (AME) modules designed to map attributes into vectors and align them with a subset of the latent vectors in a Variational AutoEncoder (VAE). This part of the latent vector will be disentangled from the remaining latent vectors by minimizing mutual information. A classifier is then trained using the mean parameters of the latent vectors from the VAE. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AGEDR outperforms both conventional classification models and existing disentangled representation learning methods. The ablation experiments also indicate the disentangling capability and fairness of AGEDR. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZhaoKe1024/DisentangledRepr.

</details>


### [194] [Facts Without Rules: Boundary Metadata Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29028)
*Yian Wang,Agam Goyal,Eshwar Chandrasekharan,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现多智能体LLM系统中的"交接（handoff）"机制是结构性隐私泄露源：摘要压缩过程优先保留操作事实，却削弱了控制事实使用权限的边界元数据，作者称之为"摘要坍缩"（summary collapse）。通过对照实验、边界显式性测试和多种缓解方案评估，证明正确识别受众边界（audience boundaries）是防止泄露的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过将上游交互压缩为交接产物（handoff artifact）来协调，下游智能体将其视为共享状态。作者发现该交接步骤本身是隐私泄露的结构性来源，但此前未被系统研究，因此设计受控实验来量化这一失效模式并探索缓解方案。

Method: 在受控多智能体协调测试平台上，使用人工验证的评判器（κ=0.74）测量边界标记存活率σ_b（σ_b=1表示所有边界标记逐字存活，σ_b=0表示全部丢失）。对比不同压缩预算（25词 vs 无压缩）、不同模型（GPT-5-mini、DeepSeek-R1-32B及第三种模型）、不同边界显式程度（模糊语言 vs 显式约束）下的行为，并设置无交接单智能体对照组，测试提示词缓解、精确字符串删除和基于gold标准的受众允许列表等方案。

Result: 边界标记与操作事实的存活在交接层面几乎不相关（Pearson r接近零）；无压缩交接的边界存活率约0.80，但25词预算使σ_b降至约0.57，而操作事实存活仍接近上限。模糊语言在73%的GPT和50%的DeepSeek案例中导致泄露，显式约束可将泄露降至15%以下。无交接对照组表明该失效不能归结为多智能体拓扑问题。提示词缓解和精确字符串删除只能部分解决问题，而基于gold的受众允许列表几乎完全消除泄露。

Conclusion: 交接压缩过程中的"摘要坍缩"是一个独立于多智能体拓扑的固有隐私泄露源。解决该问题的核心不在于压缩技术或文本删除，而在于正确识别和显式保留受众边界（audience boundaries），这是设计安全多智能体系统时最关键的因素。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often coordinate by compressing an upstream interaction into a handoff artifact that downstream agents treat as shared state. We show that this handoff step is a structural source of privacy leakage: summaries preferentially preserve operational facts while weakening the boundary metadata that governs how those facts may be used---a failure mode we call \emph{summary collapse}. On a controlled multi-agent coordination testbed we measure marker survival with a human-validated judge ($κ= 0.74$), where $σ_b = 1$ means every boundary marker survives verbatim and $σ_b = 0$ means all are lost. Boundary-marker and operational-fact survival are nearly uncorrelated at the handoff level on both GPT-5-mini and DeepSeek-R1-32B (Pearson $r$ near zero): uncompressed free-text handoffs preserve boundaries at $σ_b \approx 0.80$, whereas a $25$-word budget drops $σ_b$ to ${\approx}0.57$ while operational-fact survival stays near ceiling. Controlled downstream tests reveal that protection depends on \emph{boundary explicitness}: vague languages leak in $73\%$ of GPT and $50\%$ of DeepSeek cases, while explicit constraints reduce leakage to under $15\%$ across all three tested models. A no-handoff single-agent control further shows the failure is not reducible to multi-agent topology as direct full-marker access still leaks more often than the operationalized handoff. Prompt-only mitigation and exact-string redaction only partially address the problem, while a gold-derived audience allowlist nearly eliminates leakage across models, showing that correctly identifying audience boundaries is the key factor.

</details>


### [195] [Learning to Follow In-Context Watermark Instructions via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29030)
*Yepeng Liu,Tianyi Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Dawn Song,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ICWBench基准，用于评估LLM在上下文水印（ICW）场景下同时实现可检测性和答案质量的能力。评估14个前沿LLM后发现无一能同时在所有三个指令族上达标。为此提出两阶段训练方法：第一阶段（SDLP）通过logits扰动实现自蒸馏，使模型学会遵循ICW指令；第二阶段采用强化学习，以自动验证器作为奖励。该方法在Qwen3-14B和GPT-OSS-20B上将TPR@1%FPR分别从0.100提升至0.974和从0.337提升至0.968，同时保持高质量响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文水印（ICW）为LLM提供了一种第三方无需访问模型内部即可调用的水印接口，但现有LLM遵循ICW指令的能力尚未被系统测量。作者希望建立基准来量化这一能力，并开发无需更强模型蒸馏、无需人工标注、无需预训练ICW能力的训练方法。

Method: 1) 提出ICWBench基准，包含三个可验证的ICW指令族，从可检测性和答案质量两个维度评分，评估14个前沿专有和开源LLM。2) 提出两阶段训练方法：第一阶段采用自蒸馏+logits扰动（SDLP），使用同一基础LLM同时作为教师和学生——通过推理时logits扰动使教师遵循ICW指令，训练学生匹配教师的输出分布；第二阶段应用强化学习，以自动验证器作为奖励信号。

Result: 14个评估的LLM中没有任何一个能在三个ICW指令族上同时实现可检测性和答案质量两个目标。训练方法应用后，Qwen3-14B的平均TPR@1%FPR从0.100提升至0.974，GPT-OSS-20B从0.337提升至0.968，同时在校验困惑度和LLM-as-a-Judge评估下保持了高响应质量。

Conclusion: 现有LLM缺乏可靠的ICW指令遵循能力，但通过无需外部蒸馏或人工标注的目标训练方法，可以显著提升模型在ICW场景下的水印可检测性，同时不牺牲答案质量，为ICW的实际部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: In-context watermarking (ICW) prepends an instruction to a query asking the model to embed a statistically detectable signal in its response. It thus equips LLMs with a watermarking interface that third parties can invoke without access to model internals. Its reliability hinges on the LLM following the instruction without degrading answer quality, yet how well current LLMs do so has not been measured. We introduce $\mathsf{ICWBench}$, a benchmark of three verifiable ICW instruction families, each scored on both detectability and answer quality. Evaluating 14 frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs, we find that none of the evaluated LLMs achieves both objectives across all three families. To address this, we propose a self-contained two-stage training method, requiring no distillation from a stronger model, no manual annotation, and no pre-existing ICW IF ability. The first stage, self-distillation with logits perturbation (SDLP), uses the same base LLM as both teacher and student: an instruction-equivalent decoding-time logits perturbation makes the teacher follow the ICW instruction, and the student is trained to match the teacher's output distribution. The second stage applies reinforcement learning with the automatic verifier as the reward. Applied to Qwen3-14B and GPT-OSS-20B, our method raises average TPR@$1\%$FPR across three ICW instructions from $0.100$ to $0.974$ and from $0.337$ to $0.968$, respectively, while maintaining high response quality under both perplexity evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge.

</details>


### [196] [EmoLASP: Emotion Recognition with Language Models and Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29035)
*Thao Le,Michael Thielscher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EmoLASP框架，将语言模型与基于Answer Set Programming(ASP)的声明式推理结合，用于对话中的情感识别（VAD得分预测）。在IEMOCAP基准上跨六个LLM（3B-120B）和两个PLM（BERT, RoBERTa）实验表明，EmoLASP相比单独使用语言模型能提升预测性能，且对仅用prompt的LLM提升最大（无需微调），但对已微调的PLM在提供对话历史时增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话情感识别越来越多地使用语言模型，但这些模型在微调或使用长对话历史的prompt时存在不稳定且成本高的问题。作者希望找到一种既能减少微调成本和长prompt开销，又能保证情感预测一致性的替代方案。

Method: EmoLASP将语言模型与ASP（Answer Set Programming）声明式推理相结合。语言模型负责生成或编码预测，ASP负责通过声明式规则进行推理约束和一致性校验，最终输出VAD（Valence-Arousal-Dominance）得分。实验中对比了仅用语言模型与EmoLASP在不同LLM（6个开源LLM，参数规模3B-120B）和PLM（BERT、RoBERTa）下的性能。

Result: 在IEMOCAP基准上，EmoLASP在多种语言模型上均优于单独使用语言模型的预测性能，即使语言模型的输入中不含任何对话历史。对仅使用prompt（不做微调）的LLM提升最大；但对已微调的PLM，在提供对话历史的情况下，ASP推理器的增益很小。

Conclusion: EmoLASP展示了将声明式推理与语言模型结合在对话情感识别中的潜力，能够保证预测一致性，并显著降低微调成本和大规模prompt开销，尤其适用于不进行微调的LLM管道。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in conversations is increasingly tackled with language models, but these models can be unstable and expensive to fine-tune or to prompt with long dialogue histories. We propose EmoLASP, a framework that combines a language model with declarative reasoning via Answer Set Programming (ASP) to predict VAD scores (Valence-Arousal-Dominance) in conversations. Experiments on a widely used benchmark dataset (IEMOCAP) across six open-source LLMs (3B-120B) and two PLMs (BERT, RoBERTa) show that EmoLASP improves prediction performance compared to using the language model alone, even when the LLMs/PLMs are given no dialogue history in their prompts or input vectors. The gains are largest for prompt-only LLMs, which EmoLASP uses without any fine-tuning. However, for fine-tuned PLMs, the reasoner adds little once dialogue history is available. EmoLASP's LLM pipeline demonstrates the potential advantages of using a reasoning approach to ensure emotion prediction consistency and to reduce both the cost of fine-tuning and the cost of prompting with long dialogue histories.

</details>


### [197] [Let Prompts Bridge Defense Knowledge: Transferable Graph Purification via Vulnerability-Aware GPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29054)
*Shuomin Xue,Jingyuan Li,Ju Jia,Jingxuan Yu,Xiaojun Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可迁移的图净化方案ProGAP，通过漏洞感知的图提示学习（vulnerability-aware graph prompt learning）来桥接对抗防御知识，解决现有对抗净化方法作为域受限防御在跨域图数据上泛化不足和计算成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图对抗净化方法本质上是域受限的防御，存在两个主要缺陷：(1) 单一域数据提供的结构和语义多样性不足，难以学习鲁棒的净化准则；(2) 从头训练域特定防御策略消耗大量计算成本。

Method: 提出的ProGAP方案包含两个关键步骤：首先，在数据丰富的图上预训练一个扰动捕获边检测器（perturbation-capture edge detector），通过联合建模拓扑与语义信息来捕捉通用的对抗模式；其次，设计漏洞感知提示（vulnerability-aware prompts），将针对性的净化引导注入到有偏节点中，使得预训练检测器能够适应下游图的分布偏移，而无需进行参数密集的更新。

Result: 与最先进的基线方法相比，ProGAP在多个任务上实现了1%-9%的性能提升，同时将时间消耗降低了最多2.2倍。

Conclusion: ProGAP通过预训练加提示学习的范式，实现了跨域图对抗防御知识的高效迁移，在提升鲁棒性的同时显著减少了计算开销，为图神经网络的可迁移对抗防御提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a cornerstone for representing complex relational dependencies in diverse multimedia tasks, particularly in cross-platform user interest modeling and cross-modal semantic alignment. In the real world, a practical defense against graph adversarial perturbations is needed. However, we observe that the prevailing adversarial purification methods are essentially domain-restricted defenses, which leads to the following shortcomings: (1) single-domain data provides insufficient structural and semantic diversity for learning robust purification criteria; (2) training of domain-specific defense strategies from scratch consumes substantial computational cost. To address the above limitations, we propose a transferable graph purification scheme, named ProGAP, to bridge adversarial defense knowledge via vulnerability-aware graph prompt learning. Firstly, to capture universal adversarial patterns, a perturbation-capture edge detector is pretrained on data-rich graphs by jointly modeling topological and semantic information. Subsequently, to achieve more knowledge transfer w.r.t. robustness, vulnerability-aware prompts are designed that inject targeted purification guidance into biased nodes, during which the pretrained detector adapts to distribution shifts in downstream graphs without parameter-laborious updates. Experimental results demonstrate that compared with state-of-the-art baselines, our ProGAP achieves 1%-9% improvement, and reduces the time consumption by up to 2.2x. The code for ProGAP is available at https://github.com/Lieyoufffff/ProGAP.

</details>


### [198] [Agent2UCB: Agentic System for Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29063)
*Sheldon Yu,Rui Wang,Tong Yu,Sungchul Kim,Doga Dogan,Junda Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent2UCB，一个智能体驱动的生成式引擎优化（GEO）系统，自动评估九种GEO策略并通过基于bandit的Agent2UCB策略（融合LLM先验与在线奖励信号）加速最优策略选择，同时提供轻量级文本SEO就绪度评估以监控副作用。在GEO-Bench上的实验表明，系统在保持SEO质量的同时持续提升内容可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的搜索引擎（如Google AI Overviews和Perplexity）带来了生成式引擎优化（GEO）的新机遇，即通过优化内容来增加其被生成式系统引用或总结的可能性。现有方法缺乏自主性、反馈驱动的优化机制，需要一种能够自动评估和选择最优GEO策略的系统。

Method: Agent2UCB系统采用智能体架构，对每个内容项评估九种GEO策略，利用基于bandit的Agent2UCB策略（结合LLM先验和在线奖励信号）加速识别最有效的方法；同时提供文本SEO就绪度评估，覆盖可读性、主题覆盖和EEAT式可信度，以监测优化过程的副作用。

Result: 在GEO-Bench基准上的实验显示，系统在保持SEO质量的前提下实现了持续的内容可见性提升。

Conclusion: Agent2UCB展示了智能体驱动的GEO系统在自动化内容优化方面的有效性，平衡了GEO可见性增益与SEO质量保持，并通过轻量级评估提供可解释的副作用监控。

Abstract: Large language model driven search engines such as Google AI Overviews and Perplexity have created new opportunities for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) the practice of refining content to increase its likelihood of being cited or summarized by generative systems. We demonstrate Agent2UCB, an agentic GEO system that autonomously improves content visibility through customized, feedback-driven optimization. For each content item, the system evaluates nine GEO strategies, identifies the most effective method, and accelerates selection using a bandit-based Agent2UCB policy that integrates LLM priors with online reward signals. To monitor side effects, the system also provides a lightweight, text-only SEO readiness evaluation covering readability, topical coverage, and EEAT-style credibility. Experiments on GEO-Bench show consistent visibility gains while preserving SEO quality. The demo allows users to choose the websites of interest, observe the optimization workflow, and compare GEO/SEO outcomes across methods.

</details>


### [199] [Revolutionizing Turn-by-Turn Navigation with Cloud-Edge Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29073)
*Yiming Yang,Hao Fu,Fanxiang Zeng,Xikai Yang,Yue Liu,Ning Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer和Mixture of Experts（MoE）的深度学习框架，用于生成逐向（Turn-by-turn）驾驶导航的实时、上下文感知音频指令。通过将导航指令生成建模为多任务学习问题，并采用云边协同架构满足计算需求，该方法在实际道路测试中显著降低了偏航率，是深度学习在驾驶音频导航中的首次大规模应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐向导航系统的音频指令生成主要依赖基于规则的方法，难以在信息含量与认知负荷之间取得平衡，在复杂环境中可能导致驾驶员困惑或错过转弯。因此，需要一个能够生成清晰、上下文感知音频指令的方法来提升导航体验和驾驶安全。

Method: 首先将导航音频指令的生成建模为多任务学习问题，将音频内容分解为模块化元素的组合。然后提出一个融合Transformer（强大的时空信息处理能力）和MoE（强大多任务学习能力）的深度学习框架，用于生成实时、上下文感知的逐向导航音频指令。同时实现了一种云边协同架构来处理模型的计算需求，确保可扩展性和实时性能。
